Catalog number : M00223
Uniform title: vāruṇapaddhati
Secondary title: ātmārthapuujāpaddhati
Author : varuṇaśivācārya
Manuscript : IFP/EFEO transcript T00055
Publication country : India
########################################################################### 




śrīḥ 

śubhamastu 

ātmārtha pūjāpaddhati 

vāruṇapaddhati 

vande gaṇḍataṭadvandvasyandamāna madāmbudhau | kṛtamajjana 
līlākikalabhaṃ kalabhānanam || 1 || 

śrīmadaṅghrikamañjarī samudañjita śiñjitai | 
śabdānunvaśāsanaṃ kurvan pātu hemasabhānaṭaḥ || 2 || 

ekaṃ tu tattvaṃ bhuvane tadīśa- ssadāśivaśśānta iti trirupam | 
tajopapannaḥ paramo guruyo nigadyate pratyahamāgamajñaiḥ || 3 || 

p. 2) 

anantaka śrīgalaśailakanyā- kumāra viṣṇu bhṛgiṇobhidhānaiḥ | 
punassa evāpara ucyate ya- ssa eva bodhodadhihetubhūtaḥ || 

śrīdūrvāsāḥ paiṅgalācārya ugra- jyotissadyo 
jyotirīśāgamajñāḥ | śrīkaṇṭha śrīviṣṇu kaṇṭhassubodho 
vidyākaṇṭho rāmakaṇṭho manīṣī || 

jñātāśeṣa prauḍhagūḍhāgamārthān prītāśeṣa 
kṣoṇikāndṛgvilāsaiḥ | jñātānandadyotamānānubhāvā- netān 
vande deśikendrān kulīnān || 

p. 3) 

āptajñānaśivena tatra guruṇā 
śrījñānaratnāvalījñānākhyādimaśaṅkareṇa ca kṛtā sā 
bālaratnāvalī | anyā somaśivena sādhu racitā satkarma 
kāṇḍakramo'pyuttuṅgena śivena paddhatiriyaṃ 
tāmānukūlākṛtāḥ || 

bramākhyā prathamena śambhuguruṇā nāmānārūpā kṛtā prokteyaṃ 
trivilocanena viduṣā siddhāntasārāvalī śreṣṭho'yaṃ 
hṛdayākhyadeśikasatā siddhāntadīpaḥ kṛto mukhyā 
vāruṇapaddhatirvaruṇanāmnāyaṃ śivenoditā || 

p. 4) 

anyaccārutaraṃ kriyākaraṇakaṃ sanmaṇḍanaṃ nirmitaṃ 
prāsādākhya śivena deśikanaṭeśena svanāmocitam nāmnāsvasya ca 
rāmanātha viduṣā sā nirmitā paddhatiḥ śreṣṭheśān śevena 
naipuṇavatā siddhāntasāraḥ kṛtaḥ eṣāmadbhuta 
paddhatīracayatāmācārya varyātmanāmaṣṭāviṃśati 
śaivatantrajaladhī rāmajjajammamājatām | śaivācāra 
vidhānadurgamavanakrīḍā mṛgendrātmanā maṅghrīnambuja 
kāntikandalajuṣo vande matisphūrtaye || 

p. 5) 

ātmārthapūjā vidhaye budhānā- matyanta nityārcana lālasānām | 
saṃgṛhya sārāṃśakamāgameṣu sarveṣu vakṣye prakaṭaṃ sukhena 
|| 

śaucācamana snāna sandhyāvidhiḥ 

atha caturāśramadharmiṇāmapi śucinā karma 
kartavyamityuktatvādāgamokta prakāreṇa prathamaṃ 
śaucācamanādikaṃ pradarśyate | 

suprabhede - 

athātassaṃpravakṣyāmi śaucācamanalakṣaṇam | sādhakānāṃ 
hitārthaṃ tu caturāśramadharmiṇām || 

p. 6) 

brāhme muhūrte samutthāya śucirvāpyaśucirvā yathāsaṃbhavaṃ 
śaucayukto hṛtpadmādi sthāneṣu samayādyadhikārānuguṇyena 
sadāśivādivigrahaṃ dhyātvā yathāśakti binduvarjitaṃ praṇavaṃ 
vā mūlamaṇtraṃ vā manasā japtvā praṇamya snānādisāmagrī 
bhūta 
dantakāṣṭhamṛddaṇḍakamaṇḍalvādisahāyonijālayānnirgatya 
dravyajātamastreṇa śodhite śucau deśe nidhāya 
grāmātprācyāmudīcyāṃ vā śatadaṇḍaṃ visṛjya yathocitaṃ 
deśaṃ gatvā 

p. 7) 

nāsāgre nyastalocana ṛjukāyo nānmamānasassa malādīni visṛjet || 

tathāgame 

suprabhede - 

brāhme muhūrte vidvānvai manasā cintayecchivam | stripāda 
strighaṭikā rātryante tu dine dine || 

brahmamuhūrtamākhyātaṃ saṃghoṣastvīti kathyate | 

tahā jñānaratnāvalyām 

brāhme muhūrte yogātmāsanamutthāya samāhitaḥ | nirodhye sati taṃ 
tyaktvā nocettatraiva saṃsthitaḥ || 

yamādyaṣṭāṅga yogajñaḥ svaprakāśaṃ paraṃ śivam | paraṃ 
vyāmamayaṃ śāntaṃ vyomātītaṃ nirañjanam || 

p. 8) 

bhāvagrāhyaṃ bhāvaśūnyaṃ dehālambanavarjitam | 
hṛdayāmbhoruhasthaṃ vā jyotirūpaṃ parāparam || 

sūkṣmaṃ bindumayaṃ devaṃ paramākṣaramavyayam | yadvā brahmabile 
nādajyotirliṅgamanāhatam || 

sarvajñānottare - 

dhyānaṃ tu dvividhaṃ kuryāt sthūla sūkṣmaṃ tathā budhaḥ | 
dhyānena hi parāṃ siddhiṃ prāpnuvanti hi yoginaḥ || 

sthūlaṃ mantramayaṃ proktaṃ sarvāvayava saṃyutam | 
dvimārgāvasthitaṃ dhyāyet pūjāgnau yogavittamaḥ || 

p. 9) 

kevalaṃ śivabījaṃ tu sarvāvayavavarjitam | jyotirūpaṃ paraṃ 
śuddhaṃ tsūkṣmaṃ tu? parikīrtitam || 

śivabījamiti prāsādavācyam 

praharārdhe samutthāya dhyātvā devaṃ pinākinam | manasā 
vartayettattu praṇavaṃ binduvarjitam || 

acintyaviśvasādākhye? - 

mūlamantramanusmṛtya jihvāgre tu prasannadhīḥ | 

ajite - 

namaśśivāya śarvāya devāya paramātmane | sadaikarūparūpāya 
vikalparahitāya te || 

p. 10) 

ja?sajalya caṃ bahiṣkuryād? nijadhāmni sahāyavān | sthitvā 
yāvaduṣā pūrvaṃ stutvā stotraiḥ praṇamya ca || 

kartavya śubhakāryāṇi cintayitvā samāhitaḥ | utthāya ca 
bahirgatvā daṇḍapāṇikamaṇḍaluḥ || 

kālottare - 

mṛtkāṣṭhaṃ gomayaṃ puṣpaṃ tilamāmalakaṃ śubham | 
kuśāndravyaṃ? gṛhītvā tu nadīṃ gacchet samudragām || 

śivadharmottare - 

dakṣo vinītaścittajñastoyaṃ dānavidhānavit | 
sahāyaśśaucakāryeṣu tadabhāve kamaṇḍaluḥ || 

p. 11) 

atra prasaṅgāddaṇḍādilakṣaṇaṃ ca pradarśyate | 

atra cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

daṇḍalakṣaṇakaṃ vakṣye kalmāṣeṇa samanvitam | 
mañjusnigdhamavakraṃ ca nirvraṇaṃ ? suṣiraṃ bhavet || 

kalmāṣeṇa vihīnaṃ vā tasya lakṣaṇamucyate | eka parvā tu yayā 
yaṣṭi?rvajrakāmā sukhāvahā || 

dviparvā rākṣasī jñeyā māṃsaśoṇita bhojinī | triparvā 
padminī nāma padminī sā sukhapradā || 

kāminī rasaparvā tu sarvasiddhi vivardhinī | muniparvā vipannāma 
nāśakāraṇadā bhavet || 

p. 12) 

mūrtiparvā śivā nāma ata ūrdhvaṃ na kārayet | 
pādāṅgulamadhobhāge putranāśāya kalpate || 

tatsiddhi dvyaṅgulenaiva tribhistu dhanasiddhidam | vedāṅgulena 
strīsnigdhiḥ pañcamaṃ mṛtyudaṃ bhavet || 

rasāṅgulaṃ dhānyasiddhistannāśaṃ saptamaṃ bhavet | 
aṣṭāṅgulaṃ hitaṃ proktamata ūrdhvaṃ na kārayet || 

mulāttu dviguṇaṃ puñcaṃ evaṃ daṇḍasya lakṣaṇam | mūrdhāntaṃ 
deśikasyoktaṃ kaṭyantaṃ sādhakasya tu || 

bhruvontaraṃ putrakasya svāntaṃ? samayināṃ sadā | 
naiṣṭhikānāṃ gṛhasthānāṃ hastavyāmena kārayet || 

sarvadā dhāraṇāyāṃ tu vaiṇavaṃ daṇḍamuttamam | 

p. 13) 

suprabhede - viśeṣa uktaḥ 

viprāṇāṃ tu śirotsedhaṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ lalāṭake | 
vaiśyānāṃ nāsikotsedhaṃ śūdrādīnāṃ tu varjayet || 

sarvajñānottare 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi kamaṇḍalu vidhikramam | asahāyasya 
yogīndra sahāyo syātkamaṇḍaluḥ || 

suprabhede - 

sauvarṇaṃ rajataṃ vāpi tāmraṃ vā mṛṇmayaṃ tu vā | yaduktaṃ 
śilpaśāstreṣu tattadyuktyā tu kārayet || 

pūritaṃ cāḍhakaiḥ śreṣṭhaṃ madhyaṃ triprasthapūritam | 
dviprastha pūrṇamadhamaṃ mānamevaṃ kamaṇḍaloḥ || 

p. 14) 

ṣaṭsahasrikāyām - 

hemadārumayaṃ śastaḥ śulpajaṃ vātha pārthivam | 
bījapūrākṛtaṃ kuryād dvādaśāṅgulamucchritam || 

dīpte - 

udārasya tu vistāraṃ bhūtāṅgulamiti smṛtam | antena padmaṃ 
tannāhaṃ gambhīraṃ syādguṇāṅgulam || 

adhyardhamāsra vistāraṃ oṣṭhamadhyāṅgulena tu | karṇotsedha 
dvayaṅgulyaṃ karṇanāhaṃ ṣaḍaṅgulam || 

pārśve nāsikayā yuktaṃ nāsyāyāmaṃ tryayaṅgulam | ekāṅgula 
pramāṇena pṛṣṭhapādaṃ prakalpayet || 

pātrāṇyetāni sarvāṇi kuryānmātrāṅgulena tu | 

p. 15) 

ṣaṭsahasrikāyām - 

pūrayettena mārgeṇa yāvadvai galakāvadhi | pañcapānīya 
saṃśodhya pañcagavyena bhasmanā || 

śivenāṣṭaśataṃ japtvā pūjya saṃsthāpayecchucau | 
nivedayedaghorāya tatassaṃprārthya dhārayet || 

nityaṃ trighaṭikāyāṃ tu bhūmau nāpyaśucau kvacit | atha na 
sparśayettatra sparśātyāgo? vidhīyate || 

athavā ? kavigavetyenāṃ mantrayet pūrvavatkramāt | śaucaṃ 
tenāhvayaṃ kuryānna bhrāntiṃ tatra kārayet || 

na gṛha?sthaiḥ prakartavyaṃ śaucaṃ kāryaṃ svayaṃ kvacit | 
kamaṇḍalu vidhānaṃ tu saṃkṣepeṇa prakīrtitam || 

p. 16) 

sarvajñānottare - 

tāmramāmlena saṃśodhya tathāstrodaka bhasmanā | raupya hemau tu 
saṃśodhyau lepagandhamalānvitau || 

prajvālyāgnimaghoreṇa astreṇa ca vidhānavit | prātarutthāya 
tannityaṃ dehedevaṃ? yeḥ? kamaṇḍalum || 

tatastvāṃ? pūrayetpaścādvastrapūtena vāriṇā | nājye? nejyaṃ 
na sevitaṃ vastraṃ na śaryānna kamaṇḍalu || 

na bhasmanā balāpatyā? rāsanaṃ? bhakṣyapātrakam | * * * * * * * * 
saṃseveta kadācana || 

kālottare 

parvatādvā nadīkūlāt puṇyakṣetrācchucisthalāt | 
brahmavṛkṣātkuśā dhastādastrakhātāṃ surakṣitām || 

p. 17) 

tālamānādadhāṃ mṛtsnāmādāya hṛdayena tu | sañcayedbhūtale 
śuddhe yāvanmāsa catuṣṭayam || 

varṣākālena? gṛhṇīyāt śrāddhapakṣe viśeṣataḥ | 
kuśāssadeśajā snigdhā nātikharvā na cāyatāḥ | 
śukacchāyāssāgramūlāḥ saptaparṇā na saptajāḥ? || 

pathijātāścitājānāḥ śmaśāne pitṛtarpaṇe | 
staraṇāsanapiṇḍeṣu na grāhyāssaptadeśajāḥ || 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

utthāya ca bahirgatvā daṇḍapāṇiḥ kamaṇḍaluḥ | yogyadeśe 
samāgatya śisye daṇḍakamaṇḍalum || 

tathā santāne 

dravyajātaṃ śucisthāne nidhāyāstreṇa śodhite | gatvā 
dūrāttato maunī dakṣakarṇopavītavān || 

grāmātprācyāmudīcyāṃ vā śatadaṇḍaṃ visṛjya ca | 

p. 18) 

śivadharmottare - 

grāmāddhastaśataṃ gatvā nagarāttaccaturguṇam | mahī sarvatra 
medhyā syān? nirlepā yatra dṛśyate || 

kāraṇe - 

grāmasyai?śānadigbhāge malādīna visarjayet | 

sahasratantre tu - 

yatheṣṭadeśe gantavyaṃ aiśānyāṃ diśi varjayet | 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

nāśuddhabhūtale cityaṃ? pathi? vāriṇi piñchile | 
kṛmigomayadvāsu sasyakṣetre kṣareṣare? || 

munidevālayārāma valmīkajalasannidhau | nadīpuṣkaraṇī vāpi 
(pī?) taṭākādijalāśraye || 

p. 19) 

grāmacatvārarathyāsu vṛkṣacchāyāsu gokule | parvate'śmani 
mu(bhū?)garte kuryācceddoṣabhāgbhavet || 

mūtrotsāraṃ tu yaḥ kuryādeṣu sthāneṣu mānavaḥ | sa pacyate 
mahāghore yācadāmūtasaḥplavam || 

śivadharmottare - 

śiraḥ prāvṛtya tatkuryādantardhāya tṛṇairmahīm | 

anyatra - 

devatā ṛṣayassarve gaṇanāthāstathaiva ca | uttiṣṭha 
sarvataścaiva bahirbhūmiṃ karomyaham || 

sarvajñānottare - 

divā bhūmyuttare tiṣṭhedrātrau vai dakṣiṇāmukhaḥ | 
ardharātreṣu nair-ṛtyāṃ dve sandhye (dvisandhye) tu yathādivā || 

p. 20) 

santāne - 

vātasyābhimukhaṃ caiva na kuryāttu kadācana | 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

guhyadeśe mahāvāte kṣīṇiroge'tha? digbhrame | chāyāyāṃ ca 
dine rātrau andhakāre tathāpadi || 

yathāsukhamukho bhūtvā mṛgacorabhayeṣu ca | pāyūvāyu 
malamūtraṃ visṛjet samupasthitam || 

na yajñāṅgatṛṇacchanne bhūtale visṛjenmalam | 

kāraṇe 

strīṇāṃ govipraliṅgānāṃ pratyakṣaṃ candrasūryayoḥ | 
devatāntaritārāgni sammukhaṃ ca vivarjayet || 

kriyā saṃgrahe - 

na viṭliṅganabhodikṣu kṣipedṝṣṭiṃ vicakṣaṇaḥ | 

p. 21) 

kāmike - 

nāsāpīḍana niṣṭhīvadrapsodgārādikaṃ tyajet | 

śivadharmotare - 

na viṇmūtramudīkṣeta chāyāṃ caivātmano na ca | 

tato loṣṭādinā gudaṃ pramṛjya vastrapihitena vāmakareṇasya? 
sephaṃ saṃgṛhyotthāya dakṣiṇahastena yathoktāṃ mṛtsnāṃ 
nālikeraphalamātramādāya jalatīraṃ gatvā tatra tānnikṣipya 
tṛṇamūlādikaṃ tyaktvā jalena prīkubhyā?malī phalamātraṃ 
savyenādāya vāmakare nidhāya tena tena bhuḥ? daśavāraṃ meḍhraṃ 
trivāraṃ vṛṣaṇau ca tathā punarantarāntarā vāmakaramekavāraṃ 
punaśca daśavāraṃ karadvayaṃ? saptavāraṃ yāvadvabhāvaśaucaḥ 
(co?) vā prakṣālaya sphikśuddhiṃ vidhāya prāṅmukhaḥ 

p. 22) 

pādau udaṅmukho hastau ca jānukorparāvasānaṃ trivāraṃ 
prakṣālya tato'nyatra jalatīre gatvā yathoktagaṇḍūṣān kṛtvā 
pūrvaṃ tūṣṇī mācamya paścānmantreṇācamanaṃ vidadhyāt || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

tuṇaiḥ pāyumayajñāṅgairmṛjyādanupayogibhiḥ | mṛtpiṇḍenaiva 
vā kuryānmārjanaṃ loṣṭakādinā || 

anyairvā na tu saṃpannatvakparatra?rajaḥ phalaiḥ | athigomaya 
pāṣāṇa dārubhirgudamārjanam || 

sarvajñānottare - 

gṛhītaśiśnaścotthāya mṛttikāgrahaṇaṃ kuru | 
vālukoṣarakodyānīvṛkṣa mūlairjalāśaye || 

kodyāne - 

valmīkamṛttikāṃ caiva tathā vai grāmadhānikām? | 
viśuddhamṛttikāṃ gṛhya jalatīre nidhāpayet || 

p. 23) 

kāmike - 

karāmra mṛdvīmmamṛdaṃ śaucārtha māharet | 

raurave 

athavāmbusamīpe tu dvādaśāṅgulavarjitam | atho mṛtsnāṃ 
samādāya nālikera pramāṇakam || 

kālottare - 

nālikeraṃ phalaṃ mātraṃ śaucārthaṃ mṛdamāharet || 

kāraṇe - 

tṛṇamūlādikaṃ tyaktvā jalenā chśri(chī?) kṛtāṃ tu tām | 
āmalī phalamātreṇa savyenādāya vāmake || 

nikṣipya vāmahastena karṣayettu punaḥ punaḥ | 

kālottare - 

daśakṛtvo mṛdaṃ dadyādgudaṃ vai gṛhamedhika? | daśa meḍhre tu 
mṛdaṃ dadyādadhovṛṣaṇayostathā || 

p. 24) 

daśabhirvāmahastaṃ tu saptabhiścobhayau karau | ekaikamantare 
trīṇi evaṃ śaucaṃ kṛtaṃ bhavet || 

tribhistu pādau prakṣālya karau ca punarācamet | 

parākhye - 

kriyamāṇe yadā śauce vidravoccalanaṃ bhavet | tena 
tatkṣālanārthaṃ tu kaṭiśau caṃ vidhīyate || 

atra kṣālane digniyamaḥ cintye dārśitaḥ yathā - 

prāṅmukhaḥ kṣālayetpādau vakrahastāvudaṅmukhaḥ | jalena 
śodhayetpaścād gaṇḍūṣān? ṣoḍaśa kramāt || 

śivadharmottare - 

nagaragrāmarathyāṃ vā parikramya gṛhādbahiḥ | pādau prakṣālya 
mṛttoyairgṛhāntaḥ praviśettataḥ || 

p. 25) 

kāraṇe - 

vāmapādaṃ jale sthāpya sthale dakṣiṇapādakam | māṣamagnajalaṃ 
grāhya nirdoṣaṃ triḥpibedbudhaḥ || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

tato'nyatra samācamyettūṣṇīṃ savyopavītavān | 
punarmantraissamācamya dantadhāvanamācaret || 

tathā śivadharmottare - 

tūṣṇīmeva tato bhūyaśśivamantreṇa pūrvavat | 
ācamedbrahmatīrthena brahmasūtrī hyudaṅmukhaḥ || 

etacchaucaṃ gṛhastha viṣayamuktam - 

kāraṇe 

etacchaucaṃ gṛhasthānāṃ dviguṇaṃ brahmacāriṇām | gṛhasthā 
triguṇaṃ bhikṣoryatīnāṃ tu caturguṇam || 

yāvanmātraṃ manaśśuddhi tāvacchaucaṃ samācaret | 

p. 26) 

śivadharmottare 

gaṅgātoyena sarveṇa mṛdbhāraiśca na gopalaiḥ | ātmano 
rocayecchaucaṃ bhāviduṣṭena śudhyati || 

jñānaṃ malāmbhasāṃ puṃsā sadairāśyamṛdā punaḥ | avidyā 
rāgaviṇmūtra lepagandhavilepanam || 

gadyam? [tadanu hṛnmantritaṃ dantakāṣṭhena dantāndhāvayet |] 

kāmike - 

saṃgṛhya dantakāṣṭhaṃ tu hareddantagataṃ malam | 

dīpte 

nirmalaṃ daśanaṃ yāvattāvadvai danta dhāvanam | tatastālu 
viśodhyaṃ tu taireva pratiśodhayet || 

kāraṇe - 

hṛdā mantritakāṣṭhena dantamāṃsamabādhayan | 
dantānantarbahiśśodhya dantakāṣṭhairitīṣyate || 

p. 27) 

parākhye 

lālāmṛt śrotranāsasya reto viṇmūtramātrajam | nakhapādagataṃ 
yacca yacca gandhādilepanam || 

nāsikā śrotranetrotthamudakena viśudhyati | vaktrajaṃ 
dantakāṣṭhena kaiśyaṃ cāmalakādibhiḥ || 

adbhiśca śuddhimṛdbhiśca śeṣaṃ prakṣālayettanum | 

cintyaviśvasādākhye -

saṃharecchivatīrthaṃ tu dhyātvā saṃhāramudrayā || 

etadvasitvā veṣṭiṃ ca veṣṭayitvā viśeṣataḥ | kaupīna 
lakṣaṇaṃ tvekahastaṃ vāpi dvihastakam || 

āyāmamevaṃ vistāraṃ daśāṅgulamathāṣṭakam | 
kaupīnamevamuktaṃ ca kārpāsanādyā?dinā bhavet || 

pañcāśatsūtrasaṃyuktaṃ mekhalaṃ tryaṃśalambitaḥ | 

kāmike - 

ekadvihastavistāradīrghakaupīnamucyate | dviguṇaṃ kaṭisūtraṃ 
syā dviṣva?granthiniyantritam ||

ṣoḍaśāṅgulavistīrṇaṃ dīrghaṃ ṣaṭtriṃśadaṅgulam | yautaṃ 
śuṣkaṃ śubhaṃ sūkṣmaṃ sitaṃ kāṣāyameva vā || 

trayodaśākṣasaṃkocaṃ kaupīnaṃ brahmacāriṇām | sudṛḍhaṃ 
kaṭisūtraṃ ca mauñcīsūtraṃ palāśajam || 

ekadvitrīsarādyāvatpañcāśatsaramuttamam | yathāśomaṃ 
prakurvīta vāmadevena dhārayet || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

naikavastraḥ kriyāḥ kuryānnagnaḥ kaupīnakevalaḥ | snāne 
devārcane yāge bhojane ca vipaścitā ||

? doṣo'sti naiṣṭhikabrahmacāriṇām || 

p. 53) 

kiraṇe -

vihīnātmā śucirapyaśucirbhavet ||

nirṇijya samyagbandhā(ddhā?)ni śovi(ṣa?)yet ||

vāpi kṣāliteṣu tṛṇeṣu ca || 

p. 54) 

viṇmūtra śuklagdho?nāṃ mṛttoyena viśodhanam | kāryamādau tataḥ

kauśeyāvikacorūkṣaiḥ kṣaumāṇāṃ gaurasarṣapaiḥ |

p. 55) 

aṃśumati - 

ārdravastreṇa saṃyuktaṃ karma kuryādatandritaḥ | vṛthā bhavati 
tatkarma punaḥ snānena śudhyati || 

saśiraskasnānānittau? kaṇṭhānnābhyadhaścaraṇau vā yathā 
śaktya?prakṣālya prakṣālitamavaśiṣṭama�ngamārdravasasā

p. 56) 

kāmike -

p. 57) 

tena snātvā kaṣāyādyairvastramanyatsamāvaset |

p. 58) 

vīratantre -

mataṅge - 

evaṃ sakṛdyathāśakti snāto'mbhasi dine dine |

p. 59) 

makuṭe - 

jalasnānaṃ purā kṛtvā paścādāgneyamācaret |

kāmike - 

bhasmasnānaṃ yathāyogyaṃ yaḥ kuryāt sakṛdeva vā |

bhasma śreyo yato vahnistasminnevāllaṅgataḥ? | bhasmasnātena 
tejasvī vahnivaddṛśyate'nagha || 

vidhinā gomayādiparikalpitaṃ bhūtikośasthaṃ bhasmaṃ savyahastena 
samādāya vāmakaratale vinyasya nirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ 
saṃśodhyāṅguṣṭhatarjanyagrāhyāṃ? svalpena bhavitena

samūlassaṃhitayābhimantrayet | 
akalpanivṛttyādibhirmantraissaṃhitābhiḥ saṃśodhya pādādi 
mastakāntaṃ bhautikastu mastakātpādāntaṃ huṃphaḍantāstreṇa 
nirghaṇena? malasnānaṃ kuryāt || tathā - 

suprabhede -

p. 61) 

siddhaiśca sthānasaṃprāpte brahmaviṣṇvagni vāyubhiḥ | 
īśvarāya tanaya?syāgre śrīmāndharmavṛṣa sthitaḥ || 

yatra vīravṛṣasta?(pra?) nityaṃ gomātaraḥ sthitāḥ | 
golokaśśivalokaścāpyeka eva tataḥ smṛtaḥ || 

ye guṇā rudralokasya golokasyāpi te guṇāḥ | nandā bhadrā ca 
surabhiḥ suśīlā sumanāstathā || 

iti gomātaraḥ pañca śivaloke vyavasthitāḥ |

bhūmau snānārthaṃ hi śivasya tu 

p. 62) 

svāyambhuve - 

gosāvitrīṃ sakṛjjaptvā gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam | dīpaṃ ca

pracodayāt 

gāṃ satīmitthamārādhya gṛhnīyād gomayaṃ tathā |

brahmamantrairdahityā? tu tacchāntikamihocyate | 

p. 63) 

goyonivisṛtaṃ caiva patitaṃ bhūtale tu yat | antarastha gṛhītvaiva 
ṣaḍaṅgena dahettataḥ ||

suśuddhabhūtale caiva patitaṃ gomayaṃ tu yat || 

prāsādena dahitvā tametatkāmadamucyate | prātarutthāya deveśi? 
maunavratasamanvitaḥ ||

brāhmaṇasya tathā śvetā raktā vai kṣatriyasya tu || 

pītavarṇā tu vaiśyasya kṛṣṇā śūdrasya cocyate | kramādvai

p. 64) 

paurṇamāsyāmamāvāsyāmaṣṭamyāṃ grahaṇe dvayoḥ | 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

caturvidhaṃ tu tadbhasma kalpaṃ prathamamucyate | anukalpaṃ 
dvitīyaṃ ca tṛtīyamupakalpakam || 

akalpaṃ tu caturthaṃ syātpūrvaṃ pūrvaṃ tathottamam | vandhyā 
vṛddhā vyādhitāśca durlakṣaṇayutāstathā || 

karṇa?keśavihīne ca bālā maukhyaṃ binā kṛtā | 
amedhyāhārabhakṣiṇyastyājyaṃ gāvaśca garbhiṇī || 

salakṣaṇā ca kapilā raktā śuklā sitetarā | etāsāṃ gomayaṃ 
padmapatre rambhodbhave tathā || 

p. 65) 

palāśapatrake vāpi hastābhyāṃ vyomni vārayet | koṣṭhe vā

u(vi?)paryayaśca kravyāṃśaṃ visṛjenmadhyamaṃ punaḥ | 
golakākārakaṃ kuryādvāmadevena mantravit || 

aghoreṇa ca saṃśoṣya śuddhadeśe dahetpunaḥ | puruṣeṇa 
viśeṣeṇa navabhāṇḍe vinikṣipet || 

tathā - 

ekāhe śoṣayitvā tu nirdahecchivavahninā | nityāgnau vā 
śucirdhīmān śivamantraṃ samuccaran || 

skāndakālottare - 

rakṣārthaṃ maṅgalārthaṃ ca pavitrārthaṃ ṣaḍānana | 
bhaktānāṃ liṅgināṃ caiva bhasmadattaṃ purā mayā || 

p. 66) 

bhasmādāne tu vā mantrī śaṅkhe vā tāmrapātrake | śreṣṭhaṃ 
svarṇapātraṃ tu rajataṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet || 

kanyasaṃ tāmrapātraṃ tu alābhe mṛṇmayaṃ bhavet | 
alābupātramadhamaṃ paittakīvādhamādhamam || 

pañcabrahmaśivāṅgaiśca vyomavyāpi japetsakṛt |

karpūrośīrahrībera ketakaiścampakaistathā | madane śānamantreṇa 
japettasmādviśeṣataḥ || 

uktaṃ ca - 

ketakī mallikīnāgacampakaṃ pāṭalaṃ tathā | padmaṃ 
palāśa(śaṃ?) tulasī(sīm?) apamārgaṃ kuśāgrakam || 

jātiṃ ca tatra nikṣipya sati bhāṇḍeṣu(sadbhāṇḍeṣu?) 
parigrahe(parigrahet?) | mṛtkumbhe tu nave sthāpya 
kavacenābhirakṣayet || 

p. 67) 

tadbhasma parigṛhnīta śivamantramanusmaran | anukalpaṃ tataścaiva 
śṛṇu tvaṃ nandikeśvara || 

vane śuṣkaṃ śakṛdgāhya kalpavatpūrvavatkuru | tṛtīyamupakalpaṃ 
syādāraṇye śuṣkagomayam || 

cūrṇayitvā tu saṃgṛhya gomūtraiḥ piṇḍavatkuru | dagdhaṃ 
pūrvoktavidhinā bhavedbhasmopakalpakam || 

pūrvoktavidhimutsṛjya kevalaṃ bhasmasādhanam | saṃskāre 
piṇḍavatkuryādakalpamabhidhīyate || 

anyairāpāditaṃ bhasma akalpamiti kīrtitam | caturvidhamidaṃ 
proktaṃ sarvapāpanikṛntanam || 

p. 68) 

kāmike - 

eṣaiva kramamādāya pātreṣu kalaśeṣu ca | 
trisandhyāmācaretsnānaṃ tathā sambhavato'pi vā ||

p. 69) 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

akalpabhasma saṃgṛhya digbandhanaṃ kārayettataḥ | pañcabrahma 
ṣaḍaṅgaiśca nivṛttyādyabhimantritam || 

śodhitaṃ nāstramantreṇa mūrdhādyaṅgāni dhūlayet |

ādhvaraṃ karma tatsarvaṃ na tripuṇḍra na cārayet? | 

p. 70) 

atha vidhisnānam - 

hastaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā bhautika? brahmacāri(rī?) grahastho vā 
īśatatpuruṣāghoravāmadevasadyojātamantrairmastakamukhahṛdaya 
guhya pādeṣu tripuṇḍraṃ dhṛtvā sadyenaivāṃsayoḥ 
sarvāṅgeṣvapi | 

sanyāsī naiṣṭhiko vā dakṣiṇavāmakrameṇa pādādimastakānteṣu 
prātilomena sadyādimantraistathāṃsayossarvāṅgeṣu bhasmamudrayā 
vā aṅguṣṭhānāmikā madhyamābhirvā dhārya śeṣabhasmanā 
brahmāṅgamūlena kumbhamudrayā bhasmadevaṃ dhyātvā 
śirasyabhiṣicya huṃphaḍantāstreṇa tāla trayaṃ kṛtvā 
snānakāla patitāṃ bhūtiṃ saṃhāriṇyā hṛdyupasaṃharet | tathā 
- 

p. 71) 

śucau pradeśe śāntātmā dhyātvāstraṃ pūrvavatsudhīḥ | 
bhasmamuṣṭiṃ gṛhītvā tu dakṣiṇena kareṇa tu || 

śiro mukhaṃ ca grīvā ca leśo mārjayettataḥ | uro bāhuṃ ca 
hṛdayaṃ pṛṣṭhaṃ jaghanameva ca || 

yadagamyaṃ dakṣiṇasya tadvāmena nu(tu?) pāṇinā | 
samyaguddhūlayedaṅgaṃ spṛṣṭvānyonyakarāvubhau || 

pauṣkare - 

yati ruddhūlanenaiva snāyānnityamudaṅmukhaḥ | 

santāne - 

brāhmaṇassarvamuddhūlya kṣatriyastu galādadhaḥ | vaiśyaścaiva 
tathā jñeyaḥ śūdroddhūlaṃ? tu varjayet || 

sarveṣāmeva jātīnāṃ mantreṇa rahito bhavet | 

p. 72) 

sarvajñānottare - 

āpādamastakaṃ yāvadbhasmasnānaṃ dvijasya tu | nābherūrdhvaṃ 
mahīpānāmāraktena tu bhasmanā || 

vaiśyasya paṭṭikā proktā śūdrasya tu tripuṇḍrakam | bhasmanā 
brahmajaptena yathā snāneṣvanukramāt || 

kālottare - 

naiṣṭhikānāṃ tu viprāṇāṃ sarvāṅgaṃ snānamācaret | 
nṛpāṇāṃ ca vaiśyānāṃ śūdrāṇāṃ ca tripuṇḍrakam || 

tathā vīratantre - 

uddhūlayet tu sarvāṅgaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ viśeṣataḥ | 
tripuṇḍraṃ kārayettatra nṛpāṇāṃ ca viśāṃ tathā || 

śūdrāṇāmeva sarveṣāmevamevaṃ krameṇa tu | 

kāraṇe - 

uddhūlane'pyaśaktaścettripuṇḍraṃ sarvasiddhiṣu | tripuṇḍraṃ 
bhasmanā kṛtvā sārdreṇa dhavalena ca || 

p. 73) 

sūkṣme - 

niṣkaṃ niṣkārdhakaṃ caiva niṣkadvayadvayārdhakam | viprādīnāṃ 
ca varṇānāṃ bhasmasnānaṃ samācaret || 

kāraṇe - 

madhyāṅguṣṭha? jalairyuktamapare jalavarjitam | tripuṇḍraṃ caiva 
saṃyuktaṃ sarvadoṣanikṛntanam || 

kālottare - 

uttānau tu karau kṛtvā madhyamā tarjanī tathā | anāmikā ca vai 
vatsa parasparaṃ savyadakṣiṇe? || 

bhasmamudrā bhavedeṣā tripuṇḍrāṇāṃ vidhīyate | 

yadvā - 

madhyamānāmikau(kā?)ṅguṣṭhairlalāṭe bhasmanākṛtiḥ | 
tripuṇḍraṃ dhārayecchānto mahāpātakanāśanam || 

p. 74) 

dīpte - 

saptāṅgulaṃ tu vistāraṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ vidhīyate | 
pañcāṅgulaṃ tu vistāraṃ kṣatriyāṇāṃ tripuṇḍrakam || 

caturaṅgulaṃ vaiśyānāṃ tripuṇḍraṃ sarvakāmadaḥ(dam?) | 
tryaṅgulaṃ? tu śūdrāṇāṃ strīṇāmekāṅgulaṃ bhavet || 

tattajjātīyastrīṇāṃ tu tattadeva vidhīyate | 

kāraṇe - 

anulomānāṃ sarveṣāṃ tripuṇḍraṃ pītavarṇavat | anyeṣāmapi 
sarveṣāṃ tripuṇḍraṃ śudravadbhavet || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

bhautikaṃ? brahmacārī cet pūrvasyādaṃ mukhaḥ? | 
naiṣṭhikabrahmacārī cedajādyaiścaraṇādibhiḥ || 

uddhūlya sarvagātreṣu kuryātpuṇḍramiti kvacit | 

p. 75) 

santāniko bubhukṣuśca sādhakaśca tryāyuṣam? | bhasmanā 
secayenmūrdhni brahmāṅgaiḥ kumbhamudrayā || 

visṛjya bhūgataṃ reṇuṃ tālatrayapurassaram | dūre 
prakṣepayettatra tannirmālyaṃ nacā kramet || 

snānamitthaṃ prava(ka?)rtavyaṃ āgneyaṃ brahmacāribhiḥ | 

makuṭe - 

bhasmadevākṛtiṃ vakṣye viśeṣeṇa maheśvaraḥ | triṇetraṃ 
triśiraṃ raudraṃ trihastaṃ guṇavaktrakam || 

tripādaṃ vahninayanaṃ bhasmakuṣṭhitavigraham | 
tāṇḍavāḍambaraṃ bhīmaṃ raktapuṣpopaśobhitam || 

rakṣāmantreśvaraṃ devaṃ dhyātvā śāntiṃ labhennaraḥ | 
trisandhyamācaretsnānaṃ yathā saṃbhavato'pi vā | 

santā(santai?) 

madhyāhnāt prāgjalairyuktaṃ parato jalavarjitam | 

p. 76) 

skānde tathā brahmakaivarte ca - 

viprāṇāṃ salilena dhāryamuditaṃ bhasmaṃ trikāleṣvapi 
strībhikṣorjalavarjitaṃ prativānaprasthānāṃ gāmidam | 
dīkṣāhīnanṛṇāṃ ca vārisahitaṃ madhyāhnataḥ prāksataṃ? 
sarveṣāndhīma?metadāhurapare santo na vāme vidhiḥ 

tripuṇḍra dhāraṇasthāna bhedā ucyante - 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

dvātriṃśa snānake vāpi ṣoḍaśassthānake'pi vā | aṣṭasthāne 
tathā caiva sthāne'pi yojayet || 

uttamāṅga(ṅge?) lalāṭe ca karṇayornetrayostathā | 
nāsāvaktragaleṣvevaṃ(vamaṃ?) haṃsadvayamataḥ? param | 

korpare maṇibandhe ca hṛdaye pārśvayordvayoḥ | nābhau guhyadvaye 
caiva ūrvosphigdvandva jānuni || 

jaṅghādvaye ca pādau dvau dvātriṃśatsthānamuttamam | 

p. 77) 

aṣṭamūrtyaṣṭavidyeśadikpālān bandhubhi(vasubhi?)ssaha | dharo 
dhruvaśca somaśca āpaścaivānilo'nalaḥ || 

pratyuṣaśca prabhāsaśca ityaṣṭavasavaḥ smṛtāḥ | eteṣāṃ 
vāmamantreṇa tripuṇḍraṃ dhārayedbudhaḥ || 

vidyāṣoḍaśasthāne tripuṇḍraṃ ca samāhitaḥ | śīrṣake ca 
lalāṭe ca kaṇṭhe cāṃsadvaye'pi ca || 

korpare maṇibandhe ca hṛdaye nābhipārśvayoḥ | pṛṣṭhe caivaṃ 
pratisthānaṃ jaṅghe tatrādhidevatāḥ || 

śaktiṃ ca sādākhyamīśaṃ * * * * * * * * vidyākhyameva ca | 
vāmādi navaśaktiścāśvinau ṣoḍaśa smṛtāḥ || 

p. 78) 

athavā mūrdhnyelīkena karṇayośca gale tathā | bāhudvaye ca 
hṛdaye nābhyāmūrvoryuge tathā || 

jānudvayo(ye?) pādayośca pṛṣṭhabhāgaṃ tu ṣoḍaśa | eṣu 
sthāneṣvadhipatīnyathāyogaṃ śṛṇuṣvathān(tān?) || 

śivaḥ skandaśca candrārkau vighneṣo viṣṇureva ca | śrīścaiva 
hṛdayo deśe svanābhau tu prajāpatiḥ || 

nāgāśca nāgakanyāścarubhaye ṛṣikanyayoḥ | pādayośca 
samudrāśca tīrthāḥ pṛṣṭhe'pi cāstrakaḥ || 

evaṃ vā ṣoḍaśa dhyāyedaṣṭasthānāni cocyate | gurūsthāna? 
lalāṭaṃ ca karṇayugmamataḥ param || 

aṃsayugmaṃ ca hṛdaya(yaṃ?) nābhirityaṣṭamaṃ bhavet | 

p. 79) 

brahmā ca ṛṣayassapta devatāśca prakīrtitāḥ | athavā mastakaṃ 
bāhu(hū?) hṛdayaṃ nābhimeva ca || 

pañcasthānānyamūnyāhuḥ pañcamūrtiṃ(rtiśca?) ca 
devatāḥ(devatā?) | gurudevāgnividyānāṃ sannidhā(dhau?) 
vāntyadarśane || 

mahāpātakayuktānāṃ kṛtaghnasyāpi darśane | aśuddhabhūtale 
mārge dhūlanaṃ na vidhīyate || 

tasmādviśuddhadeśe'pi cāsane saṃsthitaśśuciḥ | uttarābhimukho 
bhūtvā maunī dhyātvā śivaṃ gurum || 

paramāṇusamaṃ bhasma bhaumau na patitaṃ bhavet | tadvatgrahayattena? 
tripuṇḍroddhūlanaṃ bhavet || 

p. 80) 

uddhūlane'pyaśaktaśce tripuṇḍrādīni kārayet | evamuddhūlanaṃ 
cāpi tripuṇḍraṃ kṛtavānathā? || 

sarvapāpavinirmuktassadyaśśuci samanvitaḥ | tasya darśanamātreṇa 
sarvapāpātpramucyate || 

bahunātra kimuktena deveśekṣaṇasannibham | anāyāsena pāpāni 
praṇaśyantyakhilānyapi || 

bhūtisādhanahīnasyā? na ca tat jñānaparaḥ | na ca 
yogyassamādhīśca na cejyā dānameva ca || 

tasmātsarvaprayatnena bhasmanā snānamuttamam | bhasma 
saṃspṛṣṭahastena vāriṇā prokṣaṇaṃ param || 

p. 81) 

dvijādyadīkṣitasparśe bhasmasnānaṃ vidhīyate | 
sandhyātraye'rdharātrau ca śaiyāpūrvāvasānake || 

suptvā bhuktvā payaḥ pītvā kṛtvā cāvaśyakādikam | tathaiva 
ṣaṇḍaśūdrastrīsparśamāgneyamabhyaset || 

śukamārjāranakulaistathā ca pikamūṣikaiḥ | yadā spṛṣṭaṃ 
tathā cānyaissnānamāgneyamācaret || 

atha mānasasnānaṃ kuryāt | tadyathā - 

pādau hastau prakṣālyācamya prāṇānsaṃyamya nyāsaṃ kṛtvā 
mūlamantraṃ manasā dvādaśāntasthitaparaśakti 
paryantāmuccāryānta?muccārya niṣkalāyāśśakteḥ 
prasṛtenāmṛtamayena | 

p. 82) 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

mūlamantreṇa? manasā prāṇāyāmaṃ samanvitaḥ | 
mānasasnānamevaṃ hi sarvatra vihitaṃ hi tat || 

iti || uktaṃ ca niṣkala śaktikṣobhāt sṛtasyāmṛtasalilasya madhye 
plavaṃ sahasaḥ? punarāgacchaṃstato dhyeya iti || 

tathā - 

mūlamantraparikṣipta kṣudhā pīyūṣadhārayā | 
sabāhyābhyantarāsyekān mānasaṃ snānamācaret || 

iti mānasa vidhiḥ || 

atha mantrasnānaṃ kuryāt pādau pāṇī ca prakṣālyācamya 
sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā pātre jalamāpūrya 
vyomavyāpipadamantrairvauṣaḍanta saṃhitayā sakṛnmūlenābhimantrya 
tena toyena mūlamantrau samuccārya mūrdhādi vāntaṃ(pādāntaṃ?) 
tridhā saṃsparśanena sarvapāpakṣayo bhavet || taduktam - 

kāraṇe - 

mantrasnānaṃ tadante tu prakṣālya pāṇipādakau | samācamyā(mya 
kṛ?) kṛtanyāso(saḥ?) pātre kṣiptvā jalaṃ tathā || 

vyomavyāpipadaiścaiva ṣaḍaṅgairbrahmapañcakaiḥ | mūlenaiva tu 
tattoyamabhimantrya sakṛtsakṛt || 

saṃspṛśettena toyena mūlamantreṇa varmaṇā | 
mūrdhādipādaparyantaṃ tridhā saṃspṛśanaṃ tathā || 

sarvavyādhivinirmuktassarvapāpakṣayo bhavet | 

p. 84) 

tato divyasnānaṃ kuryāt - 

tadyathā tataḥ pādaprakṣālanādikaṃ kṛtvā prāṅmukhassan 
mūlamantramīśāna mantraṃ vā smarannūrdhve (rdhva?) 
bāhussaptapadagamanāt sārkātapāṃ vṛṣṭiṃ divyasnānamayīṃ 
dhārayet || 

taduktam kāraṇe - 

vṛṣṭyā sātapayā tatra prāṅmukhenordhvabāhunā | mūlamantraṃ 
japedvācā yāvadve padasaptakam || 

ādityābhimukhaḥ paścāttaṃ namaskṛtya buddhimān | evaṃ kṛte 
mahāpāpamu(pānmu?)cyate nyatra(nātra?) saṃśayaḥ || 

(māhendraṃ divyamiti paryāyaḥ |) 

īśamantraṃ japan gacchedguru bhaktiviśāradaḥ | 

iti divyasnānavidhiḥ || 

p. 85) 

atha pādau hastau prakṣālyācamya pūrvāhṇe parāhṇe vā nyāsaṃ 
kṛtvā gavāṃ khurodbhūtarajaḥ saṃbandhivāyusparśayukti(kta?)ssan 
narāṇuṃ kavacaṃ vā smaran uttarasyāṃ diśi 
saptapadagamanādvāyavyaṃ snānaṃ bhavati || 

taduktaṃ cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

vāyavya snānamevāha gokhuroddhūtareṇubhiḥ | puruṣaṃ kavacaṃ 
vāpi japan saptapadāvadhi || iti || 

mṛgendre - 

saṃhitāmantraṃ smaran vāyurajo bhavet | 

|| vāyavyasnānavidhiḥ || 

p. 86) 

atha gauravasnānaṃ kuryāt - 

tadyathā - pādau hastau prakṣālyācamya śucirbhūtvā 
śivamantramuccan deśikacaraṇau śirasā dhārayet | 
tatpādavandanātsnānaphalaṃ labhet || 

tathā mṛgendre - 

śucirmūrdhno(rdhnā?) guroḥ pādau vibhṛdā 
(vibhṛyā?)cchivamuccaran | iti || 

iti gauravasnānavidhiḥ || 

atha pārthiva snanaṃ kuryāt - tadyathā - pādau hastau ca 
prakṣālyācamya sakalīkṛtya gaṅgāditīrthaṃ haste saṃgṛhya 
saṃhitāṃ japtvā mūrdhne(rdhnā?)di pādaparyantaṃ saṃhitayā 
ālabhet | tajjalābhāve taṃ? mṛttikāṃ saṃgṛhya 
pūrvavanabhimantra mūrdhādi sthāneṣu tripuṇḍraṃ kuryāt || 

p. 87) 

taduktaṃ mṛgendre - 

gaṅgārkatanayāsindhu sarasvatyādi tatpayaḥ | spṛṣṭvā

kuryāttripuṇḍrakam | evaṃ pārthivamākhyātaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ 
param || 

iti pārthiva snānavidhiḥ - 

eteṣāṃ madhye vāruṇamāgneyaṃ (ṇāgneyagānasamātraṃ?) 
mānasaṃ mātraṃ nityamitaraddivyānāṃ tu naimittikatvāt tattannai 
(nni?)mittopasthāne tattatsnānam vidadhyāt || taduktam - 

brahmaśambhupaddhatau - 

māhendrādicatuṣkaṃ yannaimittikamiti smṛtam | tvaci vāruṇa bhasma 
ca nityaṃ tadanyannaimittikamityuktam || 

p. 88) 

kāmike - 

vāruṇaṃ syād gṛhasthānāṃ yatīnāṃ bhasmanā kṛtam | 
snanāntaratayorevaṃ naimittikamudāhṛtam || 

mataṅge - 

adīkṣitena saṃspṛṣṭvā snānamāgneyamācaret | bāhyavarṇasya 
saṃsparśādvāruṇasnānamācaret || 

pādau (ācamanam) pāṇī ca prakṣālya jaṭācūḍādikaṃ badhvā 
udagāsyo nāvṛtaśirāḥ utkukkuṭamāsanena jānumadhyakara sthitvā 
phenabudbudaśaivāla kramītyādivinirmuktaiḥ śuddhajalaiḥ 
pṛthivyādi śaktitattvānnapañcatriṃśattattvaśuddhyarthaṃ 
bhāratyādi devatā prītyarthaṃ vidhivadācamanaṃ kuryāt | 

p. 89) 

|| ācamanam || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

baddhacūḍaśirāḥ pādau pāṇī prakṣālya bāhyataḥ | upaviśya 
sama(me?) pīṭhe saumyāsyaḥ pūrvadiṅmukhaḥ || 

jānumadhyakaro maunī śvānādīnnāvalokayan | ananyamānaso bhūtvā 
svacchaśītalavāribhiḥ || 

phenabudbudanirmuktairvastrapūtaissamuddhataiḥ | ācamet 
kṛmibhirjuṣṭairnāntyajātiniṣevitaiḥ || 

anyaiḥ kaluṣitairyatra vitṛṣṇā gaurna jāyate |? 
carmasthaissoṣaraissoṣṇairanyāsatpāṇipātrajaiḥ || 

ajite - 

baddhacūḍotkuṭāsīno jānurya karadvayaḥ | nāveṣṭitaśirāḥ 
pādau pāṇi(ṇī?) prakṣālya bāhyataḥ || 

p. 90) 

cinyaviśvasādākhye - 

utkakuṭāsanastha ca paṇiṃ gokarṇavadbhavet | 

utkuṭalakṣaṇa lakṣaṇaṃ bharate - 

same pārṣṇī sahasthidyāṃ bhūtale caraṇau samau || 

yadā tadotkuṭaṃ yogadhyāna sandhyā japādiṣu | 
saṃgṛhyamāṣamagnāmbu savyapāṇipuṭena ca || 

saṃvīkṣyāṅguṣṭhamūlasthaṃ brahmatīrthena tatpivet | 

ajite - 

hṛdgataṃ vipranirdiṣṭaṃ kaṇṭhakaṃ syānnṛpasya tu | tālukaṃ 
vimuvarṇānāṃ krameṇāmbuśataṃ bhavet || 

tāraṃ prathamamuccāryāḥ ātmatatvāya svadhā | etatprathamapānena 
siddhissyānnandikeśvara || 

p. 91) 

ekatriṃśattattvaśuddhiḥ pṛthivīmāyāntasaṃyutā? | praṇavaṃ 
pūrvamuccārya hīṃ vidyatattvāya svadhā || 

evaṃ dvitīyapānena śaktitattvasya śodhanam | 
śuddhavidyeśasādākhyaṃ tritattvamiti kīrtitam || 

oṃkāraṃ prathamamuccārya hūṃ śivatattvāya svadhā | 
etattṛtīyapānena śaktitattvasya śodhanam || 

pañcatriṃśattattvaśuddhirevamācamanena hi | kintvantare'ntare 
hastaṃ prakṣālya ca muhurmuhuḥ || 

atra tattvasya dhyānaṃ ca - 

jñānaratnāvalyām pradarśitam | 

caturbhujaṃ caturvaktraṃ caturaśraṃmbujasthitam | ātmatattvaṃ 
hyetattaptacāmīkara prabham || 

sṛ(sru?)kkamaṇḍalukaṃ vāme dakṣe padmākṣamālikām | 

p. 92) 

nīlaspandanibhaṃ nīlamaṣṭāśrāṣṭabhujānvitam | cakrabāṇābja 
cinmudrāśaṅkhakārmukakarṇikāḥ || 

abhayaṃ ca yathā yogaṃ dadhānaṃ dakṣavāmayoḥ | ekavaktraṃ tu 
saumyāsyaṃ vidyātattvaṃ prakīrtitam || 

śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ vṛttaṃ sarvārthagarbhitam | 
śivatattvaṃ smṛtaṃ hyetatpañcavaktraṃ caturbhujam || 

dakṣe padmākṣamālāṃ ca vāme śulakamaṇḍalam | 

iti dhyātvācamet || 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

adharoṣṭhau dviḥ pramṛjya namo'ntahṛdayena ca | 
dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhamūlena tato jyeṣṭhāṃ kaniṣṭhikām || 

vihāya madhyamasthābhi stristrassaṃ?spṛśecca tau | 
madhyāṅgulitrayeṇapi(ṇāpi?) cordhvādho mārjanaṃ punaḥ || 

mūlamantrajalenaiva? pādamūrdhvaṃ ca saṃspṛśet | 

p. 93) 

kāmike - 

pādāvabhyukṣyamūrdhānamaṅguṣṭhānāmikena tva(tu?) | cakṣuṣī 
saṃspṛśedvidvāntarjanyaṅguṣṭhakena tva(tu?) || 

nāsādvāravubhau(re ubhau?) spṛṣṭvā kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakena tu | 
śrotrau spṛṣṭvā jalopetā(ta?) madhyamāṅgu?(dhyāṅga?)ṣṭhena 
bāhukau || 

nābhimaṅguṣṭhakenaiva sarvāṅgulyagrapīḍitaḥ | hṛtpradeśaṃ tu 
saṃspṛśya sarvāṅgulyagrataśśiraḥ || 

dakṣahaste(sta?)talenaiva hṛnmantraṃ tu samuccaran | 

athavāṅguṣṭhānāmikāgrābhyāṃ mukhanāsākṣikarṇavivarāṇi 
nābhihṛdayāṃ(sa?) dvaya śirāṃśa(si?)dvādaśasthānā?ni 
dakṣiṇavāma krameṇa hṛdāssaṃ(saṃ?)spṛśet || 

p. 94) 

taduktaṃ parākhye - 

saṃspṛśecca tato'ṅgāni śironetre sanāsike | śrotrā sau 
hṛdaṃ? nābhi śire sparśaṃ tato tataḥ || 

anāmā(ma?)yāṅgasaṃsparśaḥ kartavyo'ṅguṣṭhayuktayā | 
bhavedīśassadāṅguṣṭho nyāmānāmāmṛtā ('nāmā nāmā 
mṛtā?) kalā || 

tayāmṛtāmmayā? devāmṛtatṛptāstvanāmayā | bhavanti 
tatkriyākartuḥ kriyā bhāvopabrahmakā || 

iti || 

(sakalīkaraṇam) atha agnihotrādijanitaṃ pūrvoktakalpādisiddhaṃ 
bhasma vā samādāya pūrvokta vidhinā prathamaṃ malasnānaṃ kṛtvā 
vidhivadyathoktasthāneṣu tripuṇḍrāṇi dhṛtyā(tvā?) 
pūrvavadācamya sakalīkaraṇaṃ kuryāt tat trividham || 

p. 95) 

|| sakalīkaraṇam || 

vīratantre - 

prathamaṃ sṛṣṭinyāsaṃ tu sthitinyāsaṃ dvitīyakam | 
saṃhāranyāsaṃ tṛtīyaṃ syāt trividhaṃ cocyate'dhunā || 

gṛhasthaṃ sṛṣṭimārgeṇa brahmacāri sthitena ca | 
vānaprasthayatīnāṃ tu saṃhāranyāsamucyate || 

atra vānaprasthasanyāsinau saṃhāra nyāsaṃ kuryātām | 

santāne - saṃhitāyām - 

kaniṣṭhe sadyojātaṃ tu vāmadevaṃ tvanāmike | aghoraṃ madhyame 
nyasya tarjanyāṃ puruṣaṃ nyaset || 

īśānenaiva deveśi? aṅguṣṭhe caiva vinyaset | 
pādādikeśaparyantaṃ saṃhāranyāsamucyate || 

p. 96) 

apatniko vanasthaścet saṃhāranyāsamucyate | 

bhautikanaiṣṭhika brahmacāribhyāṃ sthitinyāsaḥ kartavyaḥ || 
tathā - 

santāna saṃhitāyām - 

sthitinyāsaṃ pravakṣyāmi śṛṇuṣvāyatalocane | 
madhyamāditarjanyantaṃ pañcabrahmanyasetkramāt || 

īśānaṃ madhyame nyasya puruṣaṃ ca tvanāmike | aghoraṃ 
kaniṣṭhikāyāṃ tu vāmamaṅguṣṭhake nyaset || 

sadyojāta(taṃ?)nyasedyeda?dvi tarjanyāṃ tu viśeṣataḥ | 
madhyāditarjanyanta sthitinyāsamudāhṛtam || 

bhīimasaṃhitāyām - 

hṛdādijānuparyantaṃ sthitinyāsaṃ prakīrtitaḥ | hṛdaye 
vinyasedīśaṃ mukhe tatpuruṣaṃ nyaset || 

p. 97) 

aghoraṃ mūrdhni vinyasya vāmadevaṃ tu guhyake | pāde nyasya tu 
sadyaṃ tu sthitinyāsādimaṃ param || 

|| atha sṛṣṭinyāsaḥ || 

oṃ haḥ astrāya huṃ phaḍiti vāmakaratalaṃ tatpṛṣṭhaṃ ca 
dakṣiṇena dvivāraṃ saṃśodhya dakṣakaratalaṃ tenaiva mantreṇa 
vāmakareṇaikavāraṃ saṃśodhyāstratejasā dagdhau maṇibandhāvadhi 
hastau saṃcintya tau saṃpuṭīkṛtyāṅguṣṭhadvayamadhye 
amṛtarūpeṇa śaktimaṇḍalaṃ dhyātvā tenāmṛtena oṃ hāṃ 
śaktaye vauṣaḍiti karadvyaṃ saṃplāvya oṃ hāṃ śivāsanāya nama 
iti karapuṭe śivāsanaṃ 

p. 98) 

vinyasya tanmadhye oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ śivamūrtaye nama iti 
sūkṣmamūrtiṃ sadāśivaṃ vinyasya aṅguṣṭhayostarjanībhyāṃ oṃ 
hoṃ īśānamūrdhne namaḥ | 
tarjanyādikaniṣṭhāntamaṅguṣṭhābhyām oṃ heṃ 
tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ | oṃ huṃ aghora hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ 
hiṃ vāmadeva huhyāya namaḥ | oṃ haṃ sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ | 
karatalayoḥ oṃ hāṃ hauṃ vidyādehāya namaḥ | 
ityaṅguṣṭhābhyāṃ sadāśiva vigrahaṃ vinyasya 
punarmadhyāṅgulibhistribhiḥ oṃ hauṃ netrebhyo namaḥ | 
dṛkkriyecchātmakaṃ netraṃ vinyasya sakalahasta vytāptaśaktitvena 
madhye oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya nama iti sadāśivadehavyāpakaṃ 
śivaṃ saṃcintyāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyām 

p. 99) 

āvāhya kaniṣṭhādikaraśākhāsu oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ | oṃ 
hīṃ śirase namaḥ | oṃ hūṃ śikhāyai namaḥ | oṃ rhaiṃ 
kavacāya namaḥ | oṃ haḥ astrāya phaḍiti vinyasya 
vāmadakṣiṇakrameṇa namo'ntakavecenāvakuṇṭhyo hauṃ? hastau 
saṃyojya vauṣaḍanta mūlena paramīkṛtyāṅganyāsamārabhet | 
dakṣiṇāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ hṛdi oṃ hāṃ śivāṃsanāya 
nama iti ṣoḍaśadalakarṇikā sahitaṃ śvetapadmaṃ vicintya tatra 
oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ śivamūrtaye nama iti bhrū cibu?kāntāṃ 
jyotirūpāṃ sūkṣme mūrtiṃ vinyasyeśānamantraṃ mūrdhni 
muṣṭiṃ badhvāṅguṣṭhena vinyasya vaktrahṛdguhya jānuṣu 
tatpuruṣāghoravāmadeva sadyojātamantrān 
aṅguṣṭhayuktatarjanyādyāṅgulikrameṇa vinyasya 
aṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ hṛdi oṃ hauṃ vidyādehāya nama iti 
brahmarandhrāntaṃ pañcabrahmakalāmayaṃ sadāśivaṃ vinyasya 
netramantraṃ teṣu madhyamāṅgulibhistisṛbhirvinyasya 
viśeṣādhiṣṭhātṛtvena vidhivattanmūla dvādaśāntamuccārya 
mūrtivyāpakamaṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāmāvāhya sakalīkaraṇāya 
hṛdaya śiraśśikhā sthāneṣu 

p. 101) 

hṛdayaśiraśśikhāmantrān sāṅguṣṭhakaniṣṭhānāmikā 
madhyābhirvinyasya kaṇṭhaprāvaraṇatvena stanamadhyāntaṃ 
namo'ntakavacena tarjanībhyāṃ vinyasya 
hastatalayorastramantramanāmikābhyāṃ vinyasya huṃphaḍantāstreṇa 
tālatrayapurassaraṃ vighna nissārtthaṃ mudrayā daśadikṣu 
choṭikayā bandhanaṃ kṛtvā kavacenāveṣṭya vauṣaḍantamūlena 
pādādimūrdhāntaṃ sarvamantra prarocanārthaṃ mahāmudrāṃ 
pradarśayet || tathā - 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

astra mantraṃ dviruccārya vāmapāṇiṃ ca śodhayet | kareṇa 
dakṣiṇe naiva dakṣiṇaṃ vāmapāṇinā || 

p. 102) 

ekavārāntamuccārya śodhayennandikeśvaraḥ | astratejo'nvitau 
hastau maṇibandhāvadhi smaran || 

pāṇibhyāṃ saṃpuṭaṃ kṛtvā cāṅguṣṭhadvaya sampuṭe | 
dhyāyedamṛtabījena śaktimaṇḍalamuttamam || 

oṃ hauṃ śaktaye vauṣaḍ(ṭ?) saṃplāvyaṃ tatkaradvayam | 
padmākaraṃ puṭe bhāvyaṃ tanmadhye tu śivāsanam || 

mūrtiṃ sūkṣmyā tu vinyasya paścānmantraṃ samuccaret | evaṃ 
sadāśivaṃ rūpaṃ vinyasennandikeśvaraḥ || 

vīratantre - 

īśādipañcamantrāṇi aṅguṣṭhādi nyasettataḥ | 

p. 103) 

kāmike - 

vidyāśarīraṃ taṃ dhyātvā tataśśaktyantamuttamam | netraṃ 
hastatale nyasya paścānmūlamanusmaran || 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

sakalīkaraṇaṃ paścātkaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhakāntajam | 
hṛdādimantravinyāsaḥ sakalīkaraṇaṃ bhavet || 

kālottare - 

vinyasyāṅgulibhirmadhye kavacenāvakuṇṭhayet | karāṅgulīṣu 
sarvāsu prāsādaṃ vinyasettataḥ || 

karanyāsassamākhyātastvaṅganyāsamataḥ param | 
dakṣāṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ hṛdyāsanaṃ ca mūrtikam || 

vinyasya vidhivaddehe pañcabrahma vinyase?ttataḥ | 

p. 104) 

ajite - 

aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa mūrdhnīśaṃ vinyased grāhyamuṣṭinā | mukhe 
tatpuruṣaṃ caiva tarjanyāṅguṣṭhayogataḥ || 

madhyamāṅguṣṭhayogena hṛdyaghoraṃ tathā nyaset | 
aṅguṣṭhānāmikāyogādguhye vāmaṃ ca vinyaset || 

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena sadyojātaṃ tu pādayoḥ | 
aṅguṣṭhānāmikāgreṇa vidyādehaṃ nyaseddhṛdi || 

locane vinyasennetraṃ madhyābhistribhiraṅgulaiḥ | prāsādaṃ hṛdi 
vinyasya paścādaṅgāni vinyaset || 

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhayogena hṛdayaṃ hṛdaye nyaset | 
aṅguṣṭhānāmikāgreṇa śiraśśirasi vinyaset || 

p. 105) 

śikhāśśikhāyāmaṅguṣṭhamadhyamāgreṇa vinyaset | hṛdayādi 
nyasedevaṃ kavacaṃ stanamadhyataḥ || 

kaṇṭha prāvaraṇatvena tarjanībhyāṃ nyasettataḥ | 
anāmikābhyāmatale astramantraṃ tu vinyaset || 

tālatrayaṃ tathāstreṇa digbandhanamathācaret | 

acintyaviśvasādākhye - 

choṭikāmudrayā tena daśadigbandhanaṃ kuru | 

taduktam ajite - 

dakṣiṇāṅgulibhirvāmahastasya talatāḍanam | 
aṅguṣṭhavarjitābhissā tālamudrā tu śuddhidā || 

kṛtvā dakṣiṇahastena muṣṭiṃ dṛḍhataraṃ tataḥ | 
aṅguṣṭhāgreṇa tarjanyā samyagagreṇa pīḍya ca || 

p. 106) 

prakṣipettarjanīṃ bhede yathā śabdodayo bhavet | 
nārācāstrasamākhyeyaṃ mudrā digbandhakarmaṇi || 

iti || sarvajñānottare - 

mantrapūtakarau kṛtvā kavacenāvakuṇṭhayet | tataścālabhanaṃ 
kuryātpādādārabhya mūrdhani || 

mūlena paramīkṛtya mahāmudrāṃ tataḥ kramāt | 

taduktam samānatantre - 

hastābhyāṃ saṃspṛśetpādau tau nayedyāyama?stakam | eṣā mudrā 
mahāmudrā * * * * * * * * ntataḥ kramāt || 

* * * * * * * * * * * * karasaṃskārakarmaṇi | 

kāraṇe viśeṣa uktaḥ - 

hastābhyāṃ saṃspṛśetpādau kādipādāntakaṃ yataḥ | eṣā 
mudrā mahāmudrā karasaṃskārapaścime? || 

p. 107) 

asyaphalam pauṣkare pratipāditam - 

avibhinnasvabhāvānāṃ yadi bhinnaprayojanam | aṅgānāmaṅginā 
sārdhaṃmṛtīkaraṇaṃ(rdhamamṛtī?) hitam || iti || 

sarvajñānottare 

śivasya yādṛśaṃ rūpaṃ tādṛśaṃ cintayātmani | 
śivamantranyāsamidamātmanastu sadā kuru || 

itthaṃ mantra sanuddha vigrahatyan?sādhakaḥ śarīrarakṣārthaṃ 
sarvavighnaurnnānuhūyate | tathoktam - 

hṛdayā yat? paritrāṇabandhapaṭṭavādāhitam | śiraśca 
cchirastrāṇāṃ? śirasaḥ pariveṣṭanam || 

cūlikāmastakatrāṇamaṅgatrāṇaṃ ca kaṅkaṭam | kare śastre 
gṛhīte tu bhīṣaṇo gaṭavat? sthitaḥ || 

kṣudrāṇāmapradhṛṣyasyā tārkṣyāt dṛkśravasāmiva | iti || 

p. 108) 

atha mantrasaṃhitāyā (tayā?) triḥ prāṇānāyamya punarācamya 
śrotravandanaṃ kuryāt || 

kriyākāṇḍakramāvalyām - 

sakalīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāyāmena saṃsthitaḥ | 
trissamāvartayenmantrī manasā śivasaṃhitām || 

pūrvavatpunarācamya vidhāya śrotravandanam | 

|| prāṇāyāmaḥ || 

śivadharmottare - prāṇāyāmasvarūpamuktaṃ yathā - 

prāṇo vāyuśśarīrasthastasya yāmo nirodhanam | recakaḥ 
pūrakaścaiva kumbhakastrividhaḥ smṛtaḥ || 

nāsikāpuṭamaṅgulyā pīḍyaikamapareṇa ca | audaryaṃ 
recake(ye?)dvāyu(yuṃ?) recanādrecakaḥ smṛtaḥ || 

p. 109) 

bāhyena vāyunā dehaṃ drati?vatpūrayedbhṛśam | tathā pūrṇaśca 
saṃtiṣṭhetpūraṇātpūraṇaḥ smṛtaḥ || 

na muñcati na gṛhṇāti vāyumantarbahiḥ sthitam | saṃpūrṇe 
kumbhavattiṣṭhedacalassa tu kumbhakam(ḥ?) || 

tadanu aṅguṣṭhā nāmikāgrābhyāṃ netrayordivyamudrāṃ vinyasya 
namo'ntamūlajala nirīkṣya huṃphaḍantāstreṇāgnitīrthena 
saṃprokṣya tenaiva saṃtāḍya vauṣaḍantakavacenābhyukṣya oṃ 
hāṃ hṛdayāya vauṣaḍityaṅkuśamudrayā bindusthānād 
godāvaryādyanyatamaṃ tīrthādākṛṣya mano'ntaṃ 
hṛdayenodbhavamudrayā jale tīrthaṃ 

p. 110) 

prakṣipya mūlena śivatīrthamabhimantrya huṃphaḍantāstreṇa 
saṃrakṣya namo'ntaṃ kavacenāvakuṇṭhya vauṣaḍanta 
śaktimantreṇa dhenumudrayā amṛtīkuryāt || 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

nirīkṣaṇādibhiśśodhya jalamaṅkuśamudrayā | lalāṭe 
tīrthamākṛṣya oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya vauṣaṭ || 

śarāśaṃ nātha saṃrakṣya śaktimantreṇa mantravit | 
amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryādvighnaughānāṃ nivāraṇam || 

suprabhede - dhenumudrāsvarupamuktam | 

tadyathā - 

veṇībandhakarau kṛtvā'bhyaṅguṣṭhau yugmasaṃsthitau | tarjanī 
madhyame līnau kaniṣṭhānāmikāyutau || 

p. 111) 

evaṃ catvāriyugmaṃ tu divyāmṛtasuvarṣaṇi | iyaṃ surabhimudrā ca 
dravyaśuddhyarthamucyate || 

kāmike - 

sakalīkṛtadehastu tatassandhyāmupāsayet | 

atha sandhyākālaniyamamuttamādi krameṇocyate | - 

ādyāṃ nakṣatrasaṃhitāṃ sārkā sandhyāsu pāścimām | 
madhyamāṃ madhyame'bhyeke yatnātsandhyāsumabhyaset || 

uttamā dṛṣṭanakṣatrā madhyamā luptatārakā | ardhoditanaruṇā 
kanyā prātassandhyā tridhā smṛta || 

madhyāhne'pi tathā jñeyā ghaṭikātrayakalpitā | 
ardhārkamaṇḍale śreṣṭho madhyamāstamite ravau || 

p. 112) 

kanyasā dṛṣṭanakṣatrā sāyaṃ sandhyā tridhāmatā | 
prāksandhyāsamayasthasya viśiṣṭasyāhnikadvayam || 

nirvāṇadīkṣitasyātra sandhyātrayamudāhṛtam | caturthāmapi 
sandhyāyāṃ pūrvavat deśikottamaḥ || 

sakalaniṣkalabhedena sā sandhyā tu dvidhā matā | 

mohaśūrottare - 

sandhyāyā lakṣaṇaṃ śat? prasaṅgena nigadyate | ādyā śakti 
parā nityā yā sā karaṇa dharmiṇā || 

sā sandhyā paramārthena bodhenāvagatātmanā | 
mṛṇālasūtrasaṃkāśā paścāt * * * * la tarā bhavet? || 

pūrvaṃ kālādibhedena bhinnavarṇā pṛthak pṛthak | raktā śuklā 
tathāśyāmā caturthī guṇavarjitā || 

p. 113) 

hṛdaye ca tathā viñcau? brahmarandhre nirāspade | catasrāṇāṃ 
samuddiṣṭā dhyānasthānaṃ śatakrato || 

tatra samayaviṣaye sakalaśaktidhyānamuktam- 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

prātassandhyeti tatkāle brāhmīṃ hṛdayapaṅkaje | rakta 
bhūṣāmbarā(rāṃ?) raktāṃ raktamālyānulepanām || 

jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktāṃ caturvaktrāṃ caturbhujām | 
haṃsapaṅkajamadhyasthā tamaṣṭadṛśāvalokinīm || 

yajñopavītinīṃ bālāṃ padmamālāvirājitām | sṛgakṣamālāṃ 
sarvābhyāṃ bāhubhyāṃ dadhatīṃ parām || 

p. 114) 

daṇḍaṃ kamaṇḍaluṃ dvābhyāṃ vāmābhyāṃ ca tathaiva ca | 
tripuṇḍrāvalisandīptāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām || 

śatayojanakoṭībhirvistṛte raktamaṇḍale | tanmadhye susthitāṃ 
devīṃ sarvakarmāṇi sākṣiṇīm || 

madhyāhne vaiṣṇavīṃ dhyāyet śuklāṃ śuklāmbukojvalām | 
vainateyābjamadhyasthāmeka vaktrāṃ suyauvanām || 

vanamālopavītāṃ ca sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutām | bimboṣṭhīṃ 
cāruvadanāṃ netrayugmāṃ sukuṇḍalām || 

śaṅkhaṃ vāme ca cakraṃ ca dakṣiṇe dadhatīṃ kare | gadābhayaṃ 
ca dadhatīṃ tripuṇḍrāṅkitamastakām || 

p. 115) 

sarvālaṃkārasaṃyuktāṃ sākṣiṇīṃ sarvakarmaṇām | 
dviśatayojanavistīrṇe * * * * * * * * śvetamaṇḍale? || 

tatra madhye sthitāṃ devīṃ varāṃ saṃsthitikāriṇīm | sāyāhne 
śirasi dhyāyedraudrīṃ nīlotpalaprabhām || 

ardhacandrāṅkitajaṭāmīṣadgalitayauvanām | kṛṣṇājinaupavītiṃ 
ca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām || 

sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyuktāṃ saṃhāramapi kāriṇīm | bhasmatripuṇḍra 
saṃyuktāṃ triśūlākṣaṃ ca dakṣiṇe || 

vāme śaktyabhayaṃ devīṃ sarvakarmasu sākṣiṇīm | ardhāstamita 
dra(tri?)śatakoṭiyojana vistṛte || 

p. 116) 

bindumaṇḍalamadhyasthāṃ bhāvayennandikeśvaraḥ | tāsāṃ 
prabhābhi?rātmānaṃ bhāsitaṃ bhāvayettadā || 

atha putrakādyāstu niṣkaladhyānam - 

jñānināṃ parā sandhyāścatastrādidaye dhunā | ekaivānekatāṃ 
yāti śivaṃ śaktirupādhitaḥ || 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

uttamā paramā sandhyāmardharātre viśeṣataḥ | 
śivaśaktisvarūpeṇa caikatvenaikatāṃ gatā || 

bisatantunibhā sūkṣmā raktā śuklā sitetarā | caturthī niṣkalā 
nityā kṛṣṇopāsā vibhāvyate || 

śivabodhā parā dhātu(yātu?) sā sandhyā paramā bhavet | 

p. 117) 

mohaśūrottare - 

jñānināṃ kathitaṃ kiṃca yogināṃ kathyate hare | ante sūryasya 
candrasya dve sandhye prāṇayoginām || 

parayogena kathitā tvanyathā mama śūlinā | śuddhabodhavadāvasthā 
cātmano yā śacīpateḥ || 

yathā kālakrameṇaiva sandhyāṃ dhyātvā svabhāvataḥ | 

evaṃ yathādhikāraṃ sandhyāṃ dhyātvā pūrvābhimantrita 
śivatīrthajalaṃ dakṣahastena vāmahaste hṛdā nikṣipya 
sakṛtsaṃhitayābhimantrya praṇavarūpayā 
candrasadṛśāmṛtasravantyā kumbhamudrayā vauṣaḍantamūlena 
sakṛtprayogeṇa śirasyabhiṣecayet || 

p. 118) 

punassahṛdā vāmahastena vāri kuśānvitaṃ nikṣipya 
vauṣaḍantasaṃhitāyāmabhimantrya dakṣiṇahastena 
vāmāṅgulimadhyasṛtaṃ jalaṃ śirasi vauṣaḍantasaṃhitayā 
prokṣayediti mārjanam || 

tathāgamaḥ - 

badhvā kalaśamudrāṃ tu sudhākhyādhāraṇaṃ smaret | tārakaṃ 
pañcamātraṃ tu kumbhavaccāpyadhomukham || 

susnigdhaṃ candrasadṛśaṃ sravantamamṛtaṃ tataḥ | kumbhamudrāṃ 
purā badhvā saṃhitāyāṃ tu (tāṃ tu?) sakṛtsmaret || 

pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni tatra snātau bhavettadā | 
sarvakarmādhikārī syātsarvayajñaphalaṃ bhavet || 

p. 119) 

mohaśūrottare - 

dakṣiṇena jalaṃ gṛhya vāmahaste tu nikṣipet | mantrī saṃhitayā 
paścādabhimantrya śivātmakam || 

kāmike - 

vāmahaste jalaṃ sthāpya sruvaśśirasi yojayet | 
mūlāṅgabrahmabhirmantrairdakṣahastena nityaśaḥ || 

iti || athavā śiṣṭajalaṃ dakṣiṇakare prakṣipya pūrakavāyunā 
vāmanāsāpuṭena praviṣṭaṃ dharmarūpaṃ śuddhaśvetajalaṃ 
saṃcintyodare kumbhīkṛtya tajjalena dehāntargatapāpaṃ prakṣālya 
tena tajjalamantaḥ pāpakṣayeṇa kṛṣṇavarṇaṃ vicintya 
recakavāyunā dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭena tajjalaṃ bahirnissṛtaṃ 
vibhāvya huṃphaḍantāstreṇa 

p. 120) 

jvaladagnivarṇa vajraśilāyāṃ bahurūpeṇa dakṣiṇapāṇinā 
vāmabhāge nikṣipeditya ghamarṣaṇam || 

kāmike - 

layopiddakṣaddhaṃsthā hiśśeṣaṃ vāri sitadyuti | hāhṛtya 
pūrakeṇaiva vāmanāsāpuṭena tu || 

tajjyotsnayā ca nirdagdhaṃ pāpadeho'sitaprabhaḥ || 

recakā dakṣanāsāto nirgataṃ paricintayet | taccheṣaṃ 
dakṣiṇahastaṃ bhrūvikṣepaṃ mahāstritam || 

mṛgendrapaddhatyām - 

jalaṃ vajraśilāpṛṣṭhe kṣipettadaghamarṣaṇam | iti || 

p. 121) 

tadanvamantrakaṃ samantrakaṃ cācamya vidhivat sakalīkaraṇaṃ 
kṛtvā kavacenāstreṇa vātmānaṃ saṃveṣṭayet || tathāgamaḥ - 

tato dvidhā samācamya kṛtanyāsaḥ prasannadhīḥ | yadā yadā 
samācāmenmantranyāsaṃ tadā tadā || 

kurvīta karakāyeṣu mantrī mantrārtha siddhaye | 

acintyaviśvasādākhye - 

sudhāmayajalenaiva dehasaṃveṣṭanaṃ kuru | savyahaste jalaṃ gṛhya 
astraṃ mantra pradakṣiṇam || 

āditya maṇḍala madhyasthaṃ sadā śivaṃ smaran oṃ hāṃ hauṃ 
śivāyārghyaṃ svāheti śivāya puṣpākṣatasannihitamañjalitrayaṃ 
hṛtpradeśe nidhāyārghyaṃ dadyāt || 

p. 122) 

mṛgendre - 

tīkṣṇāṃśu bimbamadhyasthaṃ dhyātvā devaṃ sadāśivam | 
kṣipejjalāñjaliṃ mantrī viratau cetasā smaran || 

mohaśūrottare - 

śivamantraṃ grahaṇaṃ mantrī svāhāntamudakāñjaliṃ dattvā 
śivāya gāyatri japet | paścāt svabhāvataḥ yathā 
vidhānamarghyamutkṣipya vakṣyamāṇo dhena? gāyatrīṃ japet || 

kāmike - 

prāta sthitvātha sāyāhne samāsīno munīśvarāḥ | āsīno vā 
sthito vāpi madhyāhne vidhimācaret || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

utthitaḥ pūrvasandhyāyāmarghyaṃ pūrvānano(nanena?) tu | 
ādityāya pradātavyaṃ japaṃ caivotthitena tu || 

p. 123) 

madhyāhne samaye tadvat kiṃtu dakṣānanena tu | sāyamutthāya 
dattvārghyamupaviśya japettadā || 

śuṣkārdravasano vāpi gāyatrīṃ jale japet? | 
sūryānalātmikānnityaṃ virodhādvāritejasoḥ || 

nāḍīsandhānakassūryaraśmisaṃpūrṇa vigrahaḥ | gāyatrīṃ hṛdi 
saṃcintya śuklāṃ śuklopavītivīm || 

kuryāt prabhṛtadakṣāṃsi vāmārdhya jale shitām | ekavaktrāṃ 
triṇetrāṃ ca jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍanām || 

tripuṇḍrāvalibhirdīptāṃ sarvābharaṇabhūṣitām | 
kṛṣṇājinottarāsaṅgāṃ śuklavastravibhūṣitām || 

p. 124) 

sākṣasūtravarāṃ savye vāme padmālayānvitām | evaṃ dhyātvātha 
gāyatrīmārabhettu japaṃ yathā || 

oṃ tanmaheśāya padaṃ vidmahe ca tataḥ padam | mahādevāya 
mantrānte dhīmahītisamanvitam || 

tannaḥ śivapadaṃ tasmānniyojyaṃ ca pracodayāt | gāyatrī kathitā 
vatsa caturveda phalārthadā || 

trisandhyāṃ śivagāyatrīṃ japedbhaktisamanvitaḥ | yadvā 
tryakṣarāṃ gāyatrīṃ japet | 

oṃ hāṃ hrīṃ vātha gāyatrī namo'ntābhīṣṭadāyikā | iti | 

saptāvartā(vṛrttyā?) pura(nā?)tyena daśabhiḥ prāpayeddivam | 
viṃśāva(vṛ?)rtyā tu sā devī nayatyaiśvaramālayam || 

śatamaṣṭottaraṃ japtvā tārayejjanmasāgarāt | iti | 

p. 125) 

sūryamaṇḍalādāgatāṃ japānte punastanmaṇḍalameva praviṣṭāṃ 
cintayet || uktaṃ ca - 

oṃkārādyā caturviṃ?śatyakṣarānte nati varjitān | 
gā(ya)tryāgatāṃ japtvā? viṣṭopānte'rkamaṇḍaleti || 

itthaṃ pratidinaṃ japtvā svāhāntamūlena pradakṣiṇatrayaṃ 
kṛtvā mantrāṇāṃ dīpanārthāya svāhānta saṃhitāṃ tarpayet || 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

trisandhyaṃ śivagāyatrīṃ japedbhakti samanvitaḥ | evaṃ mantraṃ 
japitvārghyaṃ dattvā sadyā(dyo?) visarjanam || 

triḥ pradakṣiṇamāvartya(vṛttya?) mūlamantraṃ japettataḥ | 
mantrāṇāṃ dīpanārthāya kuryāttarpaṇamapyatha || 

p. 126) 

kāmike - 

hastābhyāṃ tarpaṇaṃ proktaṃ prīṇanaṃ tarpaṇaṃ smṛtam | 
mantrāstu mūlabrahmāṅgā hetavo munisattamāḥ || 

tadyathā - oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivāya svāhā | oṃ hom īśānāya 
svāhā | oṃ heṃ tatpuruṣāya svāhā | oṃ hum aghorāya svāhā | 
oṃ hiṃ vāma devāya svāhā | oṃ haṃ sadyojātāya svāhā | oṃ 
hāṃ hṛdayāya svāhā | oṃ hīṃ śirase svāhā | oṃ hūṃ 
śikhāyai svāhā | oṃ haiṃ kavacāya svāhā oṃ hauṃ netrebhyo 
svāhā | oṃ haḥ astrāya svāhā | sarvebhyo mantrebhyo svāhā | oṃ 
sarvebhyo devebhyo svāhā | ityaṅgulyagraissakuśākṣata 
vāribhirdevatīrthena tarpayet || 

p. 127) 

kaṇṭhopavītiḥ(tī?) oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyo ṛṣibhyo namaḥ | 
ityaṅguliparvasandhirūpa tīrthena munīn tarpayet || 

oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyo manuṣyebhyo vaṣaḍiti kaniṣṭhāmūlarūpa 
manuṣyatīrthena manuṣyān tarpayet | 

oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyo bhūtebhyo vauṣaḍiti karatalamūlamaṇibandharūpa 
bhūtatīrthena bhūtāni tarpayet || 

atha dakṣaskandhopavīti(tī?) kuśamūlya(lā?)greṇa satilavāriṇā 
oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyaḥ śivapitṛbhyaḥ svadhā || oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyo 
devapitṛbhyaḥ svadhā || oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyaḥ pitṛbhyaḥ svadhā ||

jñātibhyaḥ svadhā || oṃ hāṃ sarvebhya ācāryebhyaḥ svadhā | 
iti tarjanyaṅguṣṭhamadhyarūpapitṛtīrthena saṃtarpayet | tathā - 

p. 128)

vaṣaṭ caiva bhūtānāṃ vauṣaḍantakam || 

pitrādīnāṃ svadhāntaṃ syādyathā yogyopavītayuk |

pitṝṇāmapasavyena tarpayet * * samāhitaḥ ||

p. 129) 

kāmike -

viśeṣeṇa devādīn? tarpaṇaṃ kuru || 

idaṃ saṃkṣepatarpaṇaṃ bhavati | atra bālādīnāmadhikāraḥ | 
tathā - 

cintyaviśvasādākhye

svāhā | oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyaḥ śivāgamebhyaḥ svāhā | oṃ hāṃ 
sarvebhyaśśivebhyaḥ 

p. 130) 

svāhā || oṃ (hāṃ?) vasiṣṭhāya namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ pulastyāya 
namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ kratave namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ bhāradvājāya namaḥ |

vaṣaṭ | iti mānavatīrthena kaniṣṭhāmūlena manuṣyāṃ? 
samarpayet | 

p. 131) 

oṃ hāṃ sarvebhya ākāśādi pṛthivyantabhūtebhyo vauṣaḍiti 
saṃlagnakaratalamūlena tarpayet | atha dakṣaskandhopavītī 
kuśamūlāgreṇa satilavāriṇā oṃ hāṃ kavyavāhanāya svadhā |

yamāya svadhā | oṃ hām? aryamṇebhyaḥ svadhā | oṃ hām 
agniṣvāptāya svadhā | oṃ hāṃ barhiṣaṭ svadhā | oṃ hām 
ājyapāya svadhā | oṃ hāṃ somapāya svadhā | iti devapitṛn -

mahebhyaḥ svadhā | oṃ hāṃ 

p. 132) 

mātāmahebhyaḥ svadhā | oṃ hāṃ pramātāmahebhyaḥ svadhā | oṃ

svadhā | iti pretapitṝn |

dikpatibhyaḥ svadhā | oṃ hāṃ siddhebhyaḥ svadhā | oṃ hāṃ

huṃphaḍantāstreṇa sakuśatilaṃ jalatīre kṣipet || 

p. 133) 

athopavītī gaṇamambikādīnāṃ mantramuccārya saṃtarpya

taduktam - 

śrīmṛgendre - 

sandhyākāla paribhraṃśe śivaikādaśikāṃ japet |

p. 134)

ityātmārthapūjāpaddhatyāṃ

p. 135) 

gṛhanadītīra vanopavanādiṣu kvāpi deśe manorame pūjāgāraṃ

suprabhede - 

liṅga(ṅgā?)ddhastaśataṃ sārdhaṃ śivakṣetraṃ samantataḥ | 
yasya tatraiva vāsitvaṃ śivalokasya kāraṇam ||

sahasra(sraṃ?) syārṣe(syādārṣe?) caiva tadardhakam ||

tatsyā(tasmā?)dāvasathaṃ kāryaṃ śivakṣetrasamīpataḥ || 

ātmārthaṃ pūjayetssarvassopahāraṃ śivasyā | tasmātpatraphalaiḥ 
puṣpairambhasāmanayā? budhaḥ || 

p. 136)

puṣpārāma? maṭhādīnāṃ sthānaṃ sarvatra kalpayet |

kiraṇe ca - 

mahānadītaṭe ramye jane copavane'pi vā | parvatāgre ca vṛkṣe vā 
gṛhe vā lakṣaṇānvite || 

p. 137) 

kālottare 

devālayasamīpe ca puṇyanadyāśca tīrake | athavā suddhagehe'pi 
śivayāgaṃ prakalpayet ||

pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā gṛhārāme śivālaye | nadītīre taṭāke

anyeṣu nadītīrādideśeṣu yatheṣṭadikpradeśe kuryāt ||

aiśānyāṃ diśi saṃsthaṃ tu pūjāgāraṃ manoramam | 

kiraṇe ca - 

garuḍaṃ(ḍaḥ?) -

kīdṛśaṃ śastaṃ brūhi me tasya lakṣaṇam || 

p. 138) 

(śvaṃ?) catuśśālā gṛhāṇāṃ tu tatrānyā kalpanā matā | 
pūrvādhā śrīgṛhaṃ proktamāgneyāṃ syānmahānasam || 

śayanaṃ dakṣiṇāyāṃ syānnair-ṛtyāmāyudhāma(la?)yam |

vittānusārataḥ kāryaṃ catuśśālamathāpi vā || 

hiraṇyanābhamathavā sukṣetraṃ vā manoramam | 
(hiraṇyanābhasukṣetrādi śālāsvarūpamaṃ pratikāyāṃ 
manoramaḥ|) 

udakplavaṃ sadākāryaṃ sarvaśālānvitaṃ śubham || 

p. 139) 

mahānasamathāgneyāmaiśānyāṃ yāgamaṇḍapam | dhānyasya sarvadā 
śastuṃ sthāpanaṃ vā rūdhvani || 

yāmyāgneyāntikaṃ kāryaṃ sthāpanaṃ salilātmanām | 
bhāṇḍānāṃ muniśārdūla vidhivatparikalpitam (nām) ||

gṛhaṃ kāryaṃ vasettatraiva buddhimān || 

athāsya nirmāṇaprakāraḥ - 

tatra prathamaṃ mahītalavarṇagandharasaiḥ parīkṣya svasvocitaṃ 
parigṛhya viśodhya śilāmayaṃ pakveṣṭakāmayaṃ śuddhadārumayaṃ 
mṛṇmayaṃ vā caturaśramāyātā? suvṛttaṃ vā tripañca 
saptanavatrayodaśa saptadaśa viṃśatyekaviṃśa 
dvi(dvā?)triṃśaddhastānyatarapramāṇameka bhaumaṃ 

p. 140) 

dvibhaumaṃ tribhaumaṃ vā pūrvadakṣiṇa 
paścimānyataradvi(dvā?)raṃ manoramaṃ pūjāgāraṃ nirmāyataṃ 
pañcadhā vibhajya madhyabhāge śilāsūyo(dhe?) varjayitvā 
mṛṇmayāṣṭakāṃ vā catuṣkoṇastambhayuktāṃ stambharahitāṃ vā

p. 141)

p. 144) 

nighaṭṭanaṃ vālukābhirnimnonnata vivarjanam |

āryā(yā?)diśubhasaṃyuktaṃ yajamānānukūlataḥ | śilāṃ vā

sūkṣme tu - 

caturaśrāyataṃ vṛttaṃ yatheṣṭaṃ maṇḍapaṃ nyaset |

p. 145)

ācāryadakṣiṇe haste madhyamāṅgulimadhyame | parvadīrghasamaṃ 
tāraṃ nāhaṃ trividhamucyate ||

navahastaṃ saptahastaṃ pañcahastaṃ trihastakam | 

mohaśūrottare - 

ekabhaumaṃ dvibhaumaṃ vā tribhaumaṃ vā yathāsukham | 

p. 146) 

sarvajñānottare - 

dairghyaṃ taddviguṇaṃ kṛtvā hastārecaika?bhāginam | dvāraṃ 
tṛtīyake bhāge sārdhahastapramāṇataḥ || 

kiraṇe - 

kuryādvā paścimadvāraṃ dakṣiṇāṃ(ṇaṃ?) pūrvameva vā | madhye 
tadbhāgamātre tu saptaviṃśattadaṅgulam || 

dvātriṃśadatha ṣaṭtriṃśat * * * * bhavatyatha | aṅgulāni 
bhaveddvāraṃ viṃśatpañcāṅgulāni tu || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

vāhayetpaścimadvāraṃ pūrvadvāramathāpi vā | iti nityārcane 
śastaṃ mandiraṃ bhuktimuktidam || 

ekabhaumaṃ dvibhaumaṃ vā tadvidhānamihocyate | kṣetraṃ 
tatpañcadhā kṛtvā madhyabhāgena vedikā || 

aiṣṭikāmṛṇmayā śreṣṭhā naiva śailasudhāmayā | 
ṣoḍaśāṣṭāṅgulocchrāyaṃ koṇastambhādisaṃyutam || 

p. 147) 

kiraṇe - 

vicitra randhrasaṃyuktaṃ sakavāṭārgalānvitam | madhyastambha 
vihīnaṃ vā kartavyaṃ devatāgṛham || 

aṃśumati - 

garbhaveśmaikatālaṃ ca tatsamaṃ cārdhamaṇḍapam | tatsamaṃ 
vistṛtaṃ caiva pañcāṅgulonnataṃ tataḥ || 

pañcatālāyatāśraṃ ca turyatālaṃ ca vistṛtam | 
vidyāṅgulonnataṃ caiva nimnonnatavivarjitam || 

beramaṇḍapamākhyātaṃ yadvā samatale tataḥ | gomayālepanaṃ 
kṛtvā vāmadevena mantravit || 

garbhordhvamaṇḍapaṃ caiva dvitālaṃ caiva saṃkhyayā | tatsamaṃ 
vistṛtaṃ caiva caturaśrasamanvitam || 

p. 148) 

pañcatālāyatāśraṃ ca devatālaṃ ca vistṛtam | 
beramaṇḍapamākhyātaṃ tanmadhye tu dalaṃ kṣipet || 

sthaṇḍilaṃ patrahīnaṃ cedasurāṇāṃ praveśanam | astramantraṃ 
samuccārya patraṃ tatraiva vinyaset || 

prāsādaṃ manasā kalpya vedikāmānameva ca | 

evaṃ vidhivannirmitamaṇḍapavedikāyāṃ garbhagṛhasthānīyadeśe 
peṭakaṃ vinyasya beramaṇḍapa sthānīya deśe oṃ hām ādhāra 
śaktaye namaḥ ityabhyarcya tanmadhye - 

dvihastamāyataṃ pīṭhamekahasta pravistṛtam | 
dvādaśāṅgulamucchrāyaṃ yadvā hastonnataṃ dṛḍham || 

nānāvarṇakasaṃyuktaṃ madhye kamalabhūṣitam | 

p. 149) 

śrīmayamate - 

vyāsaṣaḍaṣṭā śivā bhātini caraṇāyatakamakampavājinakam | 
tadupari padmadalaṃ syātkarṇikāyuktamalaṃkṛtaṃ yathā śobham || 

evaṃ śobhitaṃ kheṭakaṃ nyasya tadupari namaninta?mānaṃ prasārya 
madhye nāgadantādinirmitaṃ pīṭhaṃ vinyasya tadupari paścimabhāge 
prabhāmaṇḍala sahitaṃ svarṇādirajataṃ padmapīṭhaṃ 
pūrvābhimukhaṃ sthāpayet | 

taduktam sūkṣme - 

ādhāraśaktimabhyarcya tanmadhye kheṭakaṃ nyaset | 
tadūrdhvennamanimañchādya ūrdhvapīṭhe tu paścime || 

tadūrdhve padmapīṭhaṃ syātprabhāmaṇḍala saṃyutam | 

p. 150) 

tathā aṃśumati - 

tasyordhve caivamātmānaṃ citravastrādyalaṃkṛtam | kheṭake 
paścimadeśe padmapīṭhaṃ ca vinyaset || 

prabhāmaṇḍalasaṃyuktaṃ dāmādhāraṃ ca vinyaset | 
prabhāmaṇḍalapārśve tu citravastrairalaṃkṛtam || 

tataḥ pūjāmaṇḍapaṃ prakīrṇakusumadhūpāmodacandana 
carcāvitāna muktādāma patākadhvajakarpūra kṣoda 
prara?vāraddarpaṇādibhiralaṃ kurvīta - 

tathā mataṅge - 

suguptaṃ vijanaṃ ramyaṃ prakīrṇa kusumojjvalam | 
sudhūpāmodagandhāḍhyaṃ carcitaṃ candanādibhiḥ || 

vitānopari sañchannāmuktādāmāvalambitam | 
sitavastrapatākāḍhyaṃ sitacchatra dhvajocchritam || 

p. 151) 

kuṅkumodakasaṃkṣiptaṃ karpūrakṣodadhūsaram | 
niṣkampācciḥ(rciḥ?) pratī(dī?)pārthaṃ darpaṇāṃśuprapūritam 
|| iti || 

vidhivannirmitaṃ pūjāgāraṃ praviśya saurapūjāṃ kuryāt | 

tathā saurapūjai(jā?) || 

aṃśumati - 

pūjāsthalaṃ praviśyātha bhṛṅgāraka jalairyutam | 
pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā taccheṣaṃ varjayejjalam || 

punarjalaṃ samādāya ācamya vidhinā budhaḥ | maṇṭapānte 
praviśyātha saurapūjāṃ samārabhet || 

devendrānilayormadhye sūryamūrtiṃ śivaṃ yajet | 

iti || atra kathadinda(mayaṃ?)niyama iti cet tatraiva 
prabhāvatīnāmno bhānurūpasyāvasthānādityāhuḥ | 

p. 152) 

nanu nya?tra(nvatra?) sauramūrtimeva śivaṃ yajediti tyaktam | 
itareṣāṃ candrādīnāmapi śivamūrtitvāt teṣāmabhyarcanaṃ 
kimiti na kriyate | satyam | kiṃtu ṣaḍādhāraśivārcanameva 
nityamityuktatvātteṣāṃ pūjā na kriyate | uktaṃ ca 

mohaśūrottare - 

sūryopasthānamātmejyā liṅgāni gurupustakam iti nityaṃ 
samākhyātaṃ ṣaḍādhāraśivārcanam || iti || 
sūryaśivayoraikyācca tadeva kartavyamuktam - vātule - 

ādityaṃ ca śivaṃ vidyācchivamādityameva ca | ubhayorantaraṃ 
nāsti ādityasya śivasya ca || 

p. 156) 

tahi(rhi?) śivārcanameva prathamaṃstviti(mamastiva?) cet tathāpi 
sūryassakalo yataḥ ata eva prathamamarcanīyaḥ | tathā aṃśumati - 

sakalassūryarūpastu niṣkalaśśiva eva ca | dehasthaṃ 
pūrvamārādhya paścāddevaṃ yajedbudhaḥ || 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

atha nandimahākālau daṇḍipiṅgalasaṃjñakau | śivassū(sū?)rye 
śivāṅgāni mantrā vidyeśvarā grahāḥ || 

iyaṃ saurapūjā ca dvividhā | cakrapūjā berapūjā ceti | tathā 
aṃśumati - 

sauraṃ pratimabere ca cakre vāpi samarcayet | sthaṇḍile 
padmamālikhya yathā pūrvoktamārgataḥ || 

p. 154) 

sūkṣme ca - 

kheṭake bhūtale vāpi saurapūjāṃ samārabhet | 
pūrvāśābhimukhaṃ prāpya lekhayedravimaṇḍalam || 

bera(re?) tu cakralikhite yatheṣṭaṃ maṇḍalaṃ likhet | 

vijaye - 

maṇṭape vāpi kūṭe vā maṇḍale vā viśeṣataḥ | sauvarṇa 
tāmramāṇikkapravālapratimāsu vā || 

kāraṇe - 

ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi saurārcanavidhikramam | nityaṃ 
tamudayātpūrvaṃ cāturvarṇaṃ samarcayet || 

vṛttaṃ tryaśraṃ vṛttaṃ ca ṣaḍaśraṃ vṛttayugmakam | 
aṣṭāśraṃ ca kalāśraṃ ca sauracakraṃ prakīrtitam || 

p. 155) 

vṛttasya madhye praṇavaṃ trikoṇeṃgārakaṃ likhet | punarvṛttaṃ 
likhetpañca bahiraṣṭākṣaraṃ nyaset || 

sudarśanaṃ tu ṣaṭkoṇe ṣaḍaṅgaṃ tatra sandhiṣu | vṛttayugme 
mahāsauraṃ gāyatrīṃ śirasā nyaset || 

aṣṭasvaṣṭākṣaraṃ nyasya indrādindevatānnyaset | kalāśca 
svaraṃ proktaṃ likhecca vidhivattataḥ || 

krāndrasāndraṃ ca randhreṣu śrīṃ hrīṃ klīṃ bṛja samantataḥ 
vāyvagniśakravaruṇośvara? rākṣaseṣu 

pradeśapatralikhitairathavādivarṇān | mahāmaṇṭapamadhyasthaṃ 
sauracakraṃ prakīrtitam || 

caturvedāgamamā?dītaṃ sarvapāpaharaṃ bhavet | vipreṇaivārcanaṃ 
kāryaṃ kṣatriyasyārcanaṃ śṛṇu || 

p. 156) 

vṛttaṃ ca vṛttapadmaṃ ca ṣaḍaśraṃ vṛttapadmakam | aṣṭāśraṃ 
caturaśraṃ ca likhetpraṇavamadhyame || 

sūryamantra?(ṃ?) dale nyasya śrīṃ hrīṃ caiva likhecchubham | 
kṣatriyasyārcanaṃ kuryā vaiśyānāmarcanaṃ śṛṇu || 

vṛttaṃ padmaṃ tathā padmaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛttamaśrakam | tāraṃ 
madhyadalenyasya sūryanāma likhedbudhaḥ || 

hrīṃkāramaśra(ntra?)saṃyuktaṃ śrīvañcaiva dale likhet | 
vaiśyānāmarcanaṃ kuryā śṛṇu śūdrasya cārcanam || 

vṛttaṃ ṣaḍaśrakaṃ caiva vṛttaṃ padmaṃ ṣaḍaśrakam | madhyame 
vedikaṃ proktaṃ sūryamantraṃ likheddale || 

śrīṃ hrīṃ caiva ṣaṭkoṇe śrīṃ hrīṃkāraṃ likhetpunaḥ || 

p. 157) 

berapūjā ca dvidhā sṛṣṭya(rthā?) muktyarthā ceti taduktam 
vātule - 

sṛṣṭya(rthaṃ) caiva muktyarthaṃ pūjā ca dvividhā bhavet | 
sṛṣṭyarthaṃ śaktisaṃyuktaṃ muktyarthaṃ kevalaṃ viduḥ || 

atha pūrvāsya upaviśya samācamya karāṅganyāsaṃ kṛtvā arghyaṃ 
saṃpādya darśanārghyaṃ dattvā yathāśakti japtvā 
pūjopakaraṇadravyāṇyupāhṛtya śivasūryaṃ maṇḍalādau yajet | 
tathā āsane prāṅmukho bhūtvā ādityārcanamārabhet || 

ācamya vidhivacchuddhaṃ karāṅganyāsamārabhet | 
yāgopakaraṇadravyānsarvānsaṃpādya yatnataḥ || 

p. 158) 

cintyaviśve - 

sūryātmānaṃ svayaṃ dhyātvā sādhayedarghyabhājanam 

mṛṇmayaṃ kanyasaṃ proktaṃ tāmrajaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet | suvarṇena 
kṛtaṃ pātramuttamaṃ ceti sammataḥ | caturaṅgulavistāramutsedhaṃ 
tu tadardhakam || 

arghyapātramiti proktaṃ jñātvā karma samārabhet | astreṇa 
prokṣayetpātraṃ raktacandanavāribhiḥ || 

tatpātrajalamāpūrya raktacandanataṇḍulaiḥ | 
raktapuṣpakuśairvāpi tilasarṣapataṇḍulaiḥ || 

lalāṭākṛṣṭabinduṃ ca nikṣipedamṛtākṛtim | 
aṅgairādityamabhyarcya kavacenāvakuṇṭhayet || 

astreṇa rakṣaṇaṃ kṛtvā jānubhyāmavanīṃ gataḥ | p. 159) 

āmastakāntaṃ nītvā tu sūryamaṇḍaladṛṣṭiyuk | 

oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ saḥ śivasūryāya nama iti darśanārghyaṃ dattvā 
yathāśakti japaṃ kuryāt | yadvā śivagāyatryādibhirarghyaṃ 
dadyāt - 

śivagāyatrimantreṇa praṇavena hṛdāthavā | sūryāyārghyaṃ 
pradātavyaṃ sakṛnnityaṃ prapūjayet || 

mṛgendre - 

divyāṃ samuditāṃ pūjāṃ saṃpādyārghyapurassaram | tato japtvā 
śivaṃ sāṅgamāvedyottaramārabhet || iti || 

tato(taḥ?) pātramastreṇa prākṣālya bindunirgatāntattathā 
surāmayena gandhodakena oṃ khaṃ khaṣotkāya nama ityāpūrya 
ṣaḍaṅgamūlairabhyarcya 

p. 160) 

tairevābhimantrya astreṇa saṃrakṣya kavacenāvakuṇṭhya 
vauṣaḍantamūlena dhenumudrayāmṛtīkṛtya 
tajjalairdravyātmasthānānyastreṇa saṃprokṣya puṣpaṃ 
kṣiptvāstreṇa vighnānvidrāvya sthaṇḍile 
likhitāṣṭadalāṣṭapadme tāmrādinirmitacakre bere vārcayet || 
atha kramaḥ - 

paścimadvāre svadakṣiṇe śākhāyāṃ oṃ aṃ daṇḍine namaḥ iti | 

daṇḍirdvārdakṣiṇe gauraḥ sthūlastundilakūrcavān | 
u(ma?)ṣībhājana lekhinyau dhārayanhastayordvayoḥ || 

prāṇināṃ karmasākṣī ca tatkarmāṇi likhatyasau | 

iti dhyātvābhyarcya tataḥ iti | 

p. 161) 

raktavarṇassuveṣāḍhyassūcīhastasamanvitaḥ | piṅgalo 
vāmapārśve tu pratīhāraśca daṇḍavān || 

iti dhyātvābhyarcya aiśānyāṃ oṃ aṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ - 

raktapadmāsanāsīnaṃ dantaṃ pāśāṅkuśau phalam | dadhānaṃ(ca) 
caturhastairgajavakraṃ gaṇādhipam || 

iti dhyātvābhyarcya āgneyyāṃ oṃ aṃ gurubhyo namaḥ | - 

jaṭāmakuṭaśobhāḍhyaṃ bhasmabhā(pā?)ṇḍara vigraham | 
yogapaṭṭayutaṃ brahmasūtrainnā(trairnā?)bhisthalopari || 

kṛtayogākhyamudrāṃ(draṃ?) ca dhyāyecchāntaṃ paraṃ gurum | iti 
dhyātvā saṃpūjya pīṭhe vā sthaṇḍile vā 

p. 162) 

ṣaḍutthā?sanaṃ pūjayet | madhye oṃ aṃ prabhūtāya namaḥ | 
āgneyyādicatuṣkoṇeṣu oṃ aṃ vimalāya namaḥ | oṃ aṃ sārāya 
namaḥ | oṃ aṃ ārādhyāya namaḥ | oṃ aṃ paramasukhāya namaḥ || 

siṃhavaktra(ṃ?) dvinetraṃ ca dvihastaṃ ca dvipādakam | 
vṛścikākaṇṭhamālaṃ ca muṇḍikāsanasaṃsthitam || 

iti dhyātvā iti sitaraktapītaharitasitarūpāṇyanyonyaṃ 
pṛṣṭhadarśananyathākrameṇa vicintyābhyarcya tadupari 
kandabījāṅkuranālapadmadalakesarakarṇikāsahitamabhinavasaroruhaṃ 
śvetapadmaṃ vicintya saṃpūjya 

p. 463) 

pūrvādidaleṣu dīptādyaṣṭaśaktiḥ oṃ rāṃ dīptāyai namaḥ | 
oṃ rīṃ sūkṣmāyai namaḥ | oṃ ruṃ rujāyai namaḥ | oṃ rūṃ 
bhadrāyai namaḥ | oṃ reṃ vibhūtyai namaḥ | oṃ raiṃ vimalāyai 
namaḥ | oṃ roṃ amoghāyai namaḥ | oṃ rauṃ vidyutāyai namaḥ | iti

p. 164) 

tathā sūkṣme - 

daṇḍiṃ ca piṅgalaṃ caiva gaṇeśaṃ gurumeva ca | 

vijayasaṃhitāyām - 

āsanaṃ kalpayetpaścātsiṃhapīṭhordhvapaṅkajam | prabhūtaṃ

pūrvādīśānaparyantaṃ madhye yā sarvatomukham visphūra(rā?)

hastadvayaṃ cordhvamukhaṃ kiñcidākuñcitāṅgulim |

visphureti samākhyātā devasānnidhyakāriṇī | 

p. 165) 

karṇikāmadhye māyātattvānta vyāptikamāsanaṃ vicintya oṃ aṃ 
arkāsanāya namaḥ ityāsanaṃ saṃpūjya tadupari bhuktikāmaścet

cāruvadanaṃ raktamālyānulepanam || 

caturbhujasamopetaṃ varahastāmbujadvayam | abhayavaradopetaṃ 
prabhāmaṇḍalamaṇḍitam || 

upavītasamāyuktamuṣāpratyuṣayānvitam | uṣādevīṃ dakṣabhāge 
pratyuṣāṃ vāmapārśvake || 

dvibhujāṃ ca dvinetrāṃ ca sarojavadanānvitām | 
sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktāṃ sarvalakṣaṇasaṃyutām || 

padmasthaṃ vā rathārohamaruṇena samanvitam | 

p. 166) 

suprasannaṃ sudīptāṅgaṃ dhyāyedevaṃ ravervapuḥ | iti 
śaktisahitaṃ devaṃ dhyātvā saṃpūjayet || 

muktikāmaścet - 

dhavalāmboruhārūḍhaṃ ḍāḍimī kusumaprabham | sphuradraktaṃ 
mahātejo vṛttamaṇḍalamadhyagam || 

aṃsāsaktasphuṭaśvetasanālābjakaradvayam | ekāsyaṃ 
cintayedbhānuṃ dvinetraṃ raktavāsasam || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ aṃ khaṃ khaṣotkāya sūryamūrtaye namaḥ 
ityuccārya baddhapuṣpāñjaliḥ sadāśiva tattvānta vyāptikāṃ 
mūrtiṃ vicintya vinyasya oṃ hāṃ bhānu netrābhyāṃ nama iti 
netratrayone?traṃ vinyasya puṣpairañjalimāpūrya lalāṭākṛṣṭa 
sphuradaruṇabindumañjalau śaktitattvāntavyāpakaṃ 

p. 167) 

vicintya oṃ hrāṃ hrīṃ saḥ śivasūryāya nama iti mūrtiṃ 
tejorūpāmāvāhinyāvāhya sthāpinyā sthāpya sannidhānyā 
sannidhāpya niṣṭhurayā nirodhya 

deva deva tridhāmeśa jagatprathama maṅgala | tiṣṭhatvaṃ sthāpito 
bhaktyā mayā sūrya jagatpate || 

iti evaṃ vijñāpya saṃnidhānārghyaṃ dattvā 
sannidhīkaraṇārthaṃ ca prītyarthaṃ ca bimbamudrāṃ pradarśayet | 
taduktam - 

dakṣavāme tu saṃyojya saṃprasārya taladvayam | 
madhyamāṅguṣṭhasaṃyogādbimbamudrā prakīrtitā || 

iti || sūryahṛdaya śiraśśikhākavacāstrāṇi 

p. 168) 

svasvasthāneṣu stanamadhye kavacaṃ parāṇyorastraṃ ca vinyasya 
saṃpūjya mūlenaikatvaṃ kṛtvā rakṣāvakuṇṭhane vidhāya 
vauṣaḍantamūlena dhenumudrāṃ mahāmudrāṃ ca pradarśya 
pādyādīni vauṣaḍantamūlena puṣpākṣatadravyāṇi dhūpadīpā 
camanārghyāṇi dattvā hṛdayenābhyaṅgadadhikṣīramadhu 
haridrāmalakādinā saṃsnāpya paścāt śaddhodakena saṃsnāpya 
calabimbaṃ cet pīṭhe āropya raktavastrapuṣpairalaṃkṛtya oṃ 
hrāṃ hrīṃ śivasūryāya nama iti puṣpāñjali trayeṇa 
saṃpūjyārghyaṃ dattvā padmamudrāṃ bimba mudrāṃ ca pradarśayet 
|| 

p. 169) 

taduktam - 

uttānau tu karau kṛtvā maṇibandhaṃ tu kārayet | madhyame 
kuñcitāṅguṣṭhau śeṣāḥ praviralāstathā || 

padmamudrā bhavedeṣā taloparitalaṃ nyaset | 

kiraṇe - 

aṅguṣṭhāṅguṣṭhasaṃyogāddarśanīdvayayogataḥ | śeṣā(ḥ?) 
prasārya bimbākhyamudreyaṃ bhāskarasya tu || 

atha bhogāṅgapūjārthaṃ 
vijñāpyāgneyaiśānanair-ṛtavāyavyadaleṣu hṛdayādimantrān 
sūryahṛdayādisthānebhyoṃginogānyā('gnino'ṅgānyā?)dāya 
aṅgāni saumyarūpāṇi dvibhujānmujva(nyujjva?)lāni ca 
varadāmbujahastāni bhānoraṅgāni bhāvayet iti bhānoraṅgāni 
bhāvyābhyarcya paścimadale 

p. 170) 

jvalatpāvakasaṃkāśaṃ dvinetraṃ cojjvalaprabham | iti dhyātvā 
netraṃ saṃpūjya pūrvādicaturdaleṣvastraṃ ca - 

daṣṭrākarālavadanaṃ prajvalatpāvaka prabham | 
tarjayānvighnasaṃpātamitthamastraṃ vicintayet || 

iti dhyātvā gandhādibhiḥ saṃpūjya svasvamantrairarghyaṃ dattvā 
hṛdayādīnāṃ dhenumudrayā netrayorgoviṣāṇamudrāmastrasya 
trāsanīmudrāṃ ca pradarśayet || tathāgamaḥ - 

dhenumudrāṃ caturṇāṃ tu trāsanyastrasya locane | 
darśayedgoviṣāṇākhyāṃ sannidhīkaraṇāya tu || 

p. 171) 

ajite - 

tarjanīmadhyamādyāṃ(bhyāṃ?)tu śliṣṭābhyāṃ dakṣiṇasya ca | 
vāmahastatale kuryāttāḍanaṃ sakṛdeva hi || 

trāsanī syādiyaṃ mudrā trāsane pariyojayet | visphārya 
tarjanīmadhye goviṣāṇā prasārite || 

tadeva tasya netramudrā || 

athāvaraṇa pūjā pūrvādidalāgreṣu oṃ saṃ somāya namaḥ | oṃ 
buṃ budhāya namaḥ | oṃ braṃ bṛhaspate namaḥ | oṃ haṃ 
bhārgavāya namaḥ | ityāgneyādidalāgreṣu oṃ aṃ aṅgārakāya 
namaḥ | oṃ śaṃ śanaiścarāya namaḥ | oṃ raṃ rāhave namaḥ | 
oṃ kaṃ ketave namaḥ ityabhyarcya 

p. 172) 

pūrve somasthitassaumyassākṣasūtrakamaṇḍaluḥ | āgneyyāṃ 
maṅgalo rattāśśaktyakṣī raktalocanaḥ || 

yāmyāṃ pīto budhaścāpi sākṣasūtrakaraśca saḥ | vṛddho gauro 
gurussākṣakuṇṭhīhastastunair-ṛte || 

dakṣe kākṣassitaśśukraḥ kuṇṭhakṣaṃ valayaṃjale | 
vakrapādastvaya krūradṛṣṭibhīmaśśanaiścaraḥ || 

khiṃkharī japamālīñca rājāvatti nibho'nile | 
vyāvṛtāsyordhvadehī ca rāhuḥ kṛṣṇo'kṣacarmadhṛt uttare 
śāṃkare ketu strīphaṇī khaḍgakheṭabhṛt || 

naranāgārdhadehiśca dhūmrovātha kṛtāñjaliḥ yadvā soma budha 
bṛhaspatiśukrāḥ pūrvādidikṣu maṅgalādyā vahnyādikoṇagāḥ 
pūjyāḥ 

p.149) 

kāmarūpadharān sarvān divyābharaṇabhūṣitān | vāmorunyasta 
hastāṃśca dakṣahastābhayapradān || 

iti dhyātvā samabhyarcya iti tathā - 

kiraṇe - 

somādīnāṃ namaskāraṃ dattvā dhyāyedgrahān kramāt iti || 
taduktam 

satatāṅgulisaṃlagnahastau hṛddeśamāśritau | sarveṣāṃ 
bandhanaṃ kāryaṃ vāmāṅguṣṭhena pīḍya ca || 

namaskāreti vijñeyā sarvakarmaṇi kārayet | 

iti || tadanvācamanīyaṃ dattvā dhūpadīpanaivedya 
tāmbūlamukhavāsādibhiḥ santoṣyāntarāntarā camanārghyaṃ 
dattvā mūlamantraṃ yathāśakti japitvā arghyāmbhasā 
svāhāntamūlena 

p. 174) 

bhāskarāya cūlikodaken? samarpyāṅgānāṃ yathāśakti japaṃ 
kṛtvā nivedyāṣṭapuṣpikāṃ dattvābhīṣṭaṃ prārthya stutvā 
namaskṛtyāstrādīnāmaṅgānāṃ ca khaṣelkinā svāhāntena 
parāṅmukhārghyaṃ dattvā kṣamasvetyuktvā huṃ 
phaḍantāstramantreṇa bhogasthānasthitān mantrānnārā ca 
mudrayotthāpya oṃ khaṃ khaṣolkāya sūryamūrtaye nama iti 
divyamudrayā mūrtilīnān vibhāvayet || 

kṛtvāñjaliṃ karābhyāṃ tu aṅguṣṭhānāmikānvitam | 
prasārayettataśśeṣā mudreyaṃ divyarūpiṇī || 

iti | mūrtau sāpekṣaṃ vivarjanaṃ kuryāt | 

p. 175) 

amūrtau maṇḍalādau tu nirapekṣavisarjanaḥ janā? hṛdaya 
puṇḍarīkagataṃ vibhāvayet | aiśānyāṃ diśi gomayena maṇḍalaṃ 
kṛtvā oṃ aṃ tejaścaṇḍāsanāya namaḥ | oṃ aṃ 
tejaścaṇḍamūrtaye namaḥ | oṃ aṃ tejaścaṇḍāya namaḥ | 

daṣṭrākarāladīptāsyaṃ jaṭilaṃ raktavāsasam | dadhānaṃ 
ṭaṅkamambhoja tejaścaṇḍeśvaraṃ smaret || 

iti dhyātvā hṛdayādibhiḥ saṃpūjyārghyaṃ dattvā 
sūryanirmālyaṃ dattvā naivedyapānīyādi(dī?)n nivedya visṛjet || 

yadvā śivapūjānte visṛjet | kāmyārthī viśeṣapūjāṃ kuryāt 
|| 

p. 176) 

rakṣārthaṃ bhayanāśāya astraśastra nivāraṇam | 
saubhāgyajanalābhaṃ ca āyurvardhanamuttamam || 

caṇḍamāruta sarveṣāṃ tārakaṃ bhūmivardhanam | apasmārakṣayaṃ 
kuṣṭhaṃ tvatadoṣaṃ(ṣaḥ?) sarvanāśanam || 

etasmintaddinaṃ proktaṃ sarvasiddhipradaṃ param || 

ṣaṇmāsātsarvakāmyārthaṃ saṃvatsaramupāsitam | sarvakāryeṣu 
siddhyarthaṃ sūryaloke mahīyate || 

ityātmārtha pūjāpaddhatyām ātmārtha pūjāsthāna 
saurapūjāvidhiḥ || 

p. 177) 

ātmārthapūjāvidhiḥ - 

atha śivayajanamārabhet | yajanaśabdo yaja devapūjāyāṃ 
saṅgatakaraṇadāneṣu iti dhātvarthavācakaḥ | atra ca 
devaśśivastasya pūjā | pūjya pūjakayoranyonyasamastarūpaṃ 
saṃgatakaraṇam | ahaṃ śivaṃ pūjayāmi | pūjā gṛhṇāmīti 
bhakti prasādaviśeṣaṇadānamubhayorānukūlyena pādyādisvātma 
samarpaṇāntadānaṃ bhuktimuktidānaṃ ca | etatparipūraṇārthaṃ 
śivayajanam | tacca śivasyāyadvare? jñānaśaktyātmike liṅge 
kāryam | śivasya dhāraṇā(nā?)manantatve'pi liṅgameva śreṣṭham 
| taduktam 

p. 178) 

bṛhatkālottare - 

pṛthivyāṃ yāni tīrthāni puṇyānyāyatanāni ca | vasanti 
liṅgamūle tu tasmālliṅgaṃ samarcayet || 

suprabhede - 

layaṃ gacchanti bhūtāni saṃhāre nikhilāni ca | sṛṣṭikāle 
yathā sṛṣṭiḥ? tasmālliṅgamudāhṛtam || 

dīpte - 

puruṣasya tu yaccihnaṃ puruṣavyaktikāraṇam | sadāśivasya 
taliṅgaṃ śivalliṅgamiti smṛtā || 

nādaliṅgamiti proktaṃ binduḥ pīṭhamiti smṛtam | 

liṅgaśabdena cātra liṅga citrīkaraṇa iti dhātvartha 
liṅgatyāliṅga iti sṛṣṭi sthityādibhiḥ 
kṛtyairjagaccitrīkarotīti pārameśvaraṃ tejo'bhidhīyate | 

p. 179) 

tadvyaktisthānaṃ tu śai(cai?)śādikamupacārālliṅgaśabdenocyate | 
yadāhurguravaḥ - 

liṅgaṃ? tañci(tacci?)tritaṃ yena jagatkṛtyaiśca pañcabhiḥ | 
tatteja śābha(mbha?)vaṃ liṅgaṃ bhāttaṃ(ktaṃ?) tadvyaktibhūmikam 
|| 

iti || asmin liṅge śivapūjā kāryā svaśreyaskarīnta?thā | 

kālottare - 

sarvatra saṃsthitaṃ(taḥ?) pūjyaḥ sarva(rvā?)dhāreṣu sarvadā | 
tathāpi liṅge bhagavānpūjāṃ gṛhṇāti dhūrjaṭiḥ || 

asaṃpūrṇā kṛtā pūjā mantradravya kriyāguṇaiḥ | tathāpi 
liṅge saṃpūrṇā pūjā bhavati ṣaṇmukha || 

na ca nyūnādhikā kāryā pūjā vai sthaṇḍile suta | nyūnādhikā 
tu yā pūjā phalahīnā prakīrtitā || 

p. 180) 

ebhirvā kāraṇairvatsa liṅgārādhanamārabhet iti | 

sthiraliṅget? sadā kāryaṃ siddhikāmaiḥ prayatnataḥ | 
bhuktimuktikaraṃ puṃsāṃ calaliṅge śivārcanam || 

pūjāpadanirvacanam santānasaṃhitāyām - 

pūryate sarvakāmārthaṃ jāyate jñānamātmani | pūraṇājjāyate 
yasmātpūjāśabdamihocyate || 

seyaṃ pūjā dvividhā | parārthā ātmārthā ceti | 

kāmike - 

athārcanāvidhiṃ vakṣye bhuktimukti phalapradam | parārthā(rthaṃ?) 
svārthamityevaṃ dvividhaṃ tatprakīrtitam || 

grāmakheṭe purādau vā nadyāṃ parvata eva vā | 

p. 181) 

aṣṭaṣaṣṭimahākṣetreṣvanyatra ca manorame | 
svayambhudaivagaṇārṣaliṅgayukte śivālaye || 

mānuṣaiḥ sthāpite liṅge parārthayajanaṃ smṛtam | dīkṣānte 
guruṇā datte liṅge vā sthaṇḍilādike || 

yajanaṃ svārthamākhyātaṃ svasyaiva phaladaṃ yataḥ || 

ātmārthaṃ tu kṣaṇī(ṇi?) kādau calaliṅge vā kāryam - 

makuṭe 

guru?dehe śubhe liṅge sthaṇḍile vā nijātmani | kṣaṇike maṇḍale 
toye cātmārthayajanaṃ smṛtam || 

suprabhede - 

kṣaṇike calaliṅge ca sthaṇḍile maṇḍale'pi ca | kautuke 
bhitticitre vā ātmārthārcanamārabhet || 

p. 182) 

kāraṇe - 

sikataṃ taṇḍulaṃ caiva nadīmṛdgomayaṃ tathā | navanītaṃ ca 
rudrākṣaṃ bhasmacandanakūrcakam || 

puṣpamālāgulaṃ piṣṭaṃ kṣaṇikaṃ dvādaśocyate | 

ajite - 

kṣaṇikānāṃ tu liṅgānāṃ pīṭhaṃ ne(nai?)vāsti lakṣam | 

atraiva kṣaṇikānāṃ pūjane viśeṣa uktaḥ - 

sikataṃ taṇḍulaṃ cānnaṃ dvijenaiva samarcitam | nadīmṛdgomayaṃ 
caiva navanītaṃ nṛpeṇa ca || 

vaiśyena pūjitaṃ caiva phalaṃ piṣṭaṃ ca candanam | śūdreṇa 
pūjitaṃ caiva kūrcamālyagulaṃ bhavet || 

p. 183) 

kṣaṇikānāṃ phalam aṃśumati spuṭamuktam - 

sikataṃ muktidaṃ proktaṃ taṇḍulaṃ bhuktidāyakam | 
nadīmṛttyā(tsnā?)tmaśuddhiḥ syādannamāyuṣyavardhanam | gomayaṃ 
roganāśārthaṃ navanītaṃ sukhapradam || 

rudrākṣaṃ rudrarūpatvaṃ piṣṭaṃ puṣṭipradāyakam | gandhaṃ 
dīptakaraṃ caiva kūrcaṃ saubhāgyameva ca || 

puṣpaṃ bhogārthamevaṃ tu jalaṃ sāyujyameva ca | sarvapāpaharaṃ 
bhasma gulaṃ prītipradaṃ bhavet || 

phalaṃ cābhīṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ pallavaṃ sadguṇapradam | 
kṣaṇikānāṃ tu liṅgānāṃ phalamevaṃ prakīrtitam || 

kṣaṇikaṃ sikatādiṃ ca pūjayettu vidhānataḥ | snapanāvāhanaṃ 
caiva sarvaṃ mānasakalpanam || 

p. 184) 

arcanānte visṛjyāthā? na pramāṇaṃ na ca sthiram | rudrākṣaṃ 
kṣālanācchuddhiranyeṣāṃ parivarjayet || 

kṣaṇikārcanamevaṃ syāt gurumārgeṇa dṛśyate | kṣaṇikādi 
nivedyaṃ ca pṛthulaṃ gulamudgakam || 

phalādi caiva naivedyamevameva krameṇa tu | ca(ci?)traṃ 
caivārdhacitraṃ ca paṭacatraṃ(ci?) krameṇa tu || 

saudhajaṃ citrabhedaṃ syādardhacitraṃ ca bhittikam | paṭe tu 
paṭacitraṃ syāttrividhaṃ liṅgamarcayet || 

saudhajaṃ sukhamāpnoti saundaryaṃ bhitticitrake | paṭacitre śriyaṃ 
caiva citraliṅgārcanātphalam || 

maṇḍaladvitayaṃ proktaṃ padmaṃ vā liṅgameva vā | padme tu 
śriyamāpnoti liṅge vā sarvakāmadam || 

p. 185) 

kṣaṇaliṅgādibhedānāṃ phalamevaṃ prakīrtitam | teṣu bhedeṣu 
liṅgeṣu calaliṅgaṃ viśiṣyate || 

mānasānmaṇḍalaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ maṇḍalātkṣaṇikaṃ bhavet | 
kṣaṇikātphalamutkṛṣṭaṃ paṭaṃ caiva tataḥ param || 

paṭādābhāsamutkṛṣṭamābhāsādbimbamucyate | 
bimbādvailiṅgamutkṛṣṭaṃ liṅgādvai niṣkalaṃ param || 

sthaṇḍilādermantrasiddhipradāyakatvātsarvasiddhaye śivo liṅga eva 
pūjanīyaḥ | tathā - 

āśrayetsiddhidaṃ liṅgaṃ sarvasiddhipradāyakam | sthaṇḍile'pi hi 
mantrāṇāmāśu siddhipradānataḥ || 

iti || cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

ātmārthaṃ calaliṅgaṃ ca tadviśeṣamiha śṛṇu | 

p. 186) 

śailajaṃ lohajaṃ caiva ratnajaṃ ca tridhā bhavet | 

eteṣāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ calaliṅgapratiṣṭhā prakaraṇe vakṣyāmaḥ | 
idamātmārthayajanaṃ dvividhamantaryajanaṃ bahiryajanaṃ ceti | tatra 
tāvadātmadṛṣṭīnāmantaryajanameva ca | tadanyeṣāṃ tu 
antaryajanapūrvakaṃ bahiryajanam || 

sidhānte - 

yajanaṃ saṃpravakṣyāmi yathā vidhyanupūrvaśaḥ | dvividhaṃ tacca 
vijñeyamadhikārānurūpataḥ || 

yatīnāṃ yogināṃ caiva bāhyoparataśaktiṣu | antaryāgastu vihito 
bāhyapūjā pareṣu ca || 

aṃśumati - sūtakapretakāśaucinorudattyāśca? 
coragṛhītaliṅgasyāpi mānasa yajanamuttamam | 

p. 187) 

aṃśumati - 

sūtake pretakāśauce'pyātmārtha yajanaṃ smṛtam | pūjālopo na 
kartavyaḥ sūtake pretake'pi ca || 

śivamantreṇa kartavyaṃ snānamātreṇa śudhyati | āśaucaṃ na 
bhavettatra padmapatramivāmbhasā || 

pūjārthe praviccedaṃ māśaucaṃ ca viśeṣaṭaḥ | evaṃ 
liṅgārcanaṃ kuryātkāmyānuṣṭhānahīnakam || 

āśaucānte tu kartavyaṃ nityaṃ kāmyamanuṣṭhitam | 
kāmyānuṣṭhānahīnaṃ tu? taddine dviguṇamarcayet || 

evaṃ vai śuddhaśaivānāṃ viprādīnāṃ tu kathyate | sūtake 
pretake kuryānna svayaṃ liṅgapūjanam || 

svakarmānuṣṭhitasyaṃ? tu jalahīnaṃ tu bhasmakam | 

p. 188) 

mānasaṃ ca yajeddhīmānsarvaṃ mānasakalpanā | talliṅgaṃ 
pūjayitvā tu guruṇā vā svabandhunā || 

anyena vārcanaṃ kuryādante puṣpāñjalitrayam | āśaucānte ca 
kartavyā(vyaṃ?) pūrvamārgeṇa yājanam || 

kathaṃ puṣpavatī nārī arcanaṃ kurute vada | antaryāgaṃ 
prakartavyaṃ tayā pūrvadinatrayam || 

caturthe divase snātvā gavyaṃ prāśya yajettu sā | corairliṅgaṃ 
gṛhītaṃ cenmanasā tu yajettataḥ || 

catvāriṃśaddināntaṃ ca upavāsaṃ ca kārayet | nālikerodakenaiva 
kṣīreṇaiva phalādinām || 

ātmānaṃ rakṣayeddhīmān śivadhyana parāyaṇaḥ | 
tasmātsarvaprayatnena talliṅgaṃ grāhyameva ca || 

p. 189) 

labdhaṃ cet tatsamādāya prītiyokto viśeṣataḥ | liṅga śuddhiṃ 
kramātkṛtvā pūjayetpūrvavadbudhaḥ || 

talliṅgamātrālabdhaṃ cennavaliṅgaṃ tu saṃgrahet | 
svagurorliṅgamādāya pūjayetpūrvamārgataḥ || 

dṛṣṭaṃ cetpūrvaliṅgaṃ tu dvayaṃ saṃpūjayetkramāt | 
tasmātsarvaprayatnena maraṇāntaṃ yajetsadā || 

asya yajanasya ātmārthatraividhyaṃ ca sūkṣme pratipāditam 

ātmārthayajanaṃ vakṣye śruntatpra?bhañjana | ārogyamāyuḥ 
kīrtiśca vijayasya phalapradam || 

brahmahatyāvināśārthaṃ vīrahatyā vināśanam | gohatyā 
vināśārthaṃ pūjā vṛddhikaraṃ param || 

p. 191) 

sarvakāmaprasiddhyarthaṃ sarvayajñaphalapradam | 
ātmārthayajanātsāmyaṃ na bhūto na bhaviṣyati || 

śuddhaṃ kevalaṃ miśraṃ ca ātmārthaṃ trividhaṃ smṛtam | 
śuddhaṃ vā śaktirahitaṃ kevalaṃ śaktisaṃyutam || 

saurādicaṇḍaparyantaṃ miśraṃ caiva prakīrtitam | aṃśumati ca 
traividhyaṃ prakārāntareṇa pradarśitam || 

kevalaṃ sahajaṃ miśraṃ yajanaṃ trividhaṃ bhavet | kevalaṃ 
liṅgamekaṃ syātsahajaṃ pañcaberakam || 

maheśvarādibereṇa sahitaṃ miśramucyate | gaṇeśaṃ? skandaberaṃ 
ca śaktiṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ tathā || 

liṅgenaiva samāyuktaṃ sahajaṃ ceti kīrtitam | sahajena samāyuktaṃ 
maheśaṃ nṛttamūrtinam || 

p. 191) 

kāmyārthaṃ yāni berāṇi sahitaṃ miśra ucyate | 

atra tu traividhye'pi kaścidviśeṣa niyama uktaḥ | yathā - 

śuddhakevalasaṃmiśrapūjā ca trividhā bhavet | jitendriyārcitaṃ 
śuddhaṃ kevalaṃ śaivamarcayet || 

ācāryo miśrakaṃ caiva kevalaṃ śuddhamarcayet || 

kāraṇe - ekaliṅgādiphalaṃ nirūpitam- 

ekaliṅgaṃ tathā mokṣaṃ vyādhirūpaṃ dvayaṃ bhavet | 
liṅgatrayaṃ tathā kuryādbhogamokṣapradāyakam || 

caturliṅgaṃ tu yatraiva āyuḥśrīkīrtihāni ca | pañcaliṅgamiti 
śreṣṭhaṃ pañcapātakanāśanam || 

ṣaṣṭhaṃ cenmāraṇaṃ kuryātsaptamaṃ bhavanāśanam | 

p. 192) 

aṣṭamaṃ tu mahāvyādhirathavā śatruvardhanam | navaliṅgaṃ tathā 
ceṣṭā śivaloke mahīyate || 

atra caturṇāmapi varṇānāmādi śaiva mahāśaivānuśaivāntara 
śaivādīnāṃ tattatstrīṇāṃ cādhikāraṃ(raḥ?) || 
ādiśaivādīnāṃ lakṣaṇamasmādi? auśauca dīpikāyāṃ 
pradarśitam || aṃśumatyātmārtha pūjāyām | adhikāro nirūpitaḥ 
- 

viprakṣatriyavaiśyāśca śūdrāścaiva tu dīkṣitāḥ | 
ātmārthamarcanaṃ kuryānna kuryācca parārthakam || 

cāturvarṇodbhavastrīṇāmarcanaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ | 

makuṭe - 

viprakṣatriya vidacchūdrā dīkṣītāśca praveśitāḥ | 
ātmārthayajanaṃ kuryānna kuryācca parārthakam || 

p. 193) 

sūkṣme - 

brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyastathā śudrastathaiva ca | jaṅgamaṃ 
pūjayennityamātmārthe tu viśeṣaṭaḥ || 

santāne - 

ātmārthaṃ calaliṅgaṃ tu sthiraliṅgaṃ parārthakam | brāhmaṇaḥ 
kṣatriyaścaiva vaiśyaḥ śūdrastathaiva ca || 

ātmārthaṃ pūjayennityaṃ sandhyākāle'pyakālake | 

suprabhede -

paścimadvārapūjanameva kāryamiti tadeveha prastutam || 

atha dvārapālārcana prakāraḥ - 

sūkṣme - 

paścimadvāramastreṇa prokṣayettu śivāmbhasā | gaṇeśaṃ 
dakṣiṇe'bhyarcya vāgdevīṃ vāmapārśvake || 

p. 218) 

madhyame tu mahālakṣmīṃ nandī gaṅgāṃ ca dakṣiṇe | mahākālaṃ

vāmadakṣakaropāttavīṇāvādana tatparām ||

śivāstraṃ dehalīmadhye pūrvavaccāsanādikam | 

p. 221)
