śeṣaḥ? |

cenna || 

p. 244) 

tatra calaliṅgapūjāyāṃ vido?ṣābhāvāt | anyathā tatra

tva svīkāre ca 

p. 245)

kukṣipṛṣṭhoro guhyaśiśnau ūrū(ru?)yugalamaparo jānuyugmaṃ ca 
jaṅghe | dvisphik anyā ca pārśve caraṇa karayuga ghrāṇā maulī 
ca bāhu sthānāni syuḥ kalanā śiva tanukaraṇe 
triṃśatāmaṣṭakānām || 

atha śrīkaṇṭhanyāsaḥ oṃ aṃ śrīkhaṇṭho pūrṇo?daryai namaḥ 
iti kānte | oṃ āṃ anantāya virajāyai namaḥ lalāṭe | iṃ 
sūkṣmāya śālyai namaḥ dakṣiṇanetre | īṃ trimūrtaye 
lolākṣyai namaḥ vāmanetre | oṃ uṃ maheśvarāya vartulāyai namaḥ 
dakṣiṇaśrotre | 

p. 294) 

oṃ ūṃ arghīśāya dīrghakoṇāyai namaḥ vāmakareṇe | oṃ ṛṃ 
bhārabhūtaye dīvatmukhyai namaḥ dakṣiṇanāsāpuṭe | oṃ ṛṃ 
tithīśāya gomukhyai namaḥ vāmanāsāpuṭe | oṃ ḷṃ sthāṇave 
dīrghajihvāyai namaḥ dakṣiṇagaṇḍe | oṃ ḹṃ bhārāya 
kaṇṭhodaryai namaḥ vāmagaṇḍe | oṃ aiṃ caṇḍīśāya 
ūrdhvakeśinyai namaḥ uttaroṣṭhe | oṃ aiṃ bhautikāya 
vikṛtamukhyai namaḥ adharoṣṭhe | oṃ oṃ sadyojātāya 
jvālāmukhyai namaḥ ūrdhvadante | oṃ auṃ anugraheśāya 
ulkāmukhyai namaḥ adhodante | oṃ aṃ akrūrāya śrīmukhayai 
mūrdhni | oṃ aḥ mahāsenāya vidyāmukhyai namaḥ āsye | 

p. 295) 

oṃ kaṃ krodhīśāya mahākālyai namaḥ dakṣiṇabāhumūle | oṃ 
khaṃ caṇḍīśāya sarasvatyai namaḥ korpare | oṃ gaṃ 
pañcāntakāya sarva sarva siddhigauryai namaḥ maṇibandhe | oṃ ghaṃ 
śivottamāya trailokyavidyāyai namaḥ pāṇitale | oṃ ḍaṃ 
ekarudrāya mantraśaktyai namaḥ dakṣiṇa karāgre | oṃ caṃ 
kūrmāyātmaśaktaye namaḥ vāmabāhumūle | oṃ chaṃ ekanetrāya 
bhūtamātre namaḥ korpare | oṃ jaṃ caturmukhāya lambodaryai namaḥ 
maṇibandhe | oṃ jhaṃ ajeśāyadrāviṇyai namaḥ pāṇitale | oṃ 
ñaṃ śarvāya nagaryai namaḥ vāmakarāgre | oṃ ṭaṃ someśvarāya 

p. 296) 

khecaryai namaḥ dakṣiṇorumūle | oṃ ṭhaṃ lāṅgaline mañjaryai 
namaḥ jānuni | oṃ ḍaṃ dārukāya virupiṇyai namaḥ jaṅghāgre | 
oṃ ṭaṃ ardhanārīśvarāya? viriṇyai? namaḥ pādatale | oṃ ṇaṃ 
umākāntāya kākodaryai namaḥ dakṣiṇapādāgre | oṃ taṃ 
āṣāḍhāya pūtanyai namaḥ vāmorumūle | oṃ thaṃ daṇḍine 
bhadrakālyai namaḥ jānuni | oṃ daṃ atṛtīya yoginyai namaḥ 
jaṅghāgre | oṃ dhaṃ mīnāya śaṅkhinyai namaḥ pādatale | oṃ 
naṃ meṣāya gajinyai namaḥ vāmapādāgre | oṃ paṃ lohitāya 
kālarātryai namaḥ dakṣiṇapārśve | oṃ phaṃ śikhine kubjinyai 
namaḥ 

p. 297) 

vāmapārśve | oṃ baṃ cchagaraṇḍāya kapardinyai namaḥ 
pṛṣṭhabhāge | oṃ bhaṃ dviraṇḍāya bhadrāyai namaḥ nābhideśe 
| oṃ maṃ mahākālāya jayāyai namaḥ hṛde(di?) | oṃ yaṃ 
tvagātmane baline sumukhyaiśvaryai namaḥ tvaci | oṃ raṃ raktātmane 
bhujaṅgeśāya revatyai namaḥ rudhiragocare | oṃ laṃ māṃsātmane 
pinākine mādhavyai namaḥ māṃse | oṃ vaṃ medomayāya kharvīśāya 
vāruṇyai namaḥ medasi | oṃ śaṃ asthyātmane bakeśāya vāyavyai 
namaḥ asthini | oṃ ṣaṃ majjātmane śvetāya rakṣovadhāriṇyai 
namaḥ majjāsthāne | oṃ saṃ śuklātmane bhrukuṭīśāyai namaḥ 
retasi | oṃ haṃ praṇavātmane lakulīśāya lakṣmyai namaḥ hṛdaye 
| 

p. 299) 

ardhanārīśvarassoma skandaścāṣāḍhadaṇḍinau | atrimīnaśca 
meṣaśca lohitaśca śikhī tathā || 

chagaraṇḍo dviraṇḍaśca mahākālasya?gātmanau | bhujaṅgeśaḥ 
pinākī ca khaḍgīśaśca vā punaḥ || 

rakṣaśśukaśca lakulī śiva saṃvartaka smṛtaḥ | pūrṇodarī ca 
virajā tṛtīyo śālmalī bhavet || 

lolākṣī vartulā caiva dīrghakoṇā tathaiva ca | śākhinī 
garjinī kālarātrī kubjarathaiva ca || 

kapardinī tathā vajrā jayā ca sumukheśvarī | revatī mādhavī 
caiva vāruṇī vāyavī tathā || 

rakṣovadhāriṇī cānyā tathaiva sahajācadhaḥ | lakṣmīśca 
vyāpinī māyā cākhyātā varṇaśaktayaḥ || 

p. 300) 

etābhiśśaktibhissārdhaṃ vinyasedakṣarānkramāt | akāramūrdhni 
vinyasya ākāramukhavṛttake || 

ikāraṃ dakṣiṇe netre īkāraṃ vāmanetrake | ukāraṃ dakṣiṇe 
karṇe ūkāraṃ vāmakarṇike || 

ṛkāraṃ dakṣanāse ca ṝkāraṃ vāmanāsake | ḷkāraṃ 
dakṣiṇagaṇḍe ḹkāraṃ vāmagaṇḍake || 

ekāraṃ tu tathordhvoṣṭhe aikāramadhare tataḥ | 
okāramūrdhvadantaughe aukāramadhadantake || 

aṃkāratālumūle ca aḥkāraṃ tālumadhyage | evaṃ vidhiḥ 
krameṇaiva vinyasetṣoḍaśasvarān || 

kavargaṃ dakṣiṇe haste cavargaṃ vāmahastake | ṭavargaṃ 
dakṣiṇepāde tavargaṃ vāmapādake || 

p. 301) 

pakāraṃ dakṣiṇe pārśve phakāraṃ vāmapārśvake | bakāraṃ 
pṛṣṭhadeśe tu bhakāraṃ nābhyadhastataḥ || 

nābhau makāraṃ tasyordhve yakārārṇādisaptakān | 
tvagasṛṅmāṃse medo'sthimajjāśuklādidhātuṣu || 

hakāraṃ hṛdaye nyasya lakāraṃ gudadeśataḥ | kṣakāraṃ guhyake 
nyasya nyāsamantrasamanvitam || 

athavāntaryajanaṃ kuryāt | 

siddhānte - 

antaryāgaṃ purā kṛtvā bāhyapujāmathārabhet | jyotirliṅgaṃ 
hṛdisthāyat tathaivānyarcyate bahiḥ || 

yasmāttasmātpurā kāryaṃ antaryāgamihocyate | 

p. 302) 

śivadharmottare - 

akṛtvā mānasaṃ yāgaṃ yobahiryāgamācaret | vṛthā

ātmasthaṃ yaśśivaṃ tyaktvā bahiṣṭhaṃ yajate śivam | 
hastasthaṃ piṇḍamutsṛjya lihetkorparamātmanaḥ || 

kāmike - 

pūjāhomasamādhīnāṃ sthānāni parikalpayet | 
hṛdaye'rcāvidhānaṃ tu nābhau homaṃ prakalpayet ||

kalpayeddhāma caiśvarāvāhana kramāt || 

āsanaṃ kalpayettatra vakṣyamāṇavidhānataḥ | 

taduktam - niśśvāse - 

kadalīpuṣpasaṃkāśaṃ hṛdayaṃ sarvadehinām | 

p. 303) 

suprabhede - 

pārthivāṇḍamayaskandaṃ māpyādiṃ nālameva ca | pradhāna

niśśvāse - 

padmanālaṃ navadvāraṃ samantātparikīrtitam | nālamaṣṭāṅgulaṃ

āvṛteṣu daleṣvaṣṭau oṃ śivāya namo namaḥ | 
indrādīśānaparyantaṃ nyasedaṣṭākṣaraṃ kramāt || 

kālottare - 

p. 304)

pañcāśatparamaṃ bījaṃ gadyo?nīṃ parātparam |

aṅguṣṭhaparvatulyā tu karṇikā kesarairyutam |

vidyātattvamayīṃ tasya karṇikāṃ paricintayet | mantrāśca

niśvāse -

p. 305)

uktaṃ ca |

p. 306)

taduktam - śrīmatkiraṇe -

ityetairaṣṭabhiḥ puṣpaiḥ śivaṃ saṃpūjayetsadā | 

p. 307) 

tathā kāmike -

yathā yogaṃ nivedya ca ||

piṅgalānāḍicāreṇa nābhikuṇḍe svakodi?tam | śivāgniṃ

iḍāyā pūrakā kṛṣya sudhayā ca śivāṇubhiḥ |

hṛtpuṇḍarīkamadhyā?tvā suṣumnena pathānalam ||

tataḥśrupūjāṇāṃ samāpya? ca || 

kāmike - 

sthāṇusthāne smarelliṅgaṃ sphaṭikābhaṃ mahoṇjvalam | 
tammādhyāṅgaṃ? śivaṃ dhyātvā tadekaṃ bhāvamāśritam || 

p. 309)

p. 310) 

dharmajñānaṃ tathā nālaṃ vairāgyaṃ karṇikā guhe(ha?) |

p. 311) 

asyāyaṃ kramaḥ hṛdyurdhvamukhau karau kṛtvā oṃ hāṃ astrāya

|| atha dravyaśuddhiḥ ||

p. 313)

iti || sūkṣme -

p. 316) 

kaṅkaṇaṃ lambamālā ca bilvadūrvākṣataṃ tathā |

p. 317) 

marīcilavaṇaṃ caiva jīrakaṃ sarṣapaṃ tathā | kramukaṃ ca

pādyasyādiṃ? supātrāṇāṃ lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu sāṃpratam |

oṣṭhimekā?ṅgulaṃ proktaṃ ghanamardhārdhakaṃ bahvet |

p. 318) 

sarveṣāṃ kāraṇānnāṃ? ca etatkaraṇamucyate |

tacchreṣṭhaṃ svarṇapātraṃ ca rajataṃ madhyamaṃ bahvet |

saṃprayuktaṃ yattattannirmālyato bhavet ||

kuṣṭhaṃ sacandanaṃ vāpi ghṛtamiśraṃ sudhūpayet | kṛṣṇalohaṃ 
ca niryāsaṃ candanaṃ ca krameṇa tu || 

vyomapakṣatribhāgāntamīṣatkarpūramiśritam | uttamaṃ 
dhūpamākhyātaṃ madhunā ca pariplutam || 

uśīraṃ candanopetaṃ madhuyuktaṃ madhuplutam | bhāgaṃ 
tatpūrvavatproktaṃ madhyamaṃ dhūpamucyate || 

guggulaṃ ghṛtasaṃyuktaṃ kanyasaṃ dhūpamīritam | 

p. 343) 

goghṛtena ca tailena karpūravarttidīpitā | 
catustrīdvayaṃgulā?yāmā dīpajvālā viśiṣyate | kapilāghṛtena 
dīpaḥ syāt sitāraktāghṛtena vā || 

ajāghṛtena vā lābhe śuddhatailena vā hitam | kapilāyā ghṛtaṃ 
śreṣṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ tvanyajaṃ ghṛtam || 

adhamaṃ syādajāsarpiśśuddhatailaṃ tathaiva ca | sāttvikaṃ 
rājasaṃ caiva tāmasaṃ ca kramādbhavet || 

karpūravartīkaṃ dīpayetsarva siddhidam | śreṣṭho yugāṅgulā 
jvālā madhyamastu guṇāṅgulam | adhamo dvayaṅgulā jvālā dīpe 
hyevaṃ prakīrtitaḥ || 

vṛkṣabījodbhavaiḥ sneho(hai?)śśivadīpaṃ vivarjayet | 

p. 344) 

|| atha naivedyavidhirucyate || kāmike -

p. 349)

p. 360)

p. 362)

iti vicintya oṃ hāṃ śaktimaṇḍalādhipataye īśvarāya namaḥ 
ityabhyarcya tadupari kṣityādikuṭilāprānta vyāptikam ityāsanaṃ 
vibhāvya oṃ hā śivāsanāya namaḥ iti saṃpūjya puṣpāñjaliṃ 
hṛtpradeśe nidhāya oṃ hāṃ haṃ hāṃ śivamūrtaye namaḥ iti 
bindvantamuccārya mūrtiṃ saṃpūrṇa paramajyotirūpāṃ 
daṇḍākārāmavibhaktāvayavāṃ liṅganābhe? vinyasya 

p. 397) 

tasyāṃ mūrtau daṇḍabhaṅgyā 
mūrdhno(rdhā?)dipādāntamīśānādīnvinyasyāṣṭatriṃśatkalānya 
asaṃ śrīkaṇṭhanyāsaṃ ca vidadhyāt | 

yogapīṭhaṃ vibhāvyaivaṃ mūrtiṃ tasminniveśayet 

|| āvāhanavidhiḥ || 

kāmike - 

mūrtimīśvaraka?tattvāntaṃ liṅganāheta? vinyaset | 
hṛtsaṃpuṭaśca sānto'yaṃ mūrtimantramudāhṛdaḥ(taḥ?) || 

kalānyāsaṃ tataḥ kuryāttadvidhānamihocyate | 
sadeśadehasiddhyarthamaṣṭatriṃśatkalā matāḥ || 

aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsaḥ prāgeva pratipāditaḥ | lipinyāsaṃ tu 
vā kuryāt śrīkaṇṭhādiyutaṃ tu vā || 

p. 398) 

tasmānmūrtiṃ samāvāhya vidyādehaṃ prakalpayet | yā tasya vimalā 
śaktiśśivasya samavāyinī || 

saiva mūrtikriyābhedāt sadāśivatanurmatāḥ? | 
ṣaṣṭhasvarasamopetaṃ bindunādasamanvitam || 

sāntaṃ hṛtpuṭitaṃ smṛtvā vidyādehaṃ prakalpayet | bere 
sadāśive vāpi liṅge'pyevaṃ samācaret || 

kṣityādikuṭilāprāntaṃ mantrasiṃhāsanasthitam | 
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ dvātriṃśallakṣaṇānvitam || 

padmāsanasthaṃ pañcāsyaṃ prativaktraṃ trilocanam | 
dṛkkriyecchāviśālākṣaṃ jñānacandrakalānvitam || 

dhavaleśānavadanaṃ pītaṃ tatpuruṣānanam | 
kṛṣṇāghoramukhopetaṃ raktābhoraktavaktrakam || 

p. 399) 

suśvetaṃ paścimāsyekaṃ sadyojātaṃ prakīrtitam | nāgopavītinaṃ 
śāntaṃ jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍitam || 

divyagandhānuliptāṅgaṃ divyapuṣpairalaṃkṛtam | 
śaktyasīśūlakhaṭvāṅgavaravyagrakarāmbujam || 

dakṣiṇairatha vāmasthai ḍamaruṃ bījapūrakam | nāgākṣasūtre 
nīlābjaṃ bibhrāṇaṃ pañcabhiḥ karaiḥ || 

saṃcintya mūrterupari śaktimātra vijṛmbhaṇam | 
liṅgākāropamardena bhāvane liṅgatāṃ tyajet || 

iti mūrterupari oṃ hāṃ hauṃ hāṃ vidyādehāya namaḥ | iti 
nādāntaṃ śaktyantamuccārya sadāśivatattvāntavyāpakaṃ 
vidyutprabhāsragañjalā? vāpibhūtaṃ? dhyātvā liṅgadairghye 
vidyādehaṃ 

p. 400) 

vinyasya oṃ hauṃ netrebhyo namaḥ iti sarvajñatā bījaṃ netraṃ 
mukhaṃ pañcakanetreṣu vinyasyānantaram ṛjūrdhvakāyassan 
puṣpairañjalimāpūrya hṛtpradeśe nidhāya svagurupadeśena 
mūlamantramayaṃ tejaḥ puñjanibhaṃ hṛdayāgre paraṃ śivaṃ 
dvādaśānte niṣkalaṃ svaprakāśaṃ sarvaguṇasaṃpannaṃ 
paramaśivaṃ saṃcintya tato recakena mūlamantramati 
spaṣṭākārokārādimātrāvācyaṃ 
pṛthivyādisamānāntapāśajālamatikramya unmanā śaktyatīta 
paraśivamuccārya vācyavācakabhāvena śivasamarasībhūtaṃ 
mantrameva tejaḥ piṇḍaṃ 

p. 401) 

sarvajñatādiṣaḍaṅgopetaṃ parāmṛtasyandi bindurūpaṃ śivaṃ 
sṛṣṭyā pūrakeṇa samānīya bhrūbindau 
śaradindusahasravadābhāvya recakena puṣpāñjaligatāṃ dhyātvā 
tamāvāhya namomudrayā mūlena samānīya hṛdayenāvāhyasthāpinyā 
vidyādeha hṛdambuje saṃsthāpya sannidhānyā mūlena mūrtidvayaṃ 
vyāpya sannidhībhūtaṃ vicintya sannidhānārghyam dattvā 
niṣṭhurayā niruddhya nirodhārghyaṃ ca dattvā 
kālakaṇṭhyakā(ṇṭhyā?) phaḍantāstreṇa vighnānutsārya 
liṅgamudrāṃ hṛdā dhyātvā svāminsarvajagannātha 
yāvatpūjāvasānakam | tāvatsaṃprītibhāvena liṅge'smin sannidhi 
bhava || 

p. 402) 

iti vijñāpya svāgatārghyaṃ dattvā kavacenāvakuṇṭhya 
hṛdādyaṅgaissakalīkṛtya mūlenaikatvaṃ vidhāya dhenumudrayā 
mahāmudrayā ca śakti mantreṇāmṛtīkuryāt || 

śrīparākhye - 

evaṃ śāktaṃ vapuḥ kalpi(lpya?) tasminparamakāraṇam | paramaṃ 
śāśvataṃ satyajñānānanda sukhātmakam || 

vyāpakaṃ sarvatattvānāmaprameyamanūpamam | vācyavācakarahita 
vāṅmanātītagocaram || 

vyaktaṃ kuryādviśeṣeṇa niṣkalaṃ sakalātmani | 
parāvāhanamityuktaṃ śivamantra niyantraṇāt || 

iti || tathā - 

p. 403) 

navāttejasā?hvānaṃ vidhāyāhvānamudrayā | sthāpinyā sthāpanaṃ 
kuryāccaktivigrahasaṃśaye || 

praṇatyā sannidhīkṛtya nirudhyādrodhamudrayā | bindunāda 
samāyuktasāntaṃ mātrātrayānvitam | niṣkalaṃ paramaṃ binduṃ 
tadūrdhvādhenmi?nāntakam || 

mantramūrtiṃ śivaṃ sṛṣṭyā bhrūmadhye manasā smaret | 
candrabimbasamaprakhyaṃ smṛtvā puṣpāñjalisthitam || 

āvāhinyā punarnītvā sthāpinyā sthāpayeddhṛdi | 
sūkṣmamūrtigataṃ devaṃ vidyādehaṃ samantataḥ || 

vyāpya jīvamivāśliṣṭaṃ dehadehyavibhāgataḥ | 
svasminnabhimukhībhūtaṃ saṃsmaret sannidhānayā || 

pūjāvidhi(dhau?) tu tatreśaṃ rodhayenniṣṭhurākhyayā | 

p. 404) 

nirodhārghyaṃ tadā dadyāt sannidhānāya śaṃbhave | 
kālakaṇṭhyā phaḍantena hetinā puṣpamutkṣipan || 

antāyaṃ samutsārya liṅgamudrāṃ pradarśayet | hṛdā baddhvā 
namaskāraṃ kavacenāvakuṇṭhya ca || 

hṛdaye hṛdayaṃ śuklaṃ śiraḥ pītaṃ śirassu ca | śikhāṃ 
raktāṃ śikhāmadhye tanutraṃ cāsa(si?)taṃ hṛdi || 

agnijvālānibhaṃ śastramastravargeṣu vinyaset | ityevaṃ 
sannidhānena sakalīkṛtirīritā || 

śivaikavarṇatāṃ dhyāyedaṅgānāṃ bhinnavarṇinām | 
puṣpamāropya mūlena paramīkaraṇaṃ hi tat || 

amṛtīkaraṇaṃ kuryānmantrāṇāṃ dhenumudrayā | mahāmudrāṃ 
hṛdā badhvā dīpanaṃ ca prakalpayet || 

p. 405) 

svāgataṃ te mahādevetyuktvā saṃprārthayedvibhuma | 

nanu yadyāvāhyate śivaḥ tadā tasya visarjanaṃ ca bhavati | 
tatassarvagatvahāniriti cet || maivam | vyāpakaśivasyaiva bhaktyā 
mantreṇābhivyaktīkaraṇamāvāhanamucyate | uktaṃ ca - 

sarvatra vyāpinaṃ śāntaṃ devadevaṃ sadāśivam | 
abhimukhyakaraṇaṃ yattadāvāhanamihocyate || 

pūjāparisamāptau tu vimukhaṃ na visarjanam | 

nanu sarvagasya sarvatra sannihitatvādidamapi viruddhamiti cet || 
nātra tathā vidhasannidhānena prayojanābhāvāt 

p. 406) 

iha pūjyapūjaka bhāvānuviddha sādhakaśraddhānurūpamanugrāhyānu 
grāhakatva lakṣaṇasannidhānamāvāhanam | tacca 
vācyavācakayorabhedādvācakasya mantrasya 
viśeṣavinyāsādvācyasyāpi śivasya viśiṣṭā 
sannidhirbhaviṣyatīti na viruddhyate || 

nanu tathāpittasya(pitasya?) kathamekatrābhivyaktirāvāhanamiti cet 
|| satyam | tathāpi 
tarāvāmūlāgrasthitavahnerekatrābhivyaktivadupapatteḥ | 
kāṣṭhagatavahni vyaktermathanāpekṣā vadasyā api

p. 407) 

tarhi punaḥ punarāvāhanaṃ vyarthamiti cet | na 
kuṇḍagatāgnerjvālanāya punaḥ punarvāyvāpekṣā vadupapatteḥ || 

yathā kāmike tantrasāre - 

āmūlasyā(lā?)grasthitasyāgnerabhivyaktiryathā tarau | tadvadekatra 
cidvyaktirvyāpakasya na bādhyate || 

tathā kāmike - 

yathā kāṣṭhagato vahnirvyajayate mathanādinā | tathā 
mantraprabhāvena(ṇa?) bhaktyābhivyajyate śivaḥ || 

stanu?(satanu?)stanu hino vā sarvakṛtsarvagaśśivaḥ | tathā 
suprabhede - 

yathā kuṇḍagatasyāgnervāyunā jvalanaṃ bhavet | tathātra liṅge 
mantreṇa vyajyate bhagavānapi || 

p. 408) 

kāmike - 

yathātmanastanudvārā syātāmiha gamāgamau | tadvanmantratanudvārā 
śivasyeṣṭau gamāgamau || 

acintya śaktiyuktatvādvyāpako'vyāpakaśca saḥ | 

nanvātmanātītagocarasyārūpasya śivasya kathamekatra dṛśyateti 
cet | satyam | tathāpi adṛśyasya rāhoścandrārkamaṇḍale 
dṛśyatvavadādarśe svātmana iva kāṣṭhamadhye vahniriva toyakānte 
jalasyeva sūryakānte'nalasyeva maṇimadhye dyuteriva ghanamadhye 
vidyuta iva pāṣāṇe lohasyeva kṣīre sarpiṣa iva rātryante 
arkodaya iva śivasyāpi dradraśyarttho(dṛśyatvo)papatteḥ | 

p. 409) 

vastutastu śivasyāvāhanantasma(naṃ tatsma?)raṇam | visarjanaṃ 
tadvismaraṇamiti jñeyam || 

yathā vātule - 

śivaṃ śuddharūpaṃ(ddhasvarūpaṃ?) hi vāṅmanātītagocaram | 
kathamāvāhanaṃ tasya sthāpanaṃ sannirodhanam || 

īśvara uvāca - 

akhaṇḍā(ṇḍaṃ?) sarvaga(gaṃ?)vyoma alpapātre dhṛtaṃ yathā | 
tathāpyanantaṃ sarvatra vyāpitvaṃ ca na gacchati || 

arūpaṃ dhyānasaṃyuktaṃ rūpadhyānaṃ na eva ca | 
arupārupayuktātmāvāhayettu svarūpakam || 

adṛśyā dṛśyatāmeti rāhuścandrārkamaṇḍale | ātmā sarvagato 
buddhau dṛśyate vyāpya saṃsthitaḥ || 

p. 410) 

ādarśe nirmale lohe niścaitanye ṣaḍānana | paśyatyātmānamātmā 
ca nirmalāṃyā(lāyāṃ?) dhiyāmapi || 

tathaiva śivarūpaṃ hi jñātvā dehasya madhyame | kāṣṭhamadhye 
yathā vahnistoyakāntejalaṃ yathā || 

sūryakānte'nalaṃ yāvatthite bhinnopalipyate? | maṇimadhye 
dyutiryāvadghanamadhye tu jyotiṣu | pāṣāṇe lohavajjñeyaṃ kṣīre 
sarpiryathā tathā || 

tātryante'rkodayaṃ yāvattāvadehodayaṃ? guha | gurupadeśamārgeṇa 
smṛtirāvāhanaṃ bhavet || 

vismṛtaṃ ca tathaiveha visarjanamihocyate | evameva tu bhāvena 
e(bhā?) vayettu mahāśivam || 

p. 411) 

etadāvāhanaṃ trividhaṃ sālambaṃ nirālambaṃ saṃkramaṃ ceti | 
tathā 

āvāhanaṃ tridhā proktaṃ tathaivodvāsanaṃ tridhā | sālambaṃ ca 
nirālambaṃ saṃkramaṃ ceti tattridhā || 

nityārcanārthaṃ sālambaṃ dhūpādā hanaṃ viduḥ | sarvatra ca 
pratiṣṭhāsu sthaṇḍile'ṣṭadale jale || 

ravau pūjāsu caitāsu nirālambanamagniṣu | uddhṛtya saṃkṣipya 
cārcāyāṃ sthāpanaṃ saṃkramaṃ viduḥ || 

tadanu svāgataṃ te bhagavanniti vijñāpya viśeṣārghyeṇa oṃ hāṃ 
hauṃ śivāya pādyaṃ namaḥ | iti pādayoḥ pādyamācamanaṃ 
svadheti pūrvayāmya saumyapaścimordhvamukheṣu cācamanam 

p. 412) 

arghyaṃ svāheti īśānādipañcaśirassvaṅguṣṭhānāmikā 
madhyamāṅgulyagreṇottāna mukhenārghyaṃ dattvā puṣpaṃ 
vauṣaḍiti dūrvā puṣpākṣatādikaṃ dadyāt || aṃśumati - 

āvāhane tu tanmūrtiṃ? travatī dravahemavat? | tasmādāvāhane 
kāle tvarghyaṃ dadyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 

uktaṃ ca - 

pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ dūrvāpuṣpākṣatādikam | susvāgata 
kriyā pūrvamapi kalpena kalpayet || 

tathā - 

samutkṣipraviśeṣārghyaṃ bhājanaṃ vāmapāṇinā | padbhyāṃ 
niveśayet pādyaṃ dakṣahastena saṃmukhaḥ || 

mukheṣvācamanaṃ dadyādarghyaṃ pañcasu mūrdhasu | 
dūrvāpuṣpākṣatādīni paścāllokopacārataḥ || 

p. 413) 

namaskārasvadhāsvāhāvaṣaḍgātiyujā hṛdā | catuṣṭayamidaṃ 
tatra paripādyānivedayet? || 

tatassahṛdā svāhāntamūlena dhūpadīpācamanārghyāṇi 
dattvāstreṇa piṇḍikādhobhāgaṃ vāmahastena sparśayan 
dakṣahastena navapuṣpaṃ gṛhītvā tena puṣpeṇa pūrvārcitamapohya 
tatpuṣpaṃ ca visṛjya dakṣiṇahastaṃ prakṣālya 
calāsanoktābhyaṅgādisnapanāṅgāni sthirāsanasthitepya? manasā 
vibhāvya śivagāyatrimanteṇa hṛdayena vā jalaṃ 
srāvayitvārghyodabindunā saṃsnāpya mantrapūṣpeṇābhyarcya 
dīpyamānāgniśamanārthaṃ mantrānnaṃ ca nivedayet | 

p. 414) 

tathā aṃśumati - 

calasthirāsanaṃ caiva snānaṃ dvividhameva ca | calāsane tu snānaṃ 
syādabhyaṅgādi viśeṣataḥ || 

sthirāsitebhyamāvāhya? mānasasnānamācaret | tatpīṭhaṃ 
vāmahastena sparśayedastramantrataḥ || 

navapuṣpaṃ tu saṃgrāhya tatpuṣpaṃ tu visarjayet | 
tatpuṣpārcitapuṣpaṃ ca varjayettu vicakṣaṇaḥ || 

dakṣahastādviśeṣeṇa jalopasparśanaṃ kuru | 
mantratoyābhiṣekāntamantrapuṣpaṃ tu dāpayet || 

mantrapuṣpaṃ tato dattvā mantrānnaṃ ca nivedayet | 
agnerdīpanamevaṃ ca śamanārthaṃ nivedyakam || 

mantraṃ rahasyamityuktamadanānna?mucyate | 

p. 415) 

rahasyodyotanaṃ caiva mantrānnamiti kathyate | 

tataśśivamantramanusmṛtya gandhapuṣpairabhyarcya hemakaupīna

manorathamudrāṃ darśayet ||

sarvābharaṇasaṃyuktadāmavṛttādyalaṃkṛtam | 
dāmādhārasamāyuktaṃ citradāmairalaṃkṛtam || 

p. 416)

candanāgarukarpūraṃ kuṅkumādivilepanam | kuryāddakṣiṇahastena

p. 417) 

ajite ca -

aṅguṣṭhamūle parvasthaṃ kṛtvāṅguṣṭhadvayaṃ tataḥ ||

prayoktavyā śivasānnidhyakarmaṇi | 

|| āvaraṇa pūjā ||

p. 418)

ca | athavā kevalairaṅgaiḥ kevalairbrahmabhistu vā || iti ||

p. 420) 

garbhāvaraṇamekaṃ tu pūjayelliṅgamūlagaḥ(taḥ?) |

anantādyā dalāgreṣu vidyeśāṃstatra pūjayet | pīṭhakaṇṭhe 
gaṇeśāṃstu lokeśānpīṭhapādataḥ || 

tadastrāṇi tvadhodeśe sthite brahmaśilopari | 

atra calaliṅgārcane ādhāraśilābhāve'pi brahmaśilāsthānīye 
padmapīṭha evārcayet | evaṃ svavyāptibhedena prabhorāvaraṇaṃ 
niyojayet | tatra śivasya dehātparito vaktrebhyo

yajet || 

p. 421) 

uktaṃ vijaye - 

pañcabrahma tadaṅgāni devasya paritastataḥ | vaktrebhyaśca 
tadaṅgebhyo dīpāddīpavadāharet || 

atra kramaḥ - mūlena śivamabhyarcya śiraḥ pañcake ā?dyaṃ 
īśānamādāya oṃ hoṃ īśānamūrdhnāya namaḥ iti īśāna dale | 

sphaṭikābhaṃ jagaddhetuṃ triṇetraṃ candramaulinam | samyaṃ 
prasannamiśānaṃ triśūlābhayapāṇinam || 

iti dhyātvā pūjayet | vaktrapañcakebhyo vaktramādāya oṃ heṃ 
tatpuruṣavaktrāya namaḥ iti pūrvadale - 

pītāmbaropavītaṃ ca triṇetraṃ kanakaprabham | 
mātulaṅgākṣasūtre ca dadhānaṃ candramaulinam || 

iti dhyātvā yajet | 

p. 422) 

hṛtpradeśāghoramādāya oṃ huṃ aghorahṛdayāya namaḥ iti 
dakṣiṇadale 

tryakṣaṃ maulīnduhṛtsaumyaṃ kuṇḍalālaṃkṛtaṃ tataḥ | 
vabhruśmaśruśirojaṃ ca daṃṣṭrālaṃ vikaṭānanam || 

kapālamālābharaṇaṃ sarpavṛścika bhūṣaṇam | khaṭvāṅgaṃ ca 
kapālaṃ ca kheṭakaṃ pāśameva ca || 

vāmahasta catuṣke tu dakṣahastacauṣṭaye | triśūlaṃ paraśuṃ 
khaḍgaṃ daṇḍaṃ caivārimardanam || 

dhyāyennavāmbudābhāsamaghoraṃ sarvakāmadam | 

iti dhyātvā samarcayet | 

vāmadeva guhyamaṇḍalamādāya oṃ hiṃ vāmadevaguhyāya namaḥ 
ityuttaradale - 

p. 423) 

vāmaṃ raktaṃ suraktābhaṃ mālyavastropavītakam | 
tuṅganāsāruṇoṣṇīṣaṃ khaḍgakheṭakapāṇinam || 

iti dhyātvā vāhyet || 

pādādyaṃ pādādyaṅgāt sadyojātamādāya oṃ haṃ 
sadyojātamūrtaye namaḥ iti paścimadale 

stitasraga(gga?)ndhabhūṣāḍhyaṃ sitoṣṇīṣaṃ sitāmbaram | saumya 
maulīndusaṃpannaṃ bālākāraṃ trilocanam || 

sadyojātaṃ sitaṃ dhyāyedvaradābhayahastakam | 

iti dhyātvāvāhayet || yadvā - 

īśānādikān pañca saṃ(sa?)smmitān saumyalocanān | 
suśvetasvarṇabhṛ(śṛ?)ṅgā sṛgvimalānīśvarānanān || 

jaṭāmakuṭaśobhāḍhyān 

p. 424) 

sarpabālendu bhūṣaṇān | vāmairnāgākṣasūtrau ca nīlābjaṃ 
bījapūrakam || śūlābhayavarāśaktiḥ savyairdhārayate karaiḥ | 
iti vicintya āvāhayet || 

sarvajñatābījātmakaṃ netrebhyo netramādāya oṃ hauṃ netrebhyo 
namaḥ | itīśānasyottarabhāge - 

netraṃ triṇetrasaṃyuktaṃ śvetavarṇaṃ ditgānanam | triśūlaṃ 
cākṣasūtraṃ ca bibhrāṇaṃ dakṣiṇe kare || 

kapālaṃ kuṇḍikāṃ savye jaṭākhaṇḍendumaṇḍitam | 

yadvā - 

aruṇābhaṃ smarennetraṃ dṛkkriyecchātmakaṃ vibho | 

tritattvarūpamāvāhya maṇḍalatritayāśrayaḥ | 
hṛdādyaṅgoktavadanaḥ karadhāryādisaṃyutam || 

p. 425) 

ekāsyaṃ vā triśūlābhihastaṃ yoga śivoktitaḥ | iti 
vibhāvyāvāhayet || sadbhāvajñānātmakahṛdayaṃ hṛdayamādāya 
oṃ hāṃ hṛdayāya namaḥ ityāgneyadale yajet | 
sarveśitātmalakṣaṇaṃ śiraḥ śirodeśādāya oṃ hīṃ śirase 
namaḥ iti īśānadale netrasyottare | ananyādhīnavaśitvarūpāṃ 
śikhāṃ śikhāyā āhṛtya oṃ hūṃ śikhāyai namaḥ iti 
nair-ṛtidale pūjayet | parairabhedya tejorūpaṃ kavacaṃ 
sarvāṅgādādāya oṃ haiṃ kavacāya namaḥ iti vāyavyapatre | 
pratāparūpamastraṃ hastebhyo(bhya?) ādāya pūrvādicaturdikṣu 
dalāgreṣu tatpuruṣādibhyo vāmabhāge oṃ haḥ astrāya namaḥ iti 
yajet | 

p. 426) 

tathā - 

dakṣajānusamīpasthaṃ hṛdayaṃ vahnidiggatam | 
vāmajānusamīpasthamīśāne tu śiro bhavet || 

dakṣāṃsanirgataṃ cūḍāvarmā(rma?) vāmāṃsasannidhau | 
pūrvādidikṣu catvāri bhīmānyastrāṇi vinyaset || 

kālottare - 

hṛdayaṃ candrasaṃkāśaṃ śiraścāmīkaraprabham | śikhā(khāṃ?) 
sindhūravarṇāḍhyāṃ kavacaṃ bhṛṅgabhāskaram || 

netraṃ vidyutpratīkāśamastramagni samaprabham | 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

padmāsanasthānardhendu jaṭāmakuṭadhāriṇam | 
trilocanāṃścaturbāhūn sarvābharaṇabhūṣitān || 

kapālaśūlahastā ca? varadābhayahastakān | 

p. 427) 

caturvaktrānathāstraṃ ca daṃṣṭriṇaṃ piṅgaladyutim | 
kvacitproktāścaturvaktrāścaturhastārastryambakāḥ || 

kvaci? me?kāna nāḥ proktā hṛdādinekavaktrānvā? caturhastā 
hṛdādayan varadāna bhayapradān ? | triśulābjadharānvāpi 
manasaiva vibhāvayet | proktāścaturhastā śūlābhayakaraṃ 
cāstraṃ prajvalacchikhayojvalam | iti dhyāyet | 

tathā sarvajñānottare - 

tadastu sakalaṃ kuryādavyaktaṃ vyaktalakṣaṇam | 
varṇarūpakasaṃyuktaṃ punaḥ pūjāṃ prakalpayet || 

īśānaṃ pañcadhā mūrdhnā vaktraṃ vaktracatuṣṭayet? | 
dīptatejasthitoktānāṃ pañcamaṃ praṇavena tu || 

p. 428) 

hṛdayāṅgairaghoraṃ tu guhe vāmaṃ niyojayet | pādādārabhya 
sarvāṅgaissadyojātaṃ tu vinyaset || 

netraṃ netreṣu vinyasya tato'ṅgāni niyojayet | 

pauṣkare - 

sarvajñatāyā bījaṃ yuktastena nirīkṣaṇaḥ | sadbhāvo hṛdayaṃ 
tasya śirassarvocchrito guṇāḥ || 

śivatvamaparādhīnaṃ yacchikhā soparisthitā | 
svabhāvaguptamanyeṣāṃ samarthamapi gopane || 

tejaḥ kavaca sādhamyāttannāmnā vyapadiśyate | yo'sāvaprasahaḥ 
śambhoḥ pratāpo hetirucyate || 

yathā rājā ca rājapuruṣe nāsthita sthitassanna? śobhate | tathā 
śivacchāpyekākītmayasthito? 

p. 429) 

na rājate | ataḥ parameśvarasya 
śobhārthamāvṛtīrūpeṇāṅgapūjā yuktaiva | eteṣāṃ 
śivavadāvāhana sthāpana sannidhāna sannirodha pādyācamanārghya 
puṣpadānākhyāṣṭa saṃskārapūrvakaṃ yajanaṃ kāryam | 
avakuṇṭhanaṃ amṛtīkaraṇaṃ na kāryam | taduktaṃ kāmike - 

āvahanādīnyetāni svamantrakramaśo'rcayet | 
nāvakuṇṭhanameteṣāmamṛtīkaraṇaṃ tathā || 

saṃskārāṣṭakamevaiṣāṃ pratyekaṃ vihitaṃ budhaiḥ | snapanaṃ 
dīparāptiṃ? ca na pṛthatvāṅga? bhāvataḥ || 

yadvā - 

saṃskārāṣṭakasamuditameva smṛtvārghyaṃ dattvā kriyāpūraṇāya 
īśānādīnāṃ 

p. 430) 

brahmaṇāṃ dhenupadmatriśūlamakarasragākhyā mudrāṅgānāṃ 
sānnidhyārthaṃ namaskāramudrāṃ pradarśayet | taduktaṃ mṛgendre 

tataḥ pradarśayenmudrāṃ pūraṇāya kriyāvidheḥ | tāsūrmmakara? 
śulābja surabhyo brahmaṇāṃ prabhoḥ || 

manoratho tadanyeṣāṃ nati sānnidhyakārikam | 

mṛgendrapaddhatyām - 

spṛṣṭe kaniṣṭhe cānyonyaṃ parasparatalāśrite | anāmike ca 
madhye dve prasāryasyāṃ?grasaṃgate || 

tarjanyau madhyamā pṛṣṭhe lagne prānte ca kuñcite | aṅguṣṭhau 
madhyamā lagnaprānte mudrā sragīritā || 

sammukhe tarjanīyugme kuñcite'nāmikādvaye | 

p. 431) 

jyeṣṭhābhyāṃ pṛṣṭhataḥ kānte srageva makarī bhavet | 

tathā śrījayamudrādhikāre - 

ubhayorhastayoreva jyeṣṭhāṅgulivirahite | pṛṣṭhato'nāmito? 
kārye tarjanyau tānane || 

aṅguṣṭhau susaṃśliṣṭāvāsyākāraprasāritau | 
daṃṣṭrākāre'ntare kārye aṅguṣṭhau tu kaniṣṭhake || 

makarī nātma(ma?) mudraiṣā sarvavighnapradakṣiṇī | 
karayostarjanīyugme nāmayugme ca pṛṣṭhataḥ || 

līne yugye trayośiṣṭe śiṣṭe śūlaṃ prasārite | 
aṅguṣṭhagabhyau saṃsittau pāṇīpadmaṃ ghṛtāṅgulī || 

vidarbhapāṇī saṃyojya tarjanīmadhyame mathaḥ | kaniṣṭhānāmiko 
jyeṣṭha dhenumudrājjalīnnatī || 

p. 432) 

tataḥ śivāya mūlena puṣpaṃ dattvā pūjātāmastva(satva?) 
doṣaparihārāya paramīkaraṇaṃ kṛtvā 
bhogāṅgadvāreṇārcitānāṃ sarveṣāmaṅgānām aṅginā śivena 
sārdhamekṣmatyaṃ(maikamatyaṃ?) śivatejasā vyāptitvaṃ bhāvayet 
|| 

taduktaṃ mohaśūrottare - 

tāmasī ca bhavetpūjā paramīkaraṇaṃ vinā | 

atra jñānaśivena? bhogāṅgabhogaikṣa(kya?)yoḥ phalamuktam | yathā 
- bhogāṅgā padmasthā vividhāyudhārcanamātmanaśśivaguṇa 
prāptyarthamaikyaṃ prabhoraṅgānāṃ punararcakasya śivasaṃyogāya 
śuddhātmana iti || 

p. 433) 

tato dvitīyāvaraṇe anantādīnaṣṭau pūrvādidikṣu saṃsthitān 
krameṇa pūjayet | tatra - 

vidyeśvarāścaturvaktrā haravaddaśabāhavaḥ | 
khaṇḍendumaulayassaumya padmasthā vividhāyudhāḥ || 

khaḍgakheṭadhanurbāṇāḥ? kamaṇḍalvatryakṣasūtriṇaḥ | 
varābhayakaropetāḥ? śūlapaṅkajapāṇayaḥ || 

icchārūpadharāssarve bhuktimuktiphalapradāḥ | hemāgnī(gni?) 
nīlabhṛṅgenduhimaraktāsitāḥ kramāt || 

yadvā - 

varadābhayahastāśca jñānamudrāakṣamālinaḥ | 
ekavaktrāstriṇetrāśca śaiva jñānāvatārakāḥ | 

tryakṣāḥ śaktiśūlāḍhyā ekāsyā vā jaṭānvitāḥ | 

p. 434) 

ityananta sūkṣmaśivottamaikanetraikarudra trīmūrti śrīkhaṇṭha 
śikhaṇḍīnāmakānaṣṭau vidyeśvarāndhyātvā oṃ hāṃ anantāya 
namaḥ iti pūrvavaktre (patre?) yajet | oṃ hāṃ sūkṣmāya namaḥ 
iti dakṣiṇa patre | oṃ hāṃ śivottamāya namaḥ iti varuṇapatre | 
oṃ hāṃ ekanetrāya namaḥ ityuttarapatre | oṃ hāṃ ekarudrāya 
namaḥ ityaiśānapatre | oṃ hāṃ trimūrtaye namaḥ ityāgneyapatre | 
oṃ hāṃ śrīkaṇṭhāya namaḥ nair-ṛtapatre | oṃ hāṃ 
śikhaṇḍine namaḥ iti vāyavyapatre | yajet | uktam suprabhede - 

anantaṃ pūrvataḥ pūjyaṃ trimūrtiṃ vahnigocare | sūkṣmaṃ tu 
dakṣiṇe bhāge śrīkaṇṭhaṃ nair-ṛte tathā || 

p. 435) 

śivottamaṃ tu vāruṇyāṃ śikhaṇḍiṃ vāyugocare | ekanetraṃ 
tathā saumye ekarudraṃ tathaiśake || 

kāmike'pyevamuktam | 

yadvā pūrvādīśānāntaṃ yajet | taduktam - 

sūkṣme - 

vidyeśāvaraṇaṃ proktaṃ indrādīśāntamarcayet tataḥ 
pīṭhakaṇṭhe pūrvādidikṣu krameṇa gaṇeśvarānyajet | tatra 
pūrve - 

rakto nandī caturbāhurekavaktrastridṛgjaṭī | 
sākṣasūtrestra(trastri?) śūlī ca varābhayakarānvitaḥ || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ nandine namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | 

āgneye triṇetraḥ pīnakṛṣṇāṅgo babhruśmaśruśiroruhaḥ | 
muṇḍakhaḍgau kheṭaśūle mahākālo vahankaraiḥ || 

p. 436) 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ mahākālāya namaḥ yāmye - 

nirmāṃsavigraho bhṛṅgīśveto daṇḍākṣasūtradhṛt | nityaṃ 
triṇetrassaśikho devālokanatatparaḥ || 

oṃ hāṃ bhṛṅgiṇe namaḥ ityarcayet || 

nair-ṛte - 

triṇetro vāmanākāro gajavaktro mahodaraḥ | pāśāṅkuśau 
svadantaṃ ca pakvamāmraphalaṃ vahan || 

śuṇḍāvāmakarābhyāṃ ca raktāṅgo vighnanāyakaḥ | iti dhyātvā 
oṃ hāṃ gaṇapataye namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

vāruṇe - 

vṛṣo vṛṣākṛtistryakṣo rudraikagatamānasaḥ | 
sākṣāddharmasvarūpaśca śuddhasphaṭikanirmalaḥ || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ viṣabhāya namaḥ ityabhyarcya(rca?)yet | 

p. 437) 

vāyavye - 

skando nijāṣṭabāhubhyāṃ vyāghracarmāmbaro jaṭī | muṇḍī vā 
ṣaṇmukhaṃśyāmamaśoṣaṇḍa? piṅgalacchaviḥ || 

catubhujo bālaveṣa skandaḥ kanakasuprabhā | śaktikukkuṭahastiśca 
varābhayasamanvitaḥ || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ skandāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

uttare - 

sihmopari sthitāṃ devīṃ śūlākī?darśakaradvayām | 
karaṇḍamakuṭopetāṃ muktādibhiralaṃkṛtām || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ devyai namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | 

aiśānyām - 

caṇḍeśvaraścaturbāhuścaturvaktrastrilocanaḥ | 
bibhratkamaṇḍaluṃ śūlamakṣasūtraṃ paraśvatham || 

mahoragopavītāḍhyastannirvartitakaṅkaṇaḥ | dhyeyo 
navāmbudhābhāsasamayādiniyāmakaḥ || 

p. 438) 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ caṇḍāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | vimānasthā 
gaṇāsteṣāṃ mūṣikastho gajānanaḥ || 

mayūravāhanaḥ skando vṛṣabhaḥ kevalaḥ sthitaḥ | 

nanu gaṇeśvarastṛtīye tu kauberāditaḥ kramāt | ityuktatvāttatra 
pūrvādikramo virudhyata iti cet | na | tantrabhedātkriyābhedena 
virodhābhāvāt || supradhede - 

vidyeśānāṃ gaṇeśānāṃ kālakaṇṭhīṃ tu darśayet | 

tataḥ pīṭhapāde pūrvādi dikṣu krameṇa indrādīnyajet | 

airāvatagajārūḍhaṃ svarṇavarṇaṃ kirīṭinam | sahasranayanaṃ 
śakraṃ vajrapāṇiṃ vibhāvayet || 

p. 439) 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ indrāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | 

saptārcitaṃ ca bibhrāṇaṃ akṣamālā kamaṇḍalu | 
jvālāmālākulaṃ raktaṃ śaktihastañci?gāsanam || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ agnaye namaḥ ityarcayet | 

kṛtāntaṃ mahiṣārūḍhaṃ daṇḍahastaṃ bhayānakam | 
kālapārādharaṃ kālaṃ dhyāyeddakṣiṇadikpatim || 

dhvātvā oṃ hāṃ yamāya namaḥ ityarcayet | raktanetraṃ 
śavārūḍhaṃ nīlotpaladalaprabham || 

kṛpāṇapāṇimāstraughaṃ pibantaṃ rākṣaseśvaram | 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ nir-ṛtaye namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

p. 440) 

nāgapāśadharaṃ hṛṣṭaṃ raktausa(gha?)dyutivigraham | 
śāśāṅkadhavalaṃ dhyāyedvarunaṃ makarāsanam || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ varuṇāyanamaḥ ityarbhyarcayet | āpīnaṃ 
haritacchāyaṃ viloladhvajadhāriṇam || 

prāṇabhūtaṃ ca bhūtānāṃ hariṇassaṃ(sthaṃ?) samīraṇam | iti 
dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ vāyave namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

kuberaṃ manujāsīnaṃ sa?garvaṃ kharvavigraham | svārṇacchāyaṃ 
gadāhastamuttarāśāpatiṃ smaret || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ kuberāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

īśānaṃ vṛṣabhārūḍhaṃ triśūlaṃ vyāladhāriṇam | 
śaraccandrāvadātaṃ ca candramauliṃ trilocanam || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ īśānāya namaḥ ityabhyarca 

p. 441) 

aiśānasya dakṣiṇe - 

pīnaṃ pītaṃ caturbāhuṃ brahmāṇaṃ caturānanam | haṃsāvāraṃ 
ca bibhrāṇaṃ dṛṣṭākṣa srakkumaṣṭhabhuḥ ? || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ brahmaṇe namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | 

nir-ṛtiruttare - 

anantaṃ puṇḍarīkākṣaṃ pa(pha)ṇādaśaśatairyutam | 
vidyudābhapratīkāśaṃ kūrmārūḍhaṃ prapūjayet || 

oṃ hāṃ viṣṇave namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | 

kālottare lokāvaraṇaṃ taccaturthaṃ parikīrtitam || 

pañcamo(maṃ?) lokapālāsraṃ pūjayettu samāhitaḥ | vajraṃ pūrve 
tu saṃpūjya śaktimagniyagocare || 

p. 442) 

yāmyāyāṃ tu gadāmiṣṭvā triśūlaṃ śāṅkare diśi | 
padmāstraṃ pūjayedūrdhvamaiśānyāṃdiśimāśritam || 

adhastāccakramātpūjya nair-ṛtāndīśamāśritam | 

kāmike - 

jāpe śaktigade jñeye vakrapadme napuṃsake | śeṣāḥ pumāṃsī 
vijñeyāsvā(stvā?)yudhānāṃ svarūpataḥ | 

tataḥ brahmaśilāsthānīyapadmapīṭhopari pūrvādīśāntadikṣu - 

vajraṃ tu puruṣaṃ sthūlaṃ dṛḍhaṃ karkaśavigraham | bālenaṃ 
vajramūrdhāna dīptaṃ prākvamāvṛttau? || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ vajrāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | 

p. 443) 

śaktiṃ ya yoṣitā(dā?)kārāmāgneyyāṃ diśi lohitām | 
bibhrāṇaṃ śirasā śaktiṃ trikoṇasthāmupāśrayet || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ śaktaye namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | daṇḍaṃ ca 
dakṣiṇe bhāge kṛṣṇaṃ puruṣāvigraham || 

daṇḍākāraśiroyuktaṃ vande lohitalocanam | iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ 
daṇḍāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

nair-ṛtyāṃ puruṣākāraṃ khaḍgaṃ śyāmaprabhānvitam | 
khaḍgālaṃkāramūrdhānamityaṃ saṃkrāntalocanam || 

iti dhyātvā oṃ gāṃ(hāṃ?) khaḍgāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet || 

nābhyadhobhujakā(gā?)kāraṃ na(ta?)dūrdhvaṃ puruṣākṛtim | 
pāśaṃ saptaphaṇopetaṃ mūrdhānaṃ paścime bhave || 

p. 444) 

iti dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ pāśāya namaḥ ityabhyarcayet | papadye 
vāyudigbhāge dhvajamūrdhni dhvajānvitam || 

pītaṃ ca puruṣākāraṃ vyāvṛtāsyaṃ mahābhujam | iti dhyātvā

viṣṇave namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ rudrāya namaḥ 

p. 469) 

oṃ hāṃ anantāya namaḥ ityabhyarcya agnyarbha karakṣāṃ 
vijñāpayet | 

kāmike - 

toyabinduṃ tridhā dattvā samācchādya sadindhanaiḥ | dadyāt 
śaucācamanānimastrāṇyastreṇa cārbhakam || 

sadyādinā tu vai dadyānmūlenāhutipañcakam | śivāgnistvamiti 
brūyānmūlamantrapurassaram || 

pitrorvisarjanaṃ kuryātsūtaka?sya nivṛttaye | kuṇḍamarghyāmbunā 
prokṣya prāgagramudagagrakān || 

darbhānāstīrya kuṇḍasya samantādastramantrataḥ | 

mṛgendre - 

triṃśaddarbhadale(lai?) ve(rve?)ṇī grathitā bāhusammitaiḥ | 
brahmagranthiyutā cāgre vittaṃ sā viṣṭaraṃ śubham || 

p. 470) 

yā viṇīkeśapāśākṛt sa viṣṭarantutyaccate? | 

mataṅge - 

prāguktavidhinā mantrī saumyāgrā pūrvato nyaset | pūrvāgrāṃ 
dakṣiṇe bhāge paścime cāpyudaṅmukhāt || 

prāgagrānuttare bhāge pūrvataścāpi viṣṭaram | 

mṛgendre - 

yājñīyadrumajā bāhumātrāḥ paridhayaḥ smṛtāḥ | kaniṣṭhikā 
sthūlamūlā haritā vraṇavarjitāḥ || 

etānpūrvavatparīdhāya lokapālānyajet | 

kāmike - 

indrādīnlokapālāṃśca pūjayetparidhiṣviti | viṣṭareṣu 
krameṇaiva brahmāṇaṃ rudrameva ca || 

p. 471) 

viṣṇuvī(mī?)śvaramabhyarcya prāgādiṣu caturṣvapi | tato 
brahmādayo devā indrādyā lokapālakāḥ || 

nivārya vighnasaṃghātaṃ bālakaṃ bā(pā?)layiṣyatha | 
śaivāgniṃ kathyate vipra śṛṇu tasminsamāhitāḥ || 

pañcavaktrayutaṃ raktaṃ saptajihvāvirājitam | daśahastaṃ 
triṇetraṃ ca sarvābharaṇabhūṣitam || 

raktavastraparīdhānaṃ paṅkajopari saṃsthitam | 
baddhapadmāsanāsīnaṃ daśāyudhasamanvitam || 

kanakā bahurūpā ca atiraktau tataḥ param | suprabhā caiva kṛṣṇā 
ca raktā cānyā hiraṇmayī || 

ūrdhvavaktre sthitāstisraśśeṣāḥ prāgāditaḥ kramāt ityagniṃ 
dhyātvā oṃ hāṃ agnaye namaḥ 

p. 472) 

iti saṃpūjya oṃ hāṃ hrūṃ agnaye svāheti tilāhutipañcakaṃ 
hu(ju?)yāt | 

kāmike - 

agnetvamaiśvaraṃ tejaḥ pāvanaṃ paramaṃ yataḥ | tasmāddvitīya 
hṛtpadme saṃsthāpya saṃtarpayāmyaham || 

iti kuṇḍapramāṇamagnerīpsitamukhaṃ vibhāvya agnihṛtpadme 
ādhāraśaktyādi śaktyantaṃ sadeśaṃ śivaṃ nityavatpavitrāntaṃ 
saṃpūjayet | 

kāmike - 

yathābhilaṣitaṃ vaktraṃ kuṇḍamānaṃ prakalpayet | 

ajite - 

tataśśivāsañcognau padmamekaṃ prakalpayet | 

p. 473) 

padmāsanāyetyuccārya namaskārasamanvitam | tatra liṅge yathā 
devaḥ sadāśiva udīritaḥ || 

tata dhyātvātra taṃ devaṃ pañcabrahmāṅgasaṃyutam | 
āvāhanādikaṃ sarvaṃ kṛtvā homaṃ samācaret || 

tataśśivavaktrāgnivaktrayossandhānaṃ kṛtvā svāhāntamūlena 
dhṛtādināśataṃ tadardhaṃ vā yathāśakti hutvā brahmāṅgānāṃ 
daśāṃśato hutvā oṃ hauṃ śivāya vauṣaḍiti ūrdhvavaktre 
pūrṇāṃ dattvā naivedyāya caruṃ svāhāntamūlenānnagrāsatrayaṃ 
brahmāṅgānāmekaikaṃ hutvā pūrṇāṃ dattvā ācamanaṃ 
candanatāmbūlādīni dattvā 

p. 474) 

bhasmā pādyāṣṭapuṣpikayā saṃpūjayet | 

kāmike - 

śatamardhaṃ tadardhaṃ vā viṃsatirdaśa eva vā | 
śivāddaśāṃśato'ṅgāni tarpayecca dvijottamāḥ || 

ātmārthe vā parārthe vā pratyahaṃ homamācaret | ekakālaṃ 
dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ vā samācaret || 

vātule - 

ekakālaṃ dvikālaṃ vā trikālaṃ homamācaret | trikālamuttamaṃ 
proktaṃ dvikālaṃ madhyamaṃ bhavet || 

adhamaṃ caikakālaṃ vā kārayettu śikhidvija | 
samidājyacarūllāṁjān sarṣapāṃśca yavāṃstilān || 

uttamaṃ tviti vikhyātaṃ madhyamaṃ ca tataśśṛṇu | 

p. 475) 

samidājyañcaruñcaiva sarṣapaṃ madhyamaṃ viduḥ | 
samidājyacaruñcaiva kanyasaṃ paripatyate || 

ghṛtena payasā vātha tilairvā ghṛtamiśritaiḥ | yavairvā 
vrīhibhirvāpi guggulena ghṛtena vā || 

nityahomaḥ prakartavyo yathāvittānusārataḥ | ājyaṃ vai 
karṣamātraṃ tu hotavyaṃ karmasiddhaye || 

tilā mṛśyā tu juhuyānvādīn? kambumudrayā | pāsu vyañjanādīni 
patākena tu homayet || 

kāmike - 

ājyābhāve tilairhomaḥ kartavyaḥ kṛṣṇavarṇakaiḥ | 
brīhibhirvāsminbhirvā? dadhnā vāpi yavaistu vā || 

śrīphalairbilvapatrairvā puṣpairvā yājñikaiśśubhaiḥ | 

p. 476) 

anyairhomo vidhātavyaśśraddhāvittānusārataḥ | 

ajite - 

daśakaṃ bāhutīnāṃ ca mūlena juhuyātkramāt || 
pañcabrahmaśivāṅgaiśca pratyekaṃ homamācaret | 

kiraṇe - 

svāhākāraṃ ca home syāgraṃ syādvauṣaḍantikā || 

kālottare - 

prāsādena tu mantreṇa pūrṇāhutimathācaret | 

catuśśatikālottare - 

ūrdhvavaktre mahāsena tasminpūrṇāhuti smṛtāḥ | 

kāmike - 

haviṣyaṃ grāsamātreṇa tāmbūlaṃ vinivedayet | 
pradakṣiṇyanamaskāraṃ kṛtvā vā bhaktitastataḥ || 

parāṅmukhārghyaṃ dattvā vahnisthaṃ śivamutthāpya svamūrtau 
saṃyojya vidhivannāḍīsandhānena liṅge śive nirapekṣaiva? yojayet 
| 

p. 477) 

sarvajñānottare - 

visarjayettato devaṃ punaḥ pūjāvidhāntaḥ | 

atha viprādistra?yāṇāṃ vyāhṛticatuṣṭayaṃ juhuyāt | 
caturthaścenmūlena homayet | paścādbrahmādibhyo baliṃ dattvā 
vahni namaskṛtya 

saptajihvānvitaṃ raktaṃ pañcāsyaṃ prajvalacchikham | 
vāgīśīgarbhasaṃbhūtaṃ śivāgniṃśivadaṃbhaje || 

iti smṛtvā hutaṃ hutvoparametyuktvā oṃ hāṃ hrūṃ śivāgnaye 
namaḥ iti niṣṭhurayā vahniṃ svahṛdi liṅge vā yojayet | 

p. 478) 

tadanu brahmādīn lokapālānapi krameṇa visṛjet | yadvā kuṇḍa 
vānyatrāgnimuddhṛtya navabhāṇḍe vā jagramagniṃ saṃrakṣayet | 
tadagnirnivāṇaścetprāyaścittaṃ kuryāt | 

kāmike - 

śivaṃ vyāhṛtibhiḥ paścātsantarpya śivapāvakam | rodhayitvā tu 
tatraiva rakṣayetprativāsaram || 

sūkṣme - 

niṣṭhureṇāgnimādāya mūlaliṅge samarpayet | viṣṭaraṃ ca 
samādāya granthiṃ muktavā visarjayet || 

paristaraṇamānīya ājyenaiva tu secayet | ekadarbhaṃ samadāya 
śeṣamagrādikaṃ dahet || 

p. 479) 

tadekamagnau nikṣipya tadbhasma tilakaṃ kuru | tadbhasma dhāraṇaṃ 
prāsāṃ māyuḥ śrīkīrtivardhanam || 

vijayaṃ rogagāśārthaṃ sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradam | 

aṃśumatī(ti?) 

tadbhasma tu lalāṭe tu netramantreṇa vinyaset | 

ṣaṭsahasre - 

tatoddhṛtya śivāgniṃ tu sthāpyānyatra tu rakṣayet | nirodhanaṃ 
tatraiva kāryaṃ niṣṭhuramudrayā || 

kuṇḍasthamanalaṃ śeṣaṃ namaskṛtya visarjayet | 

vijayottare - 

vahniṃ saṃrakṣayennityam caturvyāhṛtihomataḥ | yadvā 
visarjayedvahniṃ punaḥ saṃskāra yojayet || 

p. 480) 

kuṇḍasaṃskāre bhasmāpayana pūrvakam | 

kāmike - 

yadvā visarjayetkuṇḍe vahnimanyatra rakṣayet | rakṣite'gnau tu 
nirvāṇe japedghoramupoṣitaḥ || 

saśive'pi hi nirvāṇe trirātraṃ samupoṣitaḥ | japedvā 
juhuyādvātha bahurūpaṃ praśāntaye || 

svastidakṣiṇadigbhāge balyarthaṃ ho(go?)mayena caturaśramaṇḍalaṃ 
kṛtvā maṇḍalaprānte pūrvādyuttarāntadikṣu oṃ hāṃ rudrebhyo 
namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ mātṛbhyo namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ gaṇebhyo namaḥ | oṃ 
hāṃ yakṣebhyo namaḥ | īśādikoṇeṣu oṃ hāṃ grahebhyo namaḥ | 
oṃ hāṃ asurebhyo namaḥ | 

p. 481) 

oṃ hāṃ rākṣasebhyo namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ nāgebhyo namaḥ || 
maṇḍalamadhye īśānāgneye nair-ṛteṣu oṃ hāṃ nakṣatrebhyo 
namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ rāśibhyo namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ viśvebhyo namaḥ | oṃ 
hāṃ devebhyo namaḥ | vāyuvaruṇayormadhye oṃ hāṃ kṣetrapālāya 
namaḥ | ityabhyarcyārghyaṃ dattvā jalamiśrānnena svāhāntena 
tattanmantreṇa rudrādibhyo baliṃ pradāyācamanaṃ dadyāt tathā - 

kāmike - 

tatraiva vā baliṃ dadyātkṛtvā maṇḍalakaṃ bhuvi | rudrebhyaḥ 
pūrvato dadyānmātṛbhyo dakṣiṇe tathā || 

gaṇebhyaḥ paścime bhāge yakṣebhyaścottaraṃ balim | 

p. 482) 

grahebhyaśca tathaiśānyāmasurebhyo'gnigocare | pālaśyāṃ 
palabhakṣebhyo? nāgebhyo vāyugocare || 

nakṣatrarāśivargebhyastathā viśvagaṇāya ca | maṇḍalābhyantare 
deśe baliṃ tebhyaḥ prakāmataḥ || 

vāyuvaruṇadigbhāge kṣetrapālabaliṃ kṣipet | 
ātmārthe'pyevaka(mu?)ddiṣṭaṃ gṛhādau devasadmani || 

indrādiparivārāṇāṃ baliṃ dadyāt pṛthak pṛthak | 

iti vacanadindrādi bali mantranaviddaddhyāt | tathā aṃśumati 

rudrebhyaḥ pūrvato dadyād mātṛbhyo dakṣiṇe tathā | 
padadyātpaścime bhāge rakṣebhyaścottarebalim || 

p. 483) 

grahebhyaścāsurebhyaśca rākṣasebhyastathaiva ca | nāgebhyaśca 
baliṃ dadyādāgneyādi vicakṣaṇaḥ || 

maṇḍalastu tanmadhye kṣetrapālabaliṃ dadet | sarvamantraṃ 
samūhyātha ekavāraṃ baliṃ dadet | 

tathā pūrvādidigvāsibhyo 
digīśabhūtamātṛgaṇarudrakṣetrapālādibhyo namaḥ iti dadyāt | 
athavā naivedyānte parārthavat rudrādibhyo(bhyasta?) tadagre 
maṇḍale baliṃ dattvā tadagnikāryaṃ kuryāt || 

tathā aṃśumati - 

tadagre balidānaṃ ca maṇḍalaṃ caturaśrakam | pādyācamanaṃ 
cārghyaṃ gandhapuṣpaiḥ samarcayet || 

p. 484) 

balivinavasanāne? tu agnikāryaṃ samācaret | 

maṇṭapādvāhyāṅkaṇe oṃ hāṃ vāyasādidyaṃ samayavedibhya 
svāhetyabhyarcya baliṃ dattvā oṃ hāṃ asyādibhya svāhā oṃ hāṃ 
sarvebhyo gṛhavāstu devebhyo svāheti pṛthak pṛthak baliṃ dattvā 
hṛdayena sarvān visṛjet | tathā siddhāntaśekhare - 

tānvisṛjya vinirgatya bahirgomayamaṇdale | 
śvabhūtapatitapretakādibhyo baliṃ kṣipet || 

yadi vā saṃkṣepeṇānena mantreṇa baliṃ dadyāt | mṛgendre - 

oṃ - ye rudrā raudrakarmāṇo raudrasthānanivāsinaḥ | 
saumyāścaiva tu ye kecitsaumyasthāna nivāsinaḥ || 

p. 485) 

mātaro rudrarupāśca gaṇānāmadhipāśca ye | vighnabhūtāstathā 
cānye digvidikṣu samāśritāḥ || 

sarve suprītamanasaḥ pratigṛhṇantvimāṃ balim | siddhiṃ 
juṣi(ṣa?)ntu naḥ kṣipraṃ bhavebhyaḥ pāntu māṃ sadā || 

itthaṃ baliṃ dattvā mantrān svahṛdi vinyaset | tadanu 
kuṇḍagatadravyajātaṃ bhasmāvaśeṣaṃ vidhāya taduddhṛtya 
kuṇḍaṃ sabāhyābhyantaraṃ gomayavāriṇā saṃlipya punarapi 
homakāle kuṇḍasaṃskāraṃ vidhāya tatra sthapitamagniṃ prajvālya 
tasmin śivaṃ pūrvavat saṃpūjya santarpayet | ityevaṃ pratyahaṃ 
vidadhīta || 

p. 486) 

taduktam - 

patyahaṃ vihitaṃ karma kuṇḍasyollekhanādikam | jāgrāpaye? tato 
nāgnirnitya naimittikakarmaṇi || 

atra dīkṣāpratiṣṭhena vihitanaimittikena kartavya iti | saṃskṛte 
pratyahaṃ kuṇḍe nyasetprāk saṃskṛtānalam | iti | tathāgne 
śivāgne dhāraṇaṃ puṇyaṃ yāvajjīvaṃ śivājñayā | 
kuṇḍāduddhṛtya yatnena sthāpayedbhājane dṛḍhe || 

tuṣaiśśuṣkaipilavaissāndra? piṇḍenāgniṃ surakṣayet || 

saṃskṛtya pūrvavatkuṇḍaṃ pratyahaṃ ca pradakṣiṇam | tatra 
nikṣipya vahniṃ tu tatra santarpayecchivam || 

karmānte pūrvavadrakṣā kāryā yatnena dīkṣitaiḥ | 

p. 487) 

evaṃ surakṣitaṃ kṛtvā prāṇāntaṃ yaḥ pratarpayet | tasya 
puṇyaphalaṃ vaktuṃ na kenāpīha śakyate | tenaiva vahninā kāryaṃ 
tasyai vānteṣṭikarmaṇā || 

navetareṣu tatkārye na prākṛtajantuṣu | visarjanaṃ yadā 
vahnerdeśadausthyātkadācana || 

jñānāgnau hṛdaye vindau yojayitvā visarjayet | 
tadāvasthānabījaṃ tu prāyaścittaṃ samācaret || 

svasthe citte punaḥ kuryādyathāpūrvamudāhṛtam | 

tataḥ stotra vandanādikaṃ kṛtvā sakalīkṛtya 
sāmānyārghyahastaḥ śivāntikaṃ gatvā svāsane samupaviśya 
pūjāhomādikāryādyaissukṛtaṃ yanmayā kṛtam | 

p. 488) 

tatpada(tphalaṃ?) parameśāna mayā tubhyaṃ samarpitam | tadgṛhāṇa 
svayaṃ prītyā nedaṃ bhogāya me'stubhoḥ || 

karmaṇā manasā vācā tvaṅgatiṇme? maheśvara | 

ityuktvā svāhāntamūlena pūjāhomādikaṃ puṇyaphalaṃ devasya 
varadahaste arghyajalena saha udbhavamudrayā bhūmiṣṭhajānuji? 
mumukṣurnivedayet | bubhukṣuścet phalaṃ me'stu deveśa 
bhuktimuktayarthasādhanam | iti paṭhitvā samarpayet | 

aṃśumati - 

athavānyaprakāreṇa japameva samācaret | 
homopakaraṇavaikalyādaśakto vā tadanyathā || 

p. 489) 

homasaṃkhyāni mantrāṇi japtvā daśaguṇaṃ tathā | tajjapaṃ 
varadahaste tu tatpuṣpaṃ tu samarpayet || 

arghyaṃ dattvā viśeṣeṇa śivamantramanusmaran | 

tadanu dhūpadīpādīpanirīkṣaṇādibhiḥ saṃskṛtya 
dhūpadīpamudrāṃ ca pradarśya ghaṇṭāmudrāṃ dattvā 
ghaṇṭāmantreṇa nādayantaṃ śivāya dattvā 
ekatripañcasaptanavāryuktaśivaṃ nirīkṣaṇādisaṃskṛta 
dhūpadīpamudrāṃ pradarśayitvā kṣa? ātmane vānda 
manaḥkāyajanitamokṣārthaṃ śivasaṃyogārthaṃ ca 
dūrvākṣatakuśasahita puṣpamañjalau gṛhītvā oṃ hāṃ hauṃ 
ātmā- 

p. 490) 

dhipataye śivāya namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ hauṃ vidyātattvādhipataye 
śivāya namaḥ | oṃ hāṃ hauṃ śivatattvādhipataye śivāya namaḥ 
| 

itthaṃ nityapavitraṃ mumukṣurdadyāt || 
bubhukṣuścecchivatattvādimantramuccārya dadyāt | athavā oṃ hāṃ 
hauṃ sarvatattvādhipataye śivāya namaḥ iti pavitraṃ dadyāt | 

taduktam - 

pavitramupadībhūtaṃ śirasasyādhiropayet | pratyahaṃ 
vāṅmanaḥkāyavyāpārajanitādalam || 

patanāttrāyate yasmāttadetat pavitrakam | 

kāraṇe - 

p. 491) 

dūrvākuśākṣatopetairbalipatraissamanvitam | dadyātpavitraṃ 
nityaṃ mūlamantrasamāyutam || 

ātmatattvādhipataye śivāya nama ityapi | tadvaddadyācchivākhyaṃ 
ca mūrtikāmaḥ smaran paṭhet || 

pavitra tadha mūrdhni dadyādbhogī saśivāitaḥ | 

atra bhogīti śabne(bdena?) dīkṣitaḥ | 

taduktaṃ śrīmadraurave - 

dīkṣābhūtā gaṇapatigurormaṇḍala jammavantassiddhā 
mantraisturuṇadinakṛta mañjilāt bhāsideha rīkṣayā | 

bhuktvā bhogān suciramamṛtastrīnikāyairupetāḥ srastotkarṇā 
śivapadaparaiśvarya pādo bhavati? | 

p. 492) 

pavitrabhūtasya pavitradānaṃ tāpatrayaghnaṃ sakalārthasirddhaiḥ | 
ityācāryairapyuktam | etadakaraṇe pūjāyā rājasatvaṃ bhavati | 
tathā mohaśūrottare - 

pavitreṇa vinā pūjā rājasīti prakīrtitā | iti || 

navapañcatrayekānyatare saṃkhyakarpūre vartidīpayuktamārātrikaṃ 
tripādyāṃ nyasya catussatussaṃskāraṃ dhūpadīpau ca dattvā 
dhenumudrāṃ pradarśya samādāya svāhānta hṛdayena 
pratyekamayomayyo ca krameṇa tristriḥ paribhrāmya 
avatāryācamanārghyau dattvā karpūramiśraṃ 

p. 493) 

bhasma mṛgamudrayā saṃgṛhya liṅgasyoparitriḥ paribhrāmyārātrika 
pātre kṣiptvā devasya devyā berāṇāṃ vā 
yathāyogyamaṅguṣṭhānāmikābhyāṃ vā bhasma dhārayet || tathā - 

nirdhūmajvaladaṅgāra pūrṇapātrodare dṛśam | dahyamānena 
dhūpena dīpayettadanantaram || 

pañcasaptanaveti varttibhisṛta dīpikām | 

kālottare - 

kaniṣṭhāṅguṣṭhasaṃkoce śeṣāścaiva prasārayet | dhūpamudrā 
bhavedeṣā dīpamudrāṃ tataḥ śṛṇu || 

kaniṣṭhānāmikāmadhye aṅguṣṭhena tu pīḍayet | 
tarjanīmūrdhvatā? kṛtvā dīpamudrāṃ pradarśayet || 

p. 494) 

ekanādaṃ ca mokṣārthaṃ viraktānāṃ praśasyate | 
ghaṇṭātāḍanamevaṃ syādvinādaṃ bhogamokṣadam || 

jānunā bherayo nādaṃ kartavyaṃ tu na kārayet | nābherūrdhvaṃ tu 
kartavya mahāghaṇṭāpratāḍanam || 

uttamaṃ snigdhaghoṣaṃ ca madhya madhyamaṃ tathā | adhamaṃ 
ghargharāghoṣamityete śabdalakṣaṇam | 

ajite - 

ārātrikamatho vakṣye sarvapāpapraśāntaye | navadīpayutaṃ 
śreṣṭhaṃ pañcadīpaṃ tu madhyamam || 

kanyasaṃ tryekadīpena yatnaṃ pātrakramādbhavet | uddhṛtyopari 
devasya triḥ paribhrāmayettadā || 

tantrasāre - 

p. 495) 

pādādimūrdhaparyantaṃ tristri bhrāmya tato nyaset | tato bhasma tu 
saṃhṛtya karpūrādi sugandhitam || 

gṛhītvā mudrayā dṛśyā bhrāmya liṅgopari tridhā | 
prakṣipeddīpapātre tu tato liṅgasya mūrdhani | astreṇa 
vinyasedīṣaddakṣiṇena kareṇa tu || 

kāmike - 

śvitaṃ? pūtaṃ sugandhiṃ ca nirvālukamanantaram | 
aṅguṣṭhānāmikānāṃ tu tilakaṃ pañcavaktrake || 

[dīpamekaikadīpe tu prokṣayettu śivāmbhasā | badhvā tu surabhīṃ 
paścāddīpasyopari darśayet ||] 

devī vyaktasvarūpā cet tilakaṃ ca purogale | tato lalāṭamūlena 
hṛdayenāthavā nayet || 

p.496) 

bere lalāṭe hṛdbāhvoḥ pradeśe tilakaṃ nayet | 

tantrasāre - 

phālalocanamadhye tu rakṣāyāṃ kiṃ prayojanam | 
lalāṭanayanasyordhve rakṣāṃ? devasya dāpayet || 

tatoranantarāntara? jalopasparśanaṃ kṛtvā darpaṇachatra 
cāmaravyañjana tālavṛntāni pañcabrahmabhiḥ krameṇa dattvā 
vedapurāṇa pañcāṅgaśravaṇastotra śuddhanṛttarahitagītavādya 
dhvanibhiḥ santoṣya bhogāṅgasahitaṃ tuṣṭaṃ śivaṃ dhyātvā 
mūlena puṣpāñjaliṃ? tridhā dattvā mahāmudrāṃ pradarśayet || 

uktam aṃśumati - 

p. 497) 

jalopasparśanaṃ kṛtvā darpaṇaṃ darśayettataḥ | chatracāmarakaṃ 
caiva vyañja(ja?)naṃ tālavṛntakam || 

pañcabrahmamanu smṛtya yojayettu krameṇa tu | vedaiḥ purāṇamevaṃ 
ca pañcāṅga śravaṇaṃ tataḥ || 

stotranṛttaṃ ca gītaṃ ca vādyadhvani samāyutam | 

śivadharme - 

śivasya purato dadyāddarpaṇa svacchanirmitam | kalpāyutasahasraṃ tu 
śivaloke mahīyate || 

śaraccandrāṃśuvimalaṃ muktādāmopaśobhitam | 
maṇidaṇḍamayachatraṃ dadyādvā kāñcanāmayam || 

chatreṇa sapavitreṇa dhāryamāṇena śobhitam || 

vimānaśatasaṃbhogaiḥ sthiraṃ śivapure vaset | 

p. 498) 

tantrasāre - 

śivāya cāmaraṃ dadyānmaṇidaṇḍavibhūṣitam | 
hemaraupyādidaṇḍaṃ vā tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu | 
cāmarāsaktahastāgra divyaśrīparivāritaḥ || 

mahābhogavimānasthaḥ sthiraṃ śivapure vaset | 

suprabhede - 

mayūravyañja(ja?)naṃ kuryāccamaryāścāmaraṃ tathā | 
hemaratnamayaṃ daṇḍaṃ raupyaṃ tāmramayaṃ tu vā || 

dantairvā dārubhirvāpi daṇḍaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ | 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

mūlamantreṇa kartavyaṃ puṣpaṃ śirasi nikṣipet | 
nibandhayenmahāmudrānopari bhrāmayetkaram || 

p. 499) 

kāmike - 

prārthayitvā japaṃ kuryādakṣasūtreṇa buddhimān | 
akṣiṇāmakṣakalpānāṃ niyamo'yaṃ prasūyate || 

dṛṣṭādṛṣṭaphalāvāpterakṣasūtraṃ taducyate | 

tadyathā - japamālāmādāya haste vinyastahṛdā nirīkṣya 
īśānena prokṣya astreṇa diśo badhvā kavacenāvakuṇṭhya 
kalāmastrairabhimantrya gandhapuṣpādibhirhṛdā saṃpūjya

mūlamantra maṣṭottaraśataṃ tataḥ tadardhaṃ vā japet || 

p. 500) 

tathā aṃśumati - 

akṣamālāṃ samādāya vāmahaste ca vinyaset | īkṣaṇaṃ

digbandhanaṃ cāstramantreṇa kavacenāvakuṇṭhane | 
kalāmantrābhimantreṇa pañcasaṃskārasaṃyutam || 

kālottare - 

ubhayoścandrayormadhye svadehaṃ cintyayatnataḥ | tenāmṛtamayo deha 
uddhṛṣyamastradaśairapi? ||

nyasenmantraṃ na puṃsi na śive tathā || 

aśaktāḥ puruṣasthāḥ syuḥ śivasthāḥ? śāntatejasaḥ | 
tasmācchaktirga(ga?)tā mātrāḥ(mantrā?) 
bhuktimuktiphalapradām(dāḥ?) || 

nātmanīnapare tattve yojayediti | 

p. 501) 

tathā - 

na puṃsi na pare tattve śaktu mantraṃ niyojayet | puṃstattve 
jaḍatāṃ yāti bere tattve tu niṣphalam ||

siddhyate mantro nāhutistu phalapradam || 

nārcate yacchate kāmaṃ tasmātratayamācaret | 

iti || 

śaktirādyā parā vipraśaiva phala pradāḥ | śivādabhinnā 
sājñeyā sarvadā raviraśmivat ||

ravicandrāgnisaṃnibhā | ekadvitridhā tu vijñeyā iḍā 
piṅgalāndihedataḥ || 

suṣumnāntargatā gūḍhā jñātā gurumukhena tu] | * * * * * * * *? 
prāṇena vartate * * * * | 

p. 502) 

triśūlāmārgato yāti dvādaśāntaṃ tu yoginām | gamāgama 
prabhedena yāti sā śāṃkare pade || 

[ānābhe dvādaśāntastaṃ vyāptā] 

paraṃ bhāvaṃ samāśritya parānantaṃ? samaśnute | iti 
śaktādikaṃ jñātvā mantrarakṣākṣamo bhavet || 

ṣaṭsahasrike - 

īśvaraḥ - 

śatenāṣṭottareṇaiva uttamaṃ parikīrtitam | tadardhaṃ madhyamaṃ 
śastaṃ tadardhātkanyasaṃhitam || 

rudrākṣajaṃ mahābhūtyai muktidaṃ paramaṃ śubham | 
ekavaktraistrivaktraiśca caturvaktraiśca pañcabhiḥ || 

ṣaḍvaktraiścaiva kartavyaṃ tasmā miśraistu varjayet | mukhe 
mukhaṃ prakartavyaṃ mūle mūlena yojayet || 

dhātrīphalapramāṇena śreṣṭhametadudāhṛtam | 

p. 503) 

badarasya pramāṇena caṇakaṃ madhyamādhamau | navatītantunā 
proktaṃ kartavyaṃ pūrvavacchubham || 

nāgapāśaṃ tu sāvitraṃ brahmākhyaṃ vātha granthanam | 
granthyūrdhve merantu merākhyaṃ kartavyaṃ taṃ na laṅghayet || 

kṣālayetsadyamantreṇa vāmadevena gharṣayet | dhūpayedbahurūpeṇa 
lepayetpuruṣeṇa tu ||

meruṃ ca pañcabhiḥ pūjya pūrvavacca yathāśivam | 

sūkṣme - 

kaniṣṭhānāmikāmadhye puṣpaṃ saṃgṛhya buddhimān | 
pracchannapaṭamācchādya mūlamantramanusmaran || 

p. 504) 

tathā cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

mūlamantraṃ japet paścādakṣamālā samanvitam | vāmahaste tu

dakṣahastre paṭenyasya japaṃ kuryādviśeṣataḥ | 
mānasopāṃśubhāṣyāṇāṃ prathamaṃ cottamottamam || 

upāṃśu madhyamaṃ proktamadhamaṃ bhāṣyameva hi | tasmādbhāṣyaṃ 
samutsṛjya mānasopāṃśu vā japet || 

bhīmasaṃhitāyām - 

upāṃśu yajane proktaṃ vācikaṃ homakarmaṇi | mānasaṃ 
mantrajaptena sarvamantramudāhṛtam || 

kālottare - 

aṅguṣṭhānāmikā caiva nityameva japedbudhaḥ | 

p. 505) 

aṅguṣṭhānāmikā caiva sarvakāmaphalapradā | 

tathā ṣaṭsahasre - 

aṅguṣṭhānāmikāgrasya japaṃ kuryāttu mānasam | 
aṣṭottaraśatenaiva phalaṃ koṭiguṇaṃ bhavet || 

akṣarākṣarasandhānaṃ na drutaṃ na vilambitam | phalaṃ 
sahasraguṇitaṃ prāpnayānnānṛtaṃ vacaḥ || 

bhuktaye sṛṣṭimārgeṇa gharṣayedakṣasūtrake | ūrdhvamārgeṇa 
muktyarthaṃ gharṣayettu śanaiḥ śanaiḥ || 

meruṃ na laṅghayenmantrī laṅghanāddoṣabhāgbhgavet | sahasraṃ 
bahurūpasya japtavyaṃ tena śudhyati || 

pramādātpatitaṃ bhūmau japtavyaṃ tu śatadvayam | patane ca 
tathānyatra prāyaścittaṃ na ceṣyate || 

p. 506) 

meruhīnākṣamāle(lā?) tu kartavyā vā prayatnataḥ | na 
syādullaṅghano doṣo yato meruvivarjitaḥ || 

tathā - 

svaṃ mantraṃ cākṣasūtraṃ ca gurorapi na darśayet | japakāle tu 
goptavyā japamālā ca ṣaṇmukha || 

paradṛṣṭigatā mālā syānniṣphalayo japaḥ | 

evaṃ yathoktaṃ cāṣṭaśataṃ sapuṣpaṃ japtvā tatpuṣpaṃ 
japakāri svadakṣiṇakaratalādvāma karatale sthāpya astrakavaca 
hṛdayānyuparyupari vinyasya hṛdayakavacāstraiḥ saṃpūṭīkṛtya 
astreṇa rakṣitaṃ kavacenā vakuṇṭhitaṃ dakṣiṇahaste gṛhītvā 

p. 507) 

hṛtpradeśe nidhāya pūrṇacandropamaṃ 
hṛdayacandrikāṅkuraśaktisthaṃ japaṃ dhyātvā

krameṇa culukodakena śivasya varadahaste nivedayet ||

hṛdayenaiva kavacenānuveṣṭitam || 

astreṇa rakṣitaṃ caiva mudrayodbhavasaṃjñayā | 

iti || 

p. 508) 

ajite - 

culukodakavidhiṃ vakṣye tacchṛṇuṣva janārdana | 
arghodakasamāpādyaṃ dakṣiṇe hastasaṃpuṭe || 

gandhapuṣpākṣatopetaṃ hṛdi taṃ saṃniveśya ca | paṭhe 
mantryamammantrī tacchṛṇutvaṃ samāhitaḥ || 

o hāṃ guhyādiguhyagoptātvaṃ gṛhāṇāsmatkṛtaṃ japam | 

siddhirbhavati me yena tvatprasādātvadhisthite || 

oṃ hāṃ yat kiñcit karṇahe deva sadā sukṛtaduṣkṛtam | tamme 
śivapadasthasya bhuṅkṣvakṣapayaśaṃkaram || 

oṃ hāṃ śivo dātā śivo bhoktā śivassarvamidaṃ jagat | śivo 
yajati sarvatra yaśśivassohameva tu || 

asyātvaḥ(rthaḥ?) guhyādīni śuddhavidyādīni 

p. 509) 

teṣāṃ goptā tvameva ca | atassarvagoptṛtvau? manasmatkṛtaṃ? 
japaṃ gṛhāṇa | yena yasmādasmin jape turyavasthite sati 
tvatprasādādasmākaṃ bhuktimuktilakṣaṇasiddhirbahvati nānyathā 
tasmādgṛhāṇeti | he deva sukṛtaṃ duṣkṛtaṃ vā yat kiñcinme 
karmāsti | śivapadasthasya śivasya padekavasāye śivo'hamiti 
bhāvanādhyavasāye sthitasya | padaṃ vyavasitatrāṇasthāna 
lakṣmāṅghrivastuṣu ityamaraḥ | tatkarma bhukṣva | 
mokṣāntanāyā(rāyā?) pabhauraṇāya?(paharaṇāya?) sukṛtarūpaṃ 
pāla kṣapaya? | duṣkṛtarūpaṃ nāśaya | 

p. 510) 

atha mantrāntaram - 

śivo dātā śoyasthitā deśo yana ityayaṃ dhāturatra vivakṣitaḥ | 
śivo bhoktā pālayitā śivassarvamidaṃ jagat | īṣṭa iti śeṣaḥ 
| adhitiṣṭhatīti vā | yastu dīkṣāyā śivaśśuddho bhūtvā 
sarvatra deśeṣvadhikaraṇeṣu yajati śodhayitratyādi viśiṣṭaṃ 
paramaśivaṃ pūjayati | sa ārādhakaḥ śivo'hameva tvaṃ cārādhya 
śiva ityanena pūjyapūjakayoḥ jagannātha tavāsmīti 
yatsvīkāropadarśanamiti svasvāmisaṃbandha 
pradarśanamupapādayatīti 

p. 511) 

evaṃ mantraṃ samuccārya svanāmamūlamantrataḥ | 

liṅgamūle samāyojya kṣamasveti visarjayet || astreṇa

añjaliṃ dakṣiṇe hastaṃ kuñcitaṃ caturaṅgulam | puṣpavāri 
gṛhītvā tu pādamūle pradāpayet || 

culukodakamiti khyātaṃ pūjānte tu prajāpate |

tu gandhāmbuculukaistribhiḥ || 

iti | taduktaṃ kāmike - 

mūlāddaśāṃśato'ṅgānāṃ japaḥ kāryo'tra dhīmatā | 

p. 512) 

tathā cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

śivāṅgānāṃ japaṃ cāpi japāṃśca dāpayettadā | 

tadanu - sārdha mantrapūjakastadarthaṃ tu vijñāpya 
labdhānujñaśśivaliṅgamadhye pūjitasya śivasyāsanabhūtapadmasya

paścimabhāge - 

sudhākalaśamadhyasthaśvetapadmāsanasthitaḥ |

p. 513)

kapālagulikāṃ vāme yogamudrākaradvaye | anena sadṛśo mantro

p. 514)

p. 515)

śivasya dakṣiṇe bhāge vibhavena tu pūjayet |

taduktam sarvajñānottare - 

p. 516) 

tathābhyarcya maheśāna liṅgāgre

tasmāttatpadmadakṣiṇadalasthātpaścima madhyeṣveva

p. 517)

vispaṣṭastāmasaṃ(saḥ?) prokto rājasāpāṃ?śurucyate | manasā

p. 518)

divyasiddhīnāṃ japaṃ pūjyādvicakṣaṇaḥ || 

pauṣkare - 

uttamaṃ manasā mantrammadhyamānapyupāṃśunā | 
adhamānasimantrāstu bhāṣyeṇe japedbudhaḥ? || 

sarvajñānottare - 

divākṛtāmasaṃ kuryājjapaṃ rātraṇa tu rājasam? | 

sandhyāyāṃ sāttvikaṃ proktojapassarvārthasādhakam sarvadā

matāmātmanaścintayanjapaphalaṃ cirāt prāpnoti | na tu sa 
evāhamityetya bhāvanā tadānīm | 

p. 519) 

pūjyapūjakabhāvasya phaladātṛsaṃbhavādadvaitapakṣaṃ tyaktvā 
bhāvanāviśeṣeṇa bhāvayet | taduktaṃ - 

sarvajñānottare - 

ananyamānaso yogī mantre tanmayatāṃ gataḥ | acirātsiddhimāpnoti 
advaitadvaitamāśritaḥ || 

kathaṃ punassādhakoccāriteṣu 
bhūtākāśavāyuprabhaveṣvaśuddheṣu varṇeṣu śuddhānāṃ 
mantrāṇāṃ vācyatvena sānnidhyam | na tu sākṣāt | kiṃ tu 
teṣvaṅgārakasthānītveṣu abhivyaktā agnisthānīyāḥ śiva- 

p. 520) 

kṛtāḥ śuddhavarṇāsteṣāṃ vācakatvena sannidhiheto(ta?)vo 
bhavantītyarthaḥ | kāmyamantrajāpako mantrārambhakāle guruṇā 
siddhasādhyādikoṣṭheṣu sādhakaśiṣyasyānukūlyaṃ nāma 
nakṣatraṃ jñātvā guruṇā japādupadeśaḥ kartavyam | taduktam 
kāmike - 

yasyānukūlo mantro(ntraḥ?) sādhakasya sa siddhidaḥ | 
siddha?ssādhyasusiddhāni sādhakākhyāni mākṣaraḥ || 

evamanyatra sādhyādau caturthīkalpamāmatā | japādiṣu 
pradassiddhassāryoccā japatarpaṇaiḥ || 

susiddhaḥ pāṭhamātreṇa sādhakāriṣṭhato ripum? | 

p. 521) 

pracuraśśastravarṇaistu mantro nāśakaraḥ smṛtaḥ | śiṣyo'pi 
śīlasaṃpannaḥ pūtrovā śivabhaktimān || 

tasya deyo'bhi guruṇā sahasraṃ tājjapedguruḥ | prāpteṣu 
tacchalātprāptamagrahītaṃ gūḍharūpatam || 

prakārāntarataḥ jape prāptaṃ dyati vināśakṛt | 
japādvādikriyāsiddhaṃ mantrairyassādhakottamaḥ || 

tasyālpasādhanādanye mantrāssarvārthasiddhidāḥ | evaṃ 
bījajapaḥ kāryastaddvitriguṇasaṃkhyayā || 

mālātmakasya mantrasya japasaṃkhyā vidhīyate | anuktasaṃkhyā 
mantrasya śataṃ sāṣṭasahasrakam || 

japāddaśāṃśato homastvabhiṣekavidhaumataḥ | 
mūlāddaśāṃśato'ṅgānāmarca(rcā?) homajapādikam || 

p. 522) 

japākṣarādhikā mantrāstannyūnā bījasaṃjñakāḥ | 

pūrakādyakṣarāṇi yathākrameṇa likhet | 

udvanirudragrahapakṣapetamārtāṇḍadikṣaḍgajaṣoḍaśeṣān 
saptatiśiśca viśvekoṣṭhapradeśeṣvadhameva likhyaḥ | itthaṃ 
tattaccheṣākṣara vinyasya 

mālāmantrāśca vikhyātā viṃśatyarṇādhikāśca ye | iti 
dañcavidyāḥ atha raṇiraṇibhāvakramaḥ || 

nāmādyakṣaramārabhya yāvanmantrādivarṇakaḥ | 

triṣaṭdvipañcaikacaturneti mantrā yadi yateḥ eka dve iti jñeyaṃ 
sādhakāya pradīpate | 
atrādhiṣṭheyamantropādhivaśādadhiṣṭhātā 

p. 523) 

śivo'pi tāvat bhede'?bhinna upacāreṇa bhavati | tathā 
sarvajñānottare - 

prāṇināṃ cittabhedāntu(ttu?) mantrabhedaiḥ paraśśivaḥ | 
saṃsthitānugrahāttthāya sākṣibhūtastu karmaṇām || 

sthānaṃ karma ca bhāvaṃ ca devatāvyāptimeva ca | vartate vīkṣya 
viśvātmā phalakṣetra vibhaktiṣu || 

iti || tadanu vidyāpīṭhaṃ pūjayet || tathā - 

devasya paścime bhāge vidyāpīṭhaṃ tato yajet | 

kāmike tu - 

vāyavye dakṣiṇe vā vijñānakośaṃ prakalpayet | kāmikādi 
śivajñānaṃ sthānaṃ yāmyadiśi sthitam | etadvacanaṃ 
parārthālaye sākṣātsthāpanaviṣayam | 

p. 524) 

tathā śivadharmottare - 

śaileṣṭamṛṇmayaṃ vā vijñānakośaṃ prakalpayet | dārubhiḥ? 
kṛtamevaṃ vā kavāṭārgalasaṃyuktaṃ prakārāvāma? śobhitam | 

kāmike - 

trihastaṃ tu samārabhya trayastriṃśatkarāntaram | vidyāśālā 
viśālā syātprāsādo vātha maṇṭapam || 

ekaśālādibhedena śālā vā lakṣaṇānvitaḥ | vidyāpīṭhasya 
vistāraṃ saptāṅgulaviśālakam || 

vidyāṅgulavivṛddhyā tu syādekatriṃśadaṅgulaiḥ | tenāyāmaḥ 
samākhyātaḥ pañcāśatkarajāntakaḥ | caturaśrasamā 
vāsyādāyatāśrastu vā bhavet || 

p. 525) 

anyadāsana vidhyuktamārgeṇa sakalaṃ nayet | 

śivadharmotare - 

vidyāsiṃhāna śrīmānnāgamāntādinirmitam | suvarṇaratnaracitaṃ 
dukūlāstaraṇānvitaḥ || 

daṇḍāsanaṃ vā śrīmantaṃ hemaratnobhaśobhitam | 
nāgendradantaracitaṃ raktacandanadārujam || 

sthāpya puṣpagṛhasyāntargandhapuṣpaiḥ prapūjayet | oṃ hāṃ 
kāmikādi śivajñānāvatārakebhyo(bhya?) āsanaṃ nama iti 
saṃpūjya paścāttadupari vidyākośamañjūṣaṃ sthāpayet || 

taduktam śivadharmottare - 

yassauvarṇaṃ susaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvaratnopaśobhitam | sāpidhānaṃ 
samañjūṣaṃ vidyākośasamāśrayam || 

p. 526) 

kārayedvātha raupyeṇa tāmreṇa caturaśrakam | 
kāṃsyārakūṭalohairvā dāruvaṃśādinirmitam || 

satkaṣāyānuraktena carmaṇābhinavena ca | antarbahiśca 
maṭhayedvidyāvāsaṃ gṛhaṃ navam || 

sudvāra kaṭakopetaṃ madṛḍha(dṛḍha?) sūtranibandhanam | 
kuryāttālakasaṃyuktaṃ vidyārattakaraṇḍakam || 

evaṃ vittānusāreṇa kārayitvānurūpataḥ | prakṣālya gandhatoyena 
vidyākośagṛhaṃ paraḥ || 

kṛtvā vastragṛhaṃ divyamantaḥ puṣpagṛhādhikaḥ | 
vidyāsiṃhāsane tatra sthāpayedvidhivadbudhaḥ || 

tasminpuṇyāhaśabdena vidyākośagṛhenyaset | tataḥ 
saṃpūjayedvidvānmahāvibhavavistaraiḥ || 

p. 527) 

atra vidyāpīṭhapūjā dvividhā | aparokṣāparokṣā ceti | tatra 
pratyakṣalikhitakāmikāditantraviṣayā pūjāparokṣā | 
maṇḍalapadmapīṭhādiviṣayā parokṣā | prathamāyāmapi 
sarvasaṃhitā svasaṃhitā vā pūjanīyā | tathā - 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

vidyāpīṭhaṃ koṭimātraṃ pūjayedvā svasaṃhitām | 
gandhapuṣpādi naivedyamālābhiḥ śivavatsudhīḥ || 

mṛgendre ca - 

vidyāpīṭhe'pi matvaivamekoccāreṇa pūjayet | 
sraggandhadhūpanaivedyaiḥ samācarucirīśvaram? || 

śivabhede śivaḥ pūjyo rudrabhede sa eva hi | 

p. 528) 

yadvā samuditarūpeṇa | 

oṃ hāṃ sarvebhyo(bhyaḥ?) śivajñānebhyo nama iti saṃpūjya oṃ 
sarvajñānapradaṃ śambhuṃ sarvajñānavidhātakam? | kāyena manasā 
vācā vidyāpīṭhāśrayaṃ bhaje || iti smṛtvā kṣamasveti brūyāt 
| caturaśramaṇḍalapadme tu karṇikāyāṃ oṃ hauṃ śivāya namaḥ | 
oṃ hāṃ vāgīśāya namaḥ | iti yajet | 

tadāvṛta pūrvādidaśadaleṣu oṃ hāṃ kāmikāya namaḥ iti pūrve 
vahnyādinavadaleṣu yogajādisuprabhedākhyāni 
śivabhedāgamajñānāni jñānārthī pūjayet | śivena 
adhikaraṇanirapekṣeṇa sākṣādbhagavatā 

p. 529) 

śivabhedānāmuccāritatvād anādirūpādirudrādhikaraṇe śivena 
vijayādīnāṃ tantrāṇāṃ hyutpattiḥ | atra 
tadāvārakāṣṭādaśapadma daleṣu pūrvādikrameṇa 
rudrabhedākhyāni vijayādi vātulāntātya(nya?)ṣṭādaśatantrāṇi 
tanmantreṇa pūjayet | tathā 

vātule - 

kṛtvā daśadalaṃ padmaṃ maṇḍale caturaśrake | madhye śivaṃ 
savāgīśaṃ jñānārthi(rthī?) pūrvadikkramāt || 

daleṣu kāmikādīni śivajñānāni pūjayet | 
prāgvadaṣṭādaśadale padme pūrvāditaḥ kramāt || 

rudrajñānāni teṣveva guruyāge prapūjayet || iti 

p. 530) 

tadanu guruṃ pūjayet | taduktam - 

śivadharmottare - 

saṃpūjyaivaṃ śivajñānaṃ guruṃ saṃpūjayettataḥ | śivaṃ 
pūjayedbhaktyā śivajñāna prakāśakam || 

iti | gurupūjā dvividhā parokṣabhāvanā? ceti | guruṃ 
svapīṭhasthaṃ gatvā pādakamale prākṣālya śivamiva 
gandhādibhirabhyarcya puṣpāñjalitrayeṇābhyarcya samyagarcayet | 

ṣaṭsahasre - 

atha saṃnihitaṃ cātha pūrvavatpūjayedgurum | 

śivadharmottare - 

tridhā pradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā daṇḍavatpraṇametkṣitau | vijñāpayet 
samutthāya jānubhyāṃ dharaṇīṃ gataḥ || 

p. 531) 

kṛtāñjalipuṭo bhūtvā praṇipatya guruṃ punaḥ | 

mṛgendre - 

na bhuñjānaṃ samādhisthaṃ karmaṇāṃ kriyayodyatam | sthitaṃ 
guruṃ? samīpe vā matimānnābhivādayet || 

gacchantaṃ pṛṣṭhato yāyādviśantamanusaṃviśet | tadvaśo 
nānuyuñjīta śayānaṃ na prabodhayet || 

kālottare - 

īśvaraḥ - 

labhyate śāśvatā jñānaṃ guruprasādena ṣaṇmukha | devālaye 
gururyatra tiṣṭhate vai ṣaḍānana || 

pūrvaṃ guruṃ vandayitvā paścāddevaṃ hi vandayet | 

śivalope niṣkṛtiścāsti hīnaṃ guruṃ prāpyato ma?ktimathāpyati? 

p. 532) 

guruputraṃ yadā yogyaṃ tyaktvānyaṃ yadi kalpayet | niṣphalaṃ 
tasya vai dīkṣā mahākalpaṃ vrajantyathaḥ(dhaḥ?) || 

cintyaviśve - 

jñānahīnaṃ guruṃ prāpya kṛtomuktimathā prati? | tasmātsarva 
prayatnena saṃśrayejjñānapāragam || 

madhulubdho yathā bhṛṅgo mādhvīpuṣpaṃ vanāntare | 
jñānalubdhastathā śiṣyo gurorgurvantaraṃ vrajet || 

tallabdhvā visṛjecchiṣyo'pyadhaḥ patati niścayaḥ | 

ṣaṭsahasrike - 

śivavacca guruḥ pūjyaḥ pavitravaddeśikaḥ punaḥ | 
sādhakāstadvadevāhuḥ putrakā bhrātṛvatsadā || 

mitravatsamayasthāśca draṣṭavyā śivamicchatā | 

p. 533) 

atra dīkṣākartā guruḥ vyākhyātā deśika iti mantavyaḥ | 

ye kurvanti gurornindāṃ samayiputrakādiṣu | 
tasmātsthānādyajeddūraṃ sadyojātaśataṃ smaret || 

yo mohāccaiva pāpiṣṭho guruṇā śapathaṃ vadet | dadāti cānyo 
duṣṭātmā tā vubhau narakaṃ vrajet || 

praṇamya puṣpapātena prayatnātpūjayedgurum | gosahasraṃ phalaṃ 
prāpya guroḥ pādābhivandanāt || 

athānyānapi tapodhanān suvinītān sarvasannidhau bhaktyā 
yathocitaṃ praṇāmaṃ vidadhyāt | 

tathā mataṅge - 

yathānukramasaṃskārajyeṣṭho yaḥ pūrvadīkṣitaḥ | 

p. 534) 

putrakaḥ putrakasyāpi sādhakaḥ sādhakasya ca | gurorapi guruḥ 
proktaḥ samayoktinidarśanāt || 

dīkṣitassamayasthasya dīkṣitasyāpi sādhakaḥ | 
sādhakasyāpyathācāryo jyeṣṭhassa parikīrtitaḥ || 

vandayedātmanaḥ śreṣṭhān gurubhrātṛgaṇānapi | athānyānapi 
saṃśuddhācchaivānvandet tapodhanān || 

laukikādiṣu mārgeṣu caturṣvapi samāśitān | api 
vidyātapojyeṣṭhānmṛ(nvra?)tastho nābhivādayet || 

saṃskārābhyadhiko jyeṣṭho vratināṃ śivaśāsane | na tu 
jātivayovṛddhā vidyā jyeṣṭhatvakāraṇam || 

vidyamānau? śiśau jyeṣṭheda?bhiṣikte kanīyasā | gurośca 
guruṇā vāpi kaiścitpaścātpratīkṣitaiḥ || 

p. 535) 

anyonyagauravaṃ taistu kartavyaṃ vandanaṃ vinā | 

tathā - 

dīkṣābhiṣeka jyeṣṭhena kaniṣṭhasya kadācana | 
tapovidyādhikasyāpi kartavyaṃ nābhivādanam || 

bhrātṛputre yathā vṛddho yavīyānguruputrakaḥ | pratyutthāna 
gurustasya nābhivādanamarhati || 

iti || 

atha bhavanāprakāraḥ - 

devasyāgneyadigbhāge yathā saṃbhavamānaṃ kamalaṃ kṛtvā 
tanmadhye ṣaḍutthaṃ? parikalpya tatra guru(raṃ?) 
mukti(mūrti?)mantreṇa vinyasya tasyāṃ śivamāvāhyāṅgāni nyasya 
pādyādikaṃ dattvā gandhapuṣpādīni śivapadamabhyarcya dhyātvā 

p. 536) 

pāśānvimocya kṛpayā nililānanādi(nikhilānanādī?)nyo māṃ 
śarīranidhane paramuktibhājam | cakre samastaśivaśāstra 
vibodhahetuṃ nityaṃ tameva gurunāthamahaṃ prapadye || iti 
stotrādibhistoṣayet | tathāgneyadigbhāge yogapīṭhaṃ tato yajet | 

ṣaṭsahasre - 

sumitaṃ hastamātraṃ tu kamalaṃ vartayecchubham | 
karṇikādalasaṃyuktaṃ tanmadhye svaguruṃ yajet || 

tatrāsanaṃ tu saṃkalpya mūrtiṃ tasyopari nyaset | 
śivamāvāhayettatra sa brahmāṅgasamanvitam || 

p. 537) 

puṣpagandhādibhiḥ pūjya hṛnmantreṇa prayatnataḥ | 
vidhānametatkathitaṃ gurauśivapade gate || 

ājñāṃ gṛhītvā yatnena tataḥ karma samārabhet | 

tathā - kiraṇe - 

prāguktaṃ yogapīṭhaṃ tu kṛtvā prāgarcitaṃ gurum | 
caturthyantaṃ nyasenmūrtiṃ sakalīkṛtya pūrvavat || 

śivamāvāhya tatsthāne nyasedaṅgāni pūrvavat | 
gandhapuṣpādibhiḥ pūjya hṛdā guruvaraṃ khaga || 

iti || 

śivadharmottare - 

śivaṃ saṃpūjya vidhivattadvidyāṃ pūjayettataḥ | guruṃ ca 
śivabhaktayā tu tulyametattrayaṃ yataḥ || 

p. 538) 

yathā śivastathā vidyā yathā vidyā tathā guruḥ | śiva vidyā 
gurūṇāṃ ca pūjāyāṃ sadṛśaṃ phalam || 

atra ācāryapūjā prasaṅgena maṭhādau sthāpitaśiva 
bhaktapūjāvidhānamucyate | 

kāmike - 

śivabhaktisamopetā jīvanto vā mṛtāstu ca | teṣāṃ pratikṛtiṃ 
kṛtvā pratiṣṭhāpya samarcayet || 

tathā tatraiva - 

tataḥ prakṛtimā? bhaktaṃ pūjayetpratyahaṃ naraḥ | āvaśyakaṃ 
tathā śaucaṃ snānaṃ sandhyābhivandanam || 

kṛtvā praviśya harmyaṃ tu paramātmānamavyayam | vāstumadhye 
samabhyarcya bhūtaśuddhiṃ vidhāya ca || 

p. 539) 

kṛtamantratanuḥ paścātsthāna śuddhiṃ vidhāya ca | parikalpya 
viśeṣārghya kusumākṣatatoyayuk || 

prokṣayeddravyajālaṃ tu prāguktaṃ vidhinā naraḥ | praṇavādyaṃ 
paṭhenmantraṃ namo'ntaṃ mantraśuddhaye || 

astratoyena saṃsthāpya hṛdāya(hṛdā ca?) bimbaśuddhaye | 
ṣaḍutthamāsanaṃ kṛtvā pūrvoktavidhinā tataḥ || 

ādhārākhye manantaṃ(no'ntaṃ?) ca dharmādyānāṃ catuṣṭayam | 
saṃkalpya mānuṣaṃ pīṭhaṃ praṇavena prakalpayet || 

vidyāṅgāni ca pādasya syādākhyadyarṇayastadā ? | 

manuḥ - 

āvāhanaṃ tataḥ kṛtvā hṛdayādīntadā nyaset | pādyamācamanaṃ 
cārghyaṃ hṛdayena niveśa(da?)yet || 

p. 540) 

saṃsnāpya gandhatoyena pañcagavyādibhistu vā | saṃmṛjya vāsasā 
vastraṃ(ktraṃ?) gandhapuṣpaṃ ca dhūpakam || 

nityasnānaṃ vinā lohe snānaṃ parvaṇi parvaṇi | dīpena sahitaṃ 
dadyānnaivedyaṃ sopadaṃśakam || 

tāmbūlaṃ ca trisandhyāsu dadyātsandhyādvaye tu vā | dīpāntaṃ 
vā nivedyāntaṃ yajecchaktyānurūpataḥ || 

śivaṃ puṣpāñjalitrayeṇa abhyarcya stotramidaṃ vijñāpayet | 

śrīmadraurave - 

samantrapūtānsaṃspṛśya puṣpapūrṇojjvalāñjalīn | 
harṣābhyāgataromāñca prāhavātyamidaṃ kuru || 

svarādhipaṃ namaskṛtya śaśāṅkakṛtaśekharam | kapālamālinaṃ 
devaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

p. 541) 

sarvakṛtsarvavettāraṃ sarvajñamaparājitam | 
sarvabhūtārthabhūtasthaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

yo'sṛjatsarvabhūtāni brahmādīṃśca purā surān | tamahaṃ 
sarvakartāraṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

varaṃ vareṇyaṃ varadaṃ variṣṭhaṃ vara vāhanam | 
varamālādharaṃ devaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

prītimatprītidātāraṃ supriyaṃ priyadarśanam | umāpriyaṃ 
sadākāntaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

dharmadharmamayaṃ devaṃ sukhaduḥkhaphalapradam | 
bandhamokṣapradātāraṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

manobuddhirahaṃkāra tanmātrendriya gocaram || 

pradhānapuruṣeśānaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam | 

p. 542) 

dhyāyanti yaṃ mahātmāno munayaḥ saṃśitavratāḥ | 
tamoṃkāraparaṃ sūkṣmaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

sūkṣmaṃ sarvagataṃ nityaṃ sarvavidyeśvareśvaram | 
sarvatattvāśrayaṃ devaṃ praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

bhūmirāpo'gniranilaḥ khamātmā candrabhāskarau | tasmai 
paramadevāya praṇato'smi sadāśivam || 

bhaktyā saṃpūjya deveśaṃ yaḥ paṭhedagrataḥ stavam | 
stotrametadasau yāti śāntavatparamaṃ padam || 

ityādibhiḥ parameśvaraṃ stutvā pradakṣiṇaṃ sāṣṭāṅgaṃ 
praṇāmaṃ vidadhyāt || 

taduktaṃ kāmike - 

stutvā naikavidhaiḥ stotrairgranthadraviḍapūrvakaiḥ | 
gartoditairmukhyataraiḥ praṇipatya punaḥ punaḥ || 

p. 543) 

pūjānte pradakṣiṇaṃ na karoti cettattatphalamapi na labhate | 
tathoktam - 

pūjāṃ kṛtvāpi yaśśambhorna karoti pradakṣiṇam | sā pūjā 
niṣphalā tasya vaca pūjā viḍambakaḥ? || 

bhaktyā karoti yassamyakkevalaṃ tu pradakṣiṇam | pūjā sarvakṛtā 
tena sa samyak śivapūjakaḥ || 

iti || tato'ṣṭapuṣpikayā saṃpūjya viśeṣārghyaṃ dattvā 
sadyādikrameṇa pañcaśirassu śivamabhyarcya śirasi 
tirobhāvārthanirodhārghyeṇādhomukhahastena svāhāntamūlena 
pūrvavadūrdhvamukho bhaveti prārthya parāṅmukhārghyaṃ dattvā 
astrādihṛdayāntānyaṅgāni 

p. 544) 

saṃpūjya pūjāprātilomyenārghyaṃ dadyāt | yadvā oṃ hāṃ 
śivāya sāṅgāya parāṅmukhārghyaṃ svāheti arghyaṃ dadyāt || 

aṃśumati - 

layāṅgayajanaṃ kṛtvā parāṅmukhārghyaṃ dadedbudhaḥ | 
sadyādīśāntamabhyarcya vyutkrarmeṇaiva yojayet || 

caturdikṣvastramabhyarcya somādīndrāntameva ca | netramagre 
samabhyarcya kavacaṃ vāyudeśake || 

nair-ṛte ca śikhāṃ caiva śirastvīśānadeśake | āgneyyāṃ

surabhissuśīlā sumanāstathā || 

kapilaṃ kṛṣṇaśvetaṃ ca dhūmraraktaṃ tathaiva ca | 
nandādisumanāntānāṃ varṇamevaṃ prakīrtitam || 

sarvalokopakārārthaṃ devānāṃ tarpaṇāya ca | gomātarastathā 
bhūmau snānārthaṃ hi śivasya tu || 

gomayaṃ rocanā mūtraṃ kṣīraṃ dadhi ghṛtaṃ gavām | 
ṣaḍaṅgāni pavitrāṇi sarvasiddhikarāṇi ca || 

p. 573) 

gobhiryajñāḥ pravartante gobhirdevāḥ pratiṣṭhitām | 
gobhirvedāḥ samutkīrṇāssaṣaḍaṅgapatakramāḥ || 

ityāṃ gāvassamālokya pañcagavyārthamadbūtāḥ | grāsapūrvaṃ 
samabhyarcya svasvanāmnā dhruveṇa ca || 

gandhapuṣpapavitrādyairvandayenmantrapūrvakam | 

oṃ kapile nande namaḥ | oṃ kapile bhadre namaḥ | oṃ kapile suśīle 
namaḥ | oṃ kapile surabhe namaḥ | oṃ kapile samane namaḥ || 

saurabheyi jaganmātardevānāmamṛtaprade | gṛhāṇa varade 
grāsamīpsitārthaṃ ca dehi me || 

vanditāsi vasiṣṭhena viśvāmitreṇa jahnunā | 

p. 574) 

kapilā hara me pāpaṃ yanmayā duṣkṛtaṃ kṛtam || 

gāvo mamāgrato nityaṃ gāvaḥ pṛṣṭhata eva me | gāvo me hṛdaye 
vāpi gāvammadhye vasāmyaham || 

etanmanaḥ japenmantraṃ trisandhyaṃ niyataśśucaḥ | 
vimuktassarvapāpebhyo(bhyaḥ?) śivalokaṃ sa gacchati || 

athavā - 

amṛtamathanotpanne surabhe lokadhāriṇi | dadā grāsaṃ 
gṛhāṇatvaṃ idaṃ me vratamuttamam || 

gavāṃ grāse yathā devassaurabheyāśca caiva tu | tulyametatphalaṃ 
dṛṣṭaṃ kiṃ tu mantraṃ pṛthak pṛthak | sarvalokadharāheta(tā?) 
jīvitānna pradāyinaḥ | 

p. 575) 

grāsaṃ grāsaṃ gṛhṇātvaṃ ṣṭāste etanme vratamuttamam | 
nityamāpogave dadyādgṛhītvā durlabhaṃ vratam || 

rakṣanti ca bhayādghorātsparśane pāpanāśanam | stutvaivaṃ 
parayā bhaktyā tato gobhyo vicakṣaṇaḥ || 

grāhayetpañcagavyāni yadvā tāsāmabhāvataḥ | ekasyā me 
gṛhnīyātpūjā pūrvoktaśambaraiḥ || 

bhavedatrāpi sarvāsāṃ kapilā ca praśasyate | 

ityātmārthapūjāpaddhatyāṃ 

kapila(lā?)pūjāvidhiḥ 

p. 576) 

|| śivālayasevākramaḥ || 

aṃśumati - 

parārthālayaṃ praveśyātha liṅgadarśanameva ca | 
pradakṣiṇanamaskāraṃ yathā saṃkhyāvidhānataḥ || 

liṅgasya darśanaṃ paścātsvagṛhaṃ supraveśayet | 
purāṇāgamavākyāni samālokya yathāmati || 

śivālayasya sevāyāḥ kramaṃ vakṣye'dhunā sphuṭam | 

śivālayasamīpavāsi (sī?) cedyathoktānuṣṭhānapūjānantaram 
devālayaṃ sevate | sevārthaṃ niyamena dūrādagaccheddūrato 
vimānaṃ dṛṣṭvā prathamaṃ namata | darśanasthāne puṇyatīrthe 
snātvā śuddhavāsāḥ kṛtāhnikaḥ sopavāsaḥ 

p. 577) 

supuṣpādyupahāraḥ pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā śivasevāṃ kṛtvā 
śivasevāṃ kuryāt | 

tatathā śaivapurāṇe - 

svagṛhādeva saṃkalpya yātrāṃ vyāghrapūrasya tu | devadevaṃ 
samabhyaryya bhogāgatvā? dvidhānapi || 

saṃprāthya tairanujñāto nirgato bhaktisaṃyutaḥ | parānnaṃ 
varjayeddhīmāntathā naiva pratigraham || 

kāmakrodhādikāndoṣānviśeṣeṇa vivarjayet | 
upavāsādikānkuryātkārayedvapanaṃ tathā || 

snānaṃ tu vidhivatkuryāddhyātvā devaṃ triyambakam | kṛtvā 
hiraṇyaśrāddhaṃ tu piṇḍaṃ dattvā vidhānataḥ || 

yathāśaktidhanaṃ dhānyaṃ vastraṃ cānyaṃ tathaiva hi | 

p. 578) 

bhūmiṃ hiraṇyāṃ gāṃ kanyāṃ vittaśāṭhyavivarjitaḥ | 
dadyāttu śivabhaktebhyo taddvijebhyo viśeṣataḥ || 

itarakṣetreṣvapyevamanusandheyaḥ | 

tatrāpi pañcāṣṭakasthānasthitāni tīrthāni paramapuṇyāni tatra 
rudrasālokyapradāyakāni - 

ādiguhyāṣṭakā rudra atiguhyāṣṭakāstathā | 
guhyādguhyāṣṭakāścaiva pavitrāṣṭaka eva ca || 

snā(sthā?)nāṣṭakāśca pañcaite niyogādbhūmivāsinaḥ | 
anugrahāya lokānāṃ liṅgabhūtāḥ pratiṣṭhitāḥ || 

teṣāmeva samīpasthaṃ yadyattoyaṃ ṣaḍānana | tattīrthaṃ 
paramaṃ puṇyaṃ tatra snātvā śivaṃ vrajet || 

tatra dragalokyaṃ prāpnotītyarthaḥ | 

p. 579) 

tatra prāptai bhārate varṣe mānuṣāṇāṃ hitāya vai | nirmitāni 
purā yātrā yeṣu sannihito haraḥ || 

iti || 

suprabhede - 

dūrataḥ śikharaṃ dṛṣṭvā namaskuryāttu yo naraḥ | mucyate 
sarvapāpebhyo nirmokādiva pannagaḥ || 

tathā brahmāṇḍapurāṇe - 

vimāna darśanasthāne snātvā tīrthe kṛtāhnikaḥ | 
śuklavāsāssopahāraḥ praviśecchivamandiram ||

paritastāvatkailāsabhūtalam || 

śrūyate vādyaghoṣāśca yāvaddūrā śivālayāt | 

p. 580) 

tāvacchivapura sākṣāt danavadye manonmanī | sūtastotrāravā 
yāvaddūre śambhorjayadhvaniḥ || 

śrūyate rājatagireśśikharaṃ tāvaducyate | 

aṃśumātatre(mattantre?) - 

sthūlaliṅgaṃ namaskṛtya ālayaṃ tu praveśayet | sthūlaliṅgaṃ 
vimānaṃ syātsūkṣmaliṅgaṃ sadāśivam || 

balipīṭhaṃ bhadraliṅgaṃ liṅgatrayamihocyate | 

sūkṣme - 

dehaṃ prāsādamityuktaṃ dehi liṅgamiti smṛtam | ubhayorantaraṃ 
nāsti tasmāditthaṃ prabhāvayet || 

sthūlaliṅgaṃ vimānaṃ ca sūkṣmaliṅgaṃ sadāśivam | 
sadāśivasya tanmadhye śivamevaṃ vicintayet || 

p. 581) 

athavānyaprakāreṇa sthūlaliṅge sadāśivam | antarliṅge śivaṃ 
jñeyaṃ bhāvayeddeśikottamaḥ || 

vyāpakavyāptirūpeṇa liṅgameva vimānakam | hemādi 
sthūpiparyantaṃ śivarūpamiti smṛtam || 

āgneyapurāṇe - 

bere liṅge vimāne ca bhūtapīṭhe śivālaye | śivassaṃnihito 
nityaṃ namasyettatradaṃ sadā || 

puṣpādyaṃ svārjitaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ madhyamaṃ vanyamucyate | adhamaṃ 
tu krayakrītaṃ pāratyamadhamā dhamam || 

puṣpavanodbhāvanaṃ ca śivāśramāduttarapradeśe kāryam | tathā 

śivadharme - nandikeśvareṇoktam - 

p. 582) 

sarveṣāmeva varṇānāṃ śivāśramaniṣeviṇām | 
śivadharmāśśivenoktā dharmakāmārthamuktaye || 

brāhmaṇaḥ kṣatriyo vaiśyaḥ strī śūdro vā'pyathāśramāt | 
brahmacārī gṛhastho vā vānaprastho'tha bhikṣukaḥ || 

svāśramāduttare kuryātpuṣpārāmaṃ suśobhanam | 
puṣpairaraṇyasaṃbhūtaiḥ patrairvā girisaṃbhavaiḥ || 

aparyuṣitaniśchidraiḥ prokṣitairjantuvarjitaiḥ | 
ātmārāmodbhavaiścāpi puṣpaissaṃpūjayecchivam || 

kāmike - 

puṣpārāmamaṭhādīnāṃ sthānaṃ sarvatra kalpayet | 

iti vacanādyathā saṃbhavadeśenodbhāvanaṃ kuryāt || tataḥ 
śivālayaṃ seveta | 

p. 583) 

tatrāyaṃ kramaḥ - 

devālayasamīpamāgamya gopuradvārabāhyato vā 
vṛṣasyāgneyabhāgato vā pūrvaṃ sthūlaliṅgaṃ namaskuryāt | 

aṃśumāni(mati?)ti - 

sthūlaliṅgaṃ namaskuryādgopuradvārapūrvataḥ | praṇāmaḥ 
sthūlaliṅgasya mahāpīṭhasya bāhyataḥ || 

athavā vṛṣapṛṣṭhe tu kiñcidāgneyamāśritaḥ | 

tatraiva pūjāprakaraṇe - 

sthūlaliṅgaṃ namaskāramālayasya praveśanam | śivāgre paritaḥ 
sthitvā mahāpīṭhāgradeśi(śa?)ke || 

praṇamya daṇḍavadbhūmau namaskārakramaṃ tataḥ | 

ayamāgneyabhāganiyamaḥ pūrvadvāraviṣayaḥ | 

p. 584) 

praṇāmakāle svadehaṃ vastreṇācchādya na praṇamet | śivasyāgra 
pṛṣṭhavāmagarbhagṛheṣu namaskāraṃ na kuryāt | śivālaye 
tailābhyaṅgādikaṃ ca varjayet | tathā - aṃśumati 

vastreṇacchādya dehaṃ svaṃ yo naraḥ praṇamettu mām | trijanma 
jāyate mūkaḥ saptajanmasu nirdhanaḥ || 

svāyambhuve - 

agrataḥ pṛṣṭhato vāme garbhāgāre tathaiva ca | japaṃ homaṃ 
namaskāraṃ na vidadhyātkadācana || 

uttaradvārapaścimadvārayorīśvaranamaskārādikaṃ vāmabhāga eva 
kuryāt | 

tathā - 

p. 585) 

pratyaṅmukhe cottarādimukhe ca vāme sthito'nyatra tadanyapārśve | 
īśāna pūjā praṇatīśca kuryad doṣāya no cenna phalāyatāḥ? 
syuḥ | 

raurave - 

pracchannapaṭakāle na kartavyaṃ liṅgadarśanam | tatkāle darśanaṃ 
mohādbhāryāputravināśanam || 

skānde - 

tailābhyaṅgakṛtābhyaṅgaṃ śiraso veṣṭanaṃ tathā | 
kañcukāvaraṇaṃ cāpi varjayecchivamandire || 

kṣudha jṛmbhaṇahikkādi mohāni(nni?)ṣṭhīvanāni ca | 
utsargādīni ca naro varjayecchivamandire || 

upānatsātapatrādirahitaḥ kṣamayānvitaḥ | 

p. 586) 

tathā rāmāyaṇe ca - 

śivālayamupāgamya yastu dūṣayate naraḥ | malamūtraiśca 
niṣṭhīvaiḥ sa vetālo bhaviṣyati || 

yastu devālayagato vāhanādyadhirohati | so'pi te naiva doṣeṇa 
jātipaṅkurbhaviṣyati || 

sopānātko'pi yaḥ kaściddevālayamupeṣyati | saha pādikayābhi 
pādo duḥkhamupeṣyati || 

yadi vābhyarcanākāṅkṣī śivadravyāṇi pati | sa brahmarākṣaso 
bhūtvā śūnyāraṇyeṣu roditi || 

tadanu dhyānapūrvakaṃ balipīṭhaṃ namaskuryāt | 

ajite - 

bhūtapīṭhaṃ namaskuryātprathamadhyānapūrvakam | 

p. 587) 

mukhapañcasamavaktrasamanāsi 
nayane(naṃ?)nda(da?)śakarṇadaśabāhudaśaśūlavaradhṛk | 
galamekaṃ hṛdayeka(yaikama?)tiri(ra?)ktavarṇa(rṇaṃ?) dvayapādamiti 
bhūtabalipīṭhabalibhuk || 

atha dhvajastambhamapi sadhyānapūrvakaṃ namaskuryāt | tathā - 
dhvajadaṇḍanamaskāraṃ kuryāddhyānapurassaram | 

ekāsyapādaṃ? dvikaraṃ triṇetraṃ romāvalīrājitadarbhamālaḥ 
śvetāṅgarūpaṃ svakarāsikāyamārūḍha padmāsanadaṇḍarūpam || 

utsave dhyānaviśeṣaḥ - 

p. 588) 

patistambhamiti khyātaṃ paśu paṭamiti smṛtam | rajjuḥ pāśamiti 
proktaṃ trividhāste bhavanti ca || 

evaṃ sva(sa?)dhvajadaṇḍaṃ prārthya namaskṛtya vṛṣanāyakaṃ 
dhyānapūrvakaṃ namaskṛtya prārthayet | 

brahmāṇḍopapurāṇe - 

īśvarābhimukhaṃ devamāsānaṃ vṛṣanāyakam | praṇamya 
prārthayedevaṃ kusumābhyarcitāṅghrikam || 

ānandaṃ jagatāṃ śivaṃ ca 
kalayandevo'ṣṭamūrtervṛṣo'dhiṣṭhāno 
rajatācalendrasadṛśo(śaḥ?) 
śṛṅgīkakūrmatpatiḥ(kakudmatpatiḥ?) | devairaṣṭavidhairgaṇaiḥ 
parivṛto niśvāsayū(dhū?)tāmbudaḥ prīto'smānavatu 
prasādasumukhaḥ prajñāmayo dharmarāṭ || 

p. 589) 

antaḥ pradeśe śivadarśane ca śivārcane tatphalasiddhalābhe | 
dehyasyanujñāṃ bhagavatprasannaḥ tvatpādapadmapraṇatāya mahyam || 

iti vṛṣanāyakaṃ saṃprārthya śivasya purato daṇḍavatpraṇamet | 

tathā saṃprārthya dharmeśaṃ paśoḥ paśupateḥ paraḥ | praṇamya 
daṇḍavadbhūmau sāṣṭāṅgaṃ kusumaṃ kṣipet || 

aṃśumati - 

paśoḥ paśupateragre daṇḍavatpatito bhuvi | patanti pātakāssarve 
nottiṣṭhanti punassaha || 

dehasaṃsaktareṇūnāṃ yāvatsaṃkhyābhigaṇyate | tāvadvarṣa 
sahasrāṇi śivaloke mahīyate || 

p. 590) 

dīpte - asya namaskārasya cāturvidhamuttamaṃ tadyathā - 

aṣṭāṅgaiścaiva pañcāṅgatryaṅguñcaikāṅgakam | śikhā hastau 
ca karṇau ca cibukaṃ bāhuyugmakam || 

aṣṭāṅgaṃ ca namaskāraṃ pañcāṅgaṃ ca tataśśṛṇu | 
śirau(ro?) hastau ca bāhū ca kṛtvā jānuni saṃsthitau || 

idaṃ pañcāṅgamevoktaṃ tryaṅgaṃ ca tataśśṛṇu | hastau badhvā 
tu mukulaṃ mūrdhni deśe niyojayet || 

trayaṅgalakṣaṇaṃ cedaṃ namaskārasya saṃkhyayā | ekāṅgaṃ tu 
namaskāraṃ śirasaiva kṛtaṃ bhavet || 

trīṇi pañca ca saptāpi nava dvādaśa saṃkhyayā | bhaktyā kṛtvā 
namaskāraṃ śivasyāgre tato naraḥ || 

p. 591) 

aśvamedhasya sadṛśaṃ labhate phalamuttamam |

sakṛdbhaktyā yo liṅgaṃ praṇamennaraḥ ||

p. 593) 

svāyambhuve - 

āsannaprasave(vā?) nārī tailapūrṇaṃ yathā ghaṭam | 
svahanti(ntī?) śanakairyāti tathā kuryātpradakṣiṇam || 

asya kālaphalaṃ tatraiva proktam | - 

pūrvāhne vyādhināśaḥ syānmadhyāhne vāñchitārthadam | 
sāyāhne sarvapāpaghnamardhayāme vimuktidam || 

ajite - 

aṣṭamyāṃ ca caturdaśyāṃ pañcadaśyāṃ trijanmani | 
ārdrāyāmarkavāre ca karaṇe vṛṣṭisaṃjñake || 

ayane viṣuve caiva grahaṇe candrasūryayoḥ | saṃkrame cotsave caiva 
snātvā kṛtvā pradakṣiṇam || 

praṇamedyaśśivaṃ bhaktyā sarvānkāmānavāpya saḥ | 
labhecchataguṇaṃ puṇyaṃ kevalānnātra saṃśayaḥ || 

p. 594) 

evamukteṣu kāleṣu bhaktiśraddhāsamanvitaḥ | sūryodayaṃ 
samārabhya yāvadastaṃ ravirbhavet || 

tāvatpradakṣiṇaṃ kṛtvā sarvānkāmānavāpnuyāt | 
athāhorātrakaṃ vāpi kāleṣveteṣu yonaraḥ || 

pradakṣiṇaṃ prakurvīta so'nantaphalamaśnute | 
dūramārgātsamāgamya phalāśārahito'pi yaḥ | kṛtvā 
pradakṣinaṃ bhaktyā praṇamecchivamavyayam || 

tasya mārgasamudbhūtā yā gatiścāpi keśava | pradaskṣiṇaphalaṃ 
nṝṇāṃ dadātyeva na saṃśayaḥ || 

pravāsī pathi kṛtvā tu śivaliṅgaṃ pradaksiṇam | namaskṛtvā tu 
pūrvoktān phalaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet || 

p. 595) 

yogaje - 

utsave balikāle tu yathā kurvanpradakṣiṇam | pade pade'śvamedhasya 
phalamāpnoti mānavaḥ || 

ajite - 

garbhagehecca(ca?) tasyoktaṃ dīkṣitasyaiva bāhyake | pradakṣiṇaṃ 
nṛṇāṃ noktamantarmaṇḍalake sadā || 

antarhāraṃ(re?) kṛtaṃ proktaṃ phalamekapradakṣiṇam | triguṇaṃ 
madhyahāre tu maryādāyāṃ caturguṇam || 

mahāmaryādikaṃ caiva kṛtaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet | kṣetrāvadhi 
kṛtaṃ bāhye bhavecchataguṇaṃ nṛṇām || 

svāyambhuve - 

daśāśvamedhajaṃ puṇyaṃ garbhagehe pradakṣiṇam | 
tasmāddaśaguṇaṃ puṇyamantarmaṇḍaladeśake || 

p. 596) 

antarhārādisāleṣu tattaddaśaguṇaṃ bhavet | savyāpasavyamārgeṇa 
pūjānte tu pradakṣiṇam || 

atha pradakṣiṇasaṃkhyā - 

kṛtvā pradakṣiṇaṃ bhaktyā śivasyaikaṃ samāhitaḥ | 
aśvamedhasya yajñasya sukhena labhate phalam || 

ekaviṃśatisaṃkhyātamuttamaṃ tu pradakṣiṇam | tithi saṃkhyā 
madhyamasyāpyadhamaṃ munisaṃkhyayā || 

pañcasaṃkhyākṛtaṃ yattadadhame madhyamaṃ bhavet | 
adhamādhamamityuktaṃ guṇasaṃkhyākṛtaṃ tu yat || 

tasmādapyavaraṃ proktaṃ kṛtamekaṃ pradakṣiṇam | 

svāyambhuve - 

ekadaṇḍanamaskāramekam cāpi pradakṣiṇam | * * * * * * * * * * * *
