p. 597) 

śataṃ sahasramayutaṃ niyutaṃ prayutaṃ tu vā | koṭiṃ vā 
kārayediṣṭasiddhyarthaṃ tu pradakṣiṇam || 

liṅgaviśeṣaphalaṃ nirūpyate - 

mānuṣasya tu liṅgasya phalamuktaṃ pradakṣiṇam | namaskāreṇa 
tasmādvai dviguṇaṃ cārṣake bhavet || 

triguṇaṃ daivike liṅge phalaṃ gāṇeśvere ca tat | caturguṇaṃ 
samuddiṣṭaṃ phalaṃ pañcaguṇaṃ bhavet || 

svayaṃbhūtasya liṅgasya tathā saṃsparśanaiśśanaiḥ | 

tatpradakṣiṇaṃ caturvidham savyam apasavyam savyāpasavyam 
aṅgapradakṣiṇaṃ ceti | tathā aṃśumati - 

pradakṣiṇaprakārasya lakṣaṇaṃ śṛṇu suvrata | savyaṃ tu 
prathamaṃ proktamapasavyaṃ dvitīyakam || 

p. 598) 

savyāpasavyamaparaṃ svatastvaṅgapradakṣiṇam | savyaṃ? 
pradakṣiṇaṃ proktamapasavyaṃ pradakṣiṇam || 

ajite - 

savyāpa savyassoma sūtrālaṅghanataḥ kṛtam | 
savyāpasavyamityuktaṃ dīkṣitānāṃ tu tadbhavet | 
aṅgapradakṣiṇaṃ yattu sarvāṅgaiḥ parikalpitaḥ | 

somasūtrasvarūpam - 

aṃśumati nirūpitam - 

prāsādasya tu vistāraṃ tanmānaṃ somasūtrakam | liṅgamūlasya 
pārśve tu yojayetsaumyadeśi(śa?)ke || 

yāvatsūtrāntamevaṃ syādvarjayettu pradakṣiṇam | 

saṃkaṭe prāsādārdhasamaṃ vā parihṛtya kartavyam | 

p. 599) 

prāsādārdhasamaṃ vāpi prāsādasamayetyaktatvāt || 

kāraṇe - 

śambhoḥ pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt somasūtraṃ na laṅghayet | 
laṅghanādekamekaṃ syādalaṅghedyayutatrayam || 

alaṅghyapradakṣiṇaṃ savyāpasavyam | vilaṅghyapradakṣiṇaṃ 
savyādisavyādimekamekam | savyāpasavye ekamayutatrayaṃ 
bhavedityarthaḥ || 

yāmnacchāyāmapi somasūtravanna laṅghayet | 

kāraṇe - 

mahāyāmasya sūtreṇa chāyāṃ caiva na laṅghayet || 

chāyādhastātprakartavyaṃ vimāne tu pradakṣiṇam | 

p. 600) 

tathānyatra - 

chāyātilaṅghane prāpte kathaṃ kuryātpradakṣiṇam | ante 
tryaṃśaṃ vibhādhādau saṃvaredyaṃśake budhaḥ || 

tathā - 

liṅgacchāyāṃ tu pīṭhaṃ ca prāsādaṃ pratimāṃ tu vā | utsave 
balikāle ca tathaiva gurusevite || 

pradakṣiṇakriyākāle chāyādoṣo na vidyate kutracidapasavyaṃ 
niṣiddham 

somasūtradvayaṃ yatra yatra vā viṣṇumandiram | apasavyaṃ na 
kurvīta kuryādeva pradakṣinam || 

savyādisvarūpamaṃśumattantre spaṣṭamuktam 

indrādindraparyantaṃ savyaṃ proktaṃ pradakṣiṇam | 
yathāsavyaprakāreṇa apasavyaṃ samācaret || 

savyāpasavyamārgeṇa pradakṣiṇamataḥ śṛṇu | 

p. 601) 

yathā - 

vṛṣādi caṇḍanāthaṃ ca vṛṣabhaṃ somasūtrakam | 
punarvṛṣabhaṃ caṇḍanāthaṃ nāladeśasya pārśvataḥ || 

punaḥ prayāyāścaṇḍaṃ ca tathaiva vṛṣapṛṣṭhakam | evaṃ 
savyāpasavyaṃ tu pradakṣiṇamihocyate || 

evaṃ savyāpasavyapradakṣiṇaṃ kṛrvanvṛṣaṃ caṇḍānujñaiva? 
pradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāt | 

tathācāgamaḥ - 

ādyantaṃ vṛṣadevasya catussthānamudāhṛtam | 
tricaṇḍasthānasaṃyuktaṃ pakṣādyāṃ somasūtrakam || 

ātmapradakṣiṇaṃ kuryāttattatsthānaika saṃkhyayā | tathaiva 
navasaṃkhyā ca evamekaṃ vidhīyate || 

p. 602) 

aṅgapradakṣiṇa svarūpamajine pradarśitam | - 

aṅgapradakṣiṇaṃ kurvan kṛtvā bhūmau nijāṃ tanum | 
daṇḍavadbāhuyugmena saha badhvā kṛtāñjaliḥ || 

kṛtvā daṇḍanamaskāraṃ śivāgre tu sakṛnnaraḥ | pradakṣiṇaṃ 
tathā kuryādekaṃ vā trayameva vā || 

pañca vā sapta vā caiva sarvapāpanivṛttaye | kṛtvā punastathāgre 
tu namaskāraṃ ca bhaktitaḥ || 

utthāya praṇatiṃ bhūyo bhūyaḥ kṛtvā samāpayet | 

aṃśumati - 

karāgrau bandhayitvā tu pādau svastikamācaret | nemivadbhramaṇaṃ 
kṛtvā prāsādātparitastathā || 

aṅgapradakṣiṇaṃ proktamevamevaṃ pradakṣiṇam | pradakṣiṇaṃ 
sahasraṃ syādekamekaṃ pradakṣiṇam || 

p. 603) 

evamanyānyapi ca 
padmasiṃhaśṛṅkhalanāgapradakṣiṇādya(nyaṃ?)śumati 
draṣṭavyāni || 

svāyambhuve - 

apasavyaṃ yatīnāṃ tu savyaṃ tu brahmacāriṇām | 
savyāpasavyamārgeṇa vānaprasthagṛhasthayoḥ || 

svāyambhuve - 

garbhagehe'pi savyāpasavyavidhirutta(ktaḥ?)maḥ | 

yathā - 

garbhe pradakṣiṇaṃ kiṃ tu pūjakasya vidhīyate | tatrāpi 
devadevasya chāyānirmālyalaṅghanam || 

somasūtraṃ ca tatkāle parihṛtya samācaret | savyāpasavyamārgeṇa 
kartavyaṃ tatpradakṣiṇam || 

kālottare - 

niyamadvayamuktam | 

savyāpasavyamārgeṇa antarmaṇḍalake kuru | bāhyaprākāradeśe tu 
savyamārgaṃ pradakṣiṇam || 

p. 604) 

atra pūrvārdhena savyāpasavyadeśaniyama uktaḥ | aparārdhena 
bāhyadeśasya savyaniyama uktaḥ | 

yatra yatrāvṛtau kiṃ tu balipīṭhavṛṣasthitiḥ | tatra 
tatrāvṛtau kuryāttatpradhānaṃ pradakṣiṇam || 

yatra pradakṣiṇaṃ tatra balipīṭhaṃ vṛṣauravet | tadbāhyasthau 
samādhṛtya kṛtī kuryātpradakṣiṇam | deveśavṛṣayorvāpi 
balipīṭheśayostu vā || 

antarā gamanaṃ kuryāttattadāvaraṇe sudhīḥ | 

itthaṃ bhūtaṃ pradakṣiṇaṃ nivṛttya pūrvavacchivāgre namaskṛtya 
uttthāya bhūyo bhūyaḥ praṇamya samāpayet || 

p. 605) 

ajite - 

kṛtvā punastathāgre tu namaskāraṃ ca bhaktitaḥ | utthāya 
praṇatiṃ bhūyo bhūyaḥ kṛtvā samāpayet || 

tadanu dvitīyāvaraṇapālakau dhyānapūrvaṃ praṇamya prārthayet || 
tathā -brahmāṇḍe - 

dvitīyāvaraṇadvārapālakau gaṇanāyakau | natvābhyarcyāntaḥ 
pravisetstutvaiva śivabhaktimān || 

dhyānam - 

bibhrāṇau 
nijabāhubhirvaragadābhitarjanīmudrakāścaṇḍāṃśupratimaṣau 
gaṇavarau rudrālayadvārapau | koṭīrāṅgadahāranūpura 
kaṭisūtravitāhīśvarau vande'haṃ praṇatārtināśanakarau 
gaurīśasevāptayoḥ(ye?) || 

p. 606) 

tadanu dhyānapūrvaṃ gāṇapatyaṃ nandikeśvaraṃ praṇamya 
prārthayet | 

bibhrāṇaṃ paraśuṃ mṛgaṃ karatalairīśapraṇāmāñjalim 
bhasmoddhūlanapāṇḍaraṃ śaśikalāgaṅgākapardojjvalam | 
paryāyaṃ(ya?) tripurāntakaṃ pramathapaśreṣṭhaṃ 
gaṇendrairvṛtaṃ brahmendrācyuta vanditāṅghrikamalaṃ 
śrīnandikeśaṃ bhaje || 

atra prārthanā - 

antaḥ praveśe śivadarśane ca śivārcane tatphalasiddhilābhe | 
dehyabhyanujñāṃ bhagavanprasannastvatpādapadma praṇatāyamahyam || 

prārthayitveti taṃ paścādbhāvayitvā śilādijam | 

evaṃ nandikeśvaramanujñāpyāntaḥ praviśya prathamaṃ vighnarājaṃ 
namaskuryāt | 

p. 607) 

vighnarājaṃ namaskuryātstotramuccārayanbhuvi | vikīrya pādayoḥ 
puṣpaṃ mastakāhatimuṣṭikam | stuvanvahyasthitiṃ? 
kuryātkarṇāyorarpitāṅgulim || 

tato maṇḍapāntaḥ praviśya devadevaṃ namaskṛtya stotraṃ kuryāt 
|| tathā - 

aṃśumati - 

maṇḍapāntaḥ praviśyātha śivāgre tu viśeṣataḥ | devadevaṃ 
namaskṛtya stotraṃ caiva samuccaran || 

mastake hṛdaye caivata(tva?)ñjaliṃ caiva vinyaset | 

namaskāramudrā suprabhede darśitā | tathā 

satatāṅgulisaṃlagnau hastau hṛddeśamāśritau | sarveṣāṃ 
bandhanaṃ kāryaṃ vāmāṅguṣṭhanipīḍitam || 

namaskāre vijñeyā sarvakarmaṇi kārayet | 

p. 608) 

tattadvarṇāśramānuguṇaṃ stotraṃ kāmike pradarśitam | 

vedādhyayanamanyadvā stotrapāṭhādikaṃ tu vā | uta 
drāviḍabhāṣādyaṃ gānanu(nṛ?)ttayutaṃ tu vā || 

aṣṭādaśākhyabhāṣotthaṃ gānaṃ vā parikalpayet | 

viprādīnāṃ yathācitaṃ puṣpāñjalipradānamaṃśumati 
pradarśitam | - 

deśikena prakartavyā garbhagehe praveśanam | puṣpeṇaivaṃ 
sambhyarcya śivamantramanusmaret || 

viprāṇāṃ ca praveśaṃ ca ardhamaṇḍapamāviśet | kṣatriyasya 
viśeṣeṇa puṣpāñjali krameṇa tu || 

maṇḍapaṃ tu praviśyātha mukhadvāre sthitirbhavet | ātmatattvayaṃ 
caiva arcayedgurumārgataḥ || 

p. 609) 

vaiśyāścaiva tu śūdrāśca vṛṣāgre vṛṣapṛṣṭhake | 
gomayālepanaṃ kṛtvā caturaśrasamanvitam || 

arcayenmūlamantreṇa gurumārgeṇa kārayet | 

śaive - 

vastraṃ dhanaṃ bhūmidāsī divyānyābharaṇāni ca | 
dhūpadīpopahārādyaṃ mahānivedyameva ca || 

dadyāddevāya devyai ca yathāśaktyānusārataḥ | yatkiñcidapi 
devāśvatvaṇumātraṃ tu vānaraḥ || 

dattvaiva praṇameddevānnānyathā śambhuvallabhaḥ | dhanādirahito 
devaṃ patrapuṣpaphalādikam || 

dattvaiva praṇameddevamanyathā tu kadācana | devālayasthaṃ 
nirmālaṃ(lyaṃ?) tṛṇādyaṃ vā malādikam || 

p. 610) 

viśodhya vāpi deveśaṃ praṇamennānyathā kvacit | sarvābhāve 
vāksahāyamapi kṛtvā nameddharam || 

śambhu bhakto nānyathā syāditi vedānuśāsanam | evaṃ kṛtvā 
yathāśakti paśyeddevaṃ ca bhaktitaḥ || 

niḥśvāse - 

indunibhapañcamukhahastadaśayuktaṃ śūladharaṭaṅkamasi 
vajramabhayaṃ syāt | pāśa varadāṅkuśamaṇirdahana 
vāmeśvetakamalāsanasadāśivasvarūpam || 

upānaḥ pādukaṃ caiva jagaruṃ(tiṃ?) jaghanameva ca | kumudaṃ 
kukṣirityāhu bāhubhyāṃ kampakena ca || 

kaṇṭhaṃ karṇamiti jñeyamadhare adharaṃ smṛtam | 

p. 611) 

mahāpaṭṭilalāṭaṃ syātkeśāntaṃ ghṛtavāriṇā | evaṃ 
dhyātvā namaskṛtya tatpuṣpaṃ varjayettataḥ || 

liṅgasya darśanaṃ prātarnaiśamaṃhye vyapohyati | ājanma 
janmamadhyāhne [(ayamaṅkaḥ mātṛkāyāṃ dattaḥ?) sāyāhne 
saptajanmani || 

evaṃ yaḥ kurute martthaṃ(rtyaḥ?) so'nte sāyujyamāpnuyāt | 

liṅgapurāṇe ca 

yaḥ prātardevadeveśaṃ śivaliṅgasvarūpiṇam | paśva?(śyan?) 
śa(sa?) yāti sarvasmāt(da?) adhikāṃ gatimeva vai || 

madhyāhne] ca mahādevaṃ dṛṣṭvā yajñaphalaṃ bhavet | sāyāhne 
sarvayajñānāṃ phalaṃ prāpya vimucyate || 

ardhamāsakṛtaṃ pāpaṃ dṛṣṭvā parvaṇi santyajet | 
aṣṭamyāmaṣṭadivasaiḥ kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tyajennaraḥ || 

p. 612) 

pradoṣe śaṅkaraṃ dṛṣṭvā sarvapāpaiḥ pramucyate | 
mānasairvācikaiḥ pāpaiḥ kāyikaiśca mahattaraiḥ || 

tathopapātakaiścaiva pātakaissaṃpramucyate | saṃkrame 
devamīśānaṃ dṛṣṭvā liṅgākṛtiṃ vibhum || 

māsena vai kṛtaṃ pāpaṃ tyaktvā yāti śivaṃ padam | ayane 
cārdhamāse ca dakṣiṇe cottare tathā || 

viṣuve caiva taṃ dṛṣṭvā prayāti paramaṃ padam | 
nṛttamūrtyādidevānāṃ tattaddhyānapurassaram || 

tattatpuṣpopahāreṇa kuryāttu praṇatiṃ tataḥ | 

brahmāṇḍe va(ca?) purāṇe 

tatrasthaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtiṃ somāskandeśvaraṃ śivam | 
vṛṣārūḍhaṃ candracūḍaṃ namaskuryānnaṭeśvaram || 

p. 613) 

mātṛsaptāpi durgāṃ ca vaṭukaṃ praṇamedravim | 

tathā - 

apasmāratadūrdhve tu svasthitaṃ savyapādakam | kuñcitaṃ 
vāmapādaṃ tu savye ḍamarukaṃ tathā || 

vāme cāgnidharaṃ devaṃ jaṭānāmaṣṭa sapta ca | savye 
gaṅgādharaṃ devaṃ vāmarūpendusamanvitam || 

sarvāvayavasaṃpūrṇaṃ sarvālaṃkāraśobhitam | 
japākusumasaṃkāśameva tāṇḍavavigraham || 

ekamukhavedakara vidrumasvarūpaṃ 
kṛṣṇamṛgaṭaṅkavaradābhayacatuṣkam | locana 
tṛtīyavaravastraparidhānaṃ candradhṛtamauli sahagauri 
sahasūna(nu?)m || 

p. 614) 

padmāsanaṃ raktatanuṃ dvinetraṃ śvetābjayuktāṃsakahastayugmam | 
raktambarālepanamālya bhūṣyaṃ sthitaṃ 
sahāṅgaiśśivasūryamīḍe || 

rauravottare - 

vighneśaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtiṃ viṣṇumūrtiṃ tathaiva ca | 
brahmamūrtiṃ ca durgāṃ ca pañcamūrtiṃ tu vandayet || 

vaṭaviṭapisamīpe bhūmibhāge niṣaṇṇaḥ sakalamunijanānāṃ 
jñānadatānyamārāt | tribhuvanagurumīśaṃ dakṣiṇāmūrtidevaṃ 
jananamaraṇaduḥkhacchedadakṣaṃ namāmi || 

brāhmī maheśvarī caiva kaumārī vaiṣṇavī tathā | vārāhī 
caiva māhendrī cāmuṇḍā saptamātaraḥ || 

p. 615) 

vīravighnasthitā etāḥ praṇatārtibhayāvahāḥ | 
śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ gajavaktraṃ caturbhujam || 

lambodharaṃ triṇayanaṃ pāśāṅkuśadharaṃ param | 
varadābhayahastaṃ ca calatkarṇaṃ sadā smaret || 

pītaṃ śaktiṃ ca ghaṇṭāṃ dhvajamatha kamalaṃ kukkuṭaṃ 
prā?sadaṇḍau bibhrāṇā vāyubhāge 
varamabhayadhanurbāṇaṭaṅkāṃśca hastaiḥ | ṣaḍvaktraṃ 
dvādaśākṣaṃ śikhini śarabhayaṃ pāṇibhirvā 
caturbhirvaktraikyaṃ śaktyabhibhyāṃ bhajatarayutaṃ kukkuṭaṃ 
cādadhānam || 

kālavarṇa caturbhujayuktaṃ śūlaḍuṇḍubhaṭaṅkakapālam | 
maulivahnitriṇetrasangnaṃ kṣetrapālamimaṃ kamalastham || 

p. 616) 

brahmāṇḍopapurāṇe - viśeṣa uktaḥ 

utkarṣamīśasya paṭhetstavaṃ ca stotrāvasāne graha bhasma 
śambhoḥ | ādāya dhṛtvā punarevamekaṃ pradakṣiṇānte kila 
cametya? || 

praṇamya cābhyarcya kṛtastutissan tristāḍayitvā karakaṃ kareṇa | 
saṃprārthya sevāphalamīśvarasya vṛṣasya 
śṛṅgāntaramīśaliṅgam || 

atha dhvaniścaṇḍeśvaraṃ sevepta - 

dvinetraṃ dvibhujaṃ caiva śuklavastrasudhāriṇam | 
jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktaṃ śaśyardhakṛtaśekharam || 

p. 617) 

lambitaṃ dakṣiṇaṃ pādaṃ śayanaṃ vāmapādakam | jānūrdhvo 
vāmapādaṃ tu savyahastaṃ tu ṭaṅkadhṛk || 

pīṭhādi caṇḍaparyantaṃ sevāṃ kuryāttu diśikam | 

brahmāṇḍe - 

api sakalatīrthe rāśritāṇḍadvayodya- dbhavabhayamanutrāhi 
māndriṣṭaliṅgam | 

iti saṃprārthya tatpārśve saṃniviṣṭa udaṅmukhaḥ | 
pañcākṣarajapaṃ kṛtvā dhyānenaiva sadāśivam || 

evaṃ devyālayamapi seveta - 

kāmike - 

devyālaye'pi kartavyaṃ pūrvoktena vidhānataḥ | siṃho vā 
vṛṣabho vāpi vāhanaḥ parikīrtitaḥ || 

p. 618) 

īśvarābhimukhaṃ devamāsīnaṃ vṛṣanāyakam | prārthayitvā tu 
tatpaścāt(d) bhāvayeddvāranāyikau || 

devyāstu dvārapālau tu khaḍgakheṭakahastakai | 
jaṭāmakuṭasaṃyuktau raktaśyāmavibhūṣaṇau || 

khaḍgakheṭakadhāriṇyau dvibhujau vā caturbhujau | 
devyoktadvārapālānhā sarvābhīṣṭaphalapradān || 

tato devaṃ namaskṛtya stotraṃ caiva samuccaran | 

padmākṣamālābhaya ūruhastāṃ śyāmāṅganetratrayasundarāḍhyām 
| kirīṭavaktrendu jaṭāpya ca cūḍyā 
śrīkāmakoṣṭhaśśivaśaktimīḍe || 

p. 619) 

digdevāsudevyāstu rūpabhedāḥ prakīrtitāḥ | śivāyatanadevānvā 
gaṇa ṣaṇmukho'pi vā || 

yāmni digdevatā sthāpyāstaddevīrūpavigrahaḥ | 
vāmādyāśśaktayo vāpi parivārā vyavasthitāḥ || 

nirmālyadhāriṇīṃ devīṃ yamanīmīśagocarām | 

kāraṇe - 

bhujadvayadvinetraṃ ca śyāmalā śubhameva ca | vāmahaste tu 
jānūrdhve savyahaste nu puṣpadhṛk || 

athavāñjalitaṃ hasta dvihaste puṣpadhārayet | caṇḍeśīṃ 
yamanīṃ caiva dhyāyeduktakrameṇa tu || 

kāmike - 

sā devī śyāmalā hastadvayākhilavibhūṣitā | yamanītyākhyayā 
khyātā dhṛtanīlotpalāvarā || 

p. 620) 

itthaṃ pīṭhādicaṇḍāntaṃ namaskṛtya sannidhiṃ kṛtvā aparādha 
nivṛttaye yathā śakti mūlamantraṃ japet | devasa praveśe ca 
sthitau devasya sannidhau punaśca nirgame pṛṣṭhīkaraṇaṃ śubhaṃ 
śivanandinoḥ | 

bhuktirnidrā hyadhītiścābhicārakriyā tataḥ | 
gopurapratimāchāyā laṅghanasparśane tathā || 

nirmālyalaṅghanasparśau devapīṭhāntarā gatiḥ | 
pramadābhūṣaṇaṃ tatra sparśanaṃ rāganīkṣaṇam || 

ekahastapramāṇaṃ ca ekaṃ cāpi pradakṣiṇam | akāladarśanaṃ 
caiva dyathā saṃbhāṣaṇaṃ tathā || 

asatyabhāṣaṇaṃ tadvadasahya śravaṇaṃ tathā | udvāsana 
sthitiścāpi sannidhāvāsanaṃ param || 

p. 621) 

vṛthā hāso vṛthā gānaṃ vṛthāgānaśrutistathā | devadravye 
rāgabuddhistathā vigrahavispṛśi || 

asatāṃ bhāṣaṇaṃ tadvatsatāṃ vai bhū(dū?)ṣaṇaṃ tathā | 
sottarīyanamaskāra pradakṣiṇakṛtī ubhe || 

devasya vāmabhāge tu namaskārakriyā japaḥ | durdevatopāsanaṃ 
cetyaparādhāḥ pari(pra?)kīrtitāḥ || 

ajñānataḥ kṛtānetān sahate karuṇānidhiḥ |

vidhāyoparamedūrdhvaṃ vada(yāvadvi?)rvighnairnirudhyate || 

p. 647) 

yatra bīja ivārūḍho mahāmantrārthapādapaḥ | āhā(bhā?)ti 
mūlasūtraṃ tadathaśabdādyalaṃkṛtam || 

tasyādde(de?)śakamanyat? syātsaṃkhyāsaṃjñāti(di?) vācakam | 
tallakṣaṇasvarūpoktistadanyo bhaṣyasaṃrahaḥ (bhāṣya 
saṃgrahaḥ?) || 

brūte evaṃ dhoyī totā pubhau hatakalmaṣau | loke prāpya yaśo 
dīptaṃ viṃśetāṃ dharmaśāsanam || 

tadūrdhvadineṣu adhvākanirādhāra pramāditāṃ sā kṣatradhṛk 
vyāpaka pālagālavapaureyānumiti pāvakām | api 
cārayināmakāramiṣva surakṣita śobhana sūtra pūrvoktapīṭha na 
yantropari nidhāya deśikaḥ svayaṃ prāṃbhimukhovāsinaḥ 
kṛntārthaṃ kṛṣyā 

p. 648) 

saṃmukhamavasthāpyāṃ(pya?) bho-ityāmantrya paṭhetyādiśya 
saṃskṛta prākṛtāditattaddeśocita 
bhāṣānurūpaiśśiṣyānurūpairvākyaiḥ para 
madantarāladivyādivyarūpa ṣaṭprakāra saṃbandhābhidheya 
prayojanavarṇanapurassaraṃ vyākhyāmupakramya kāmikādyūrdhve 
sroto'ṣṭaṃ sitāṅgāni dakṣiṇasrotasvame māhātyuttara 
srautālādiprācya srotaścaṇḍāpidharādi paścimasrotorūpāṇi 
pañcasrotāṃsi pratisroto'ṣṭavidhāni vai mantreśvaragaṇādi 
vyāṣagauhyayoginī kālakākhyāni anusrotaṃ vivasva gurukramaṃ ca 
śāstrapravṛttihetuṃ vadet | 

p. 649) 

taduktaṃ kāmike - 

gomayāliptabhūbhāge raṅgarekhāsamanvite | 
prakīrṇapuṣpaprakaraiḥ sudhūpena ca dhūpite || 

jvaladdīpa samopeta śuddhaśaivasamāvṛte | na samīpe paśūnāṃ tu 
na kuryādadhyayanaṃ budhaḥ || 

adhyāpayecchanaiśśiṣyān śivabhaktiṃ prabodhayet 
śivavidyānusāreṇa vidyādānaṃ taducyate | 

* * * * * * * * * * * * saṃskṛtairapi || 

atra laukikaśabdaiśca yaśśiṣyamanurūpataḥ | 
deśabhāṣādyupāyaiśca tathā bhūtāgamairapi || 

pradeśavartibhiḥ sarvadeśasthaṃ bodhayedbudhaḥ | 

tathā mṛgendre - 

p. 650) 

vyākuryācchiva bhaktebhyastatrārthaṃ gatamānasaḥ | ya 
tonyāyavartibhyaḥ pālayangurusantatim || 

nā jīrṇāmyarasodgāre na ca viṇmutrabādhane | na 
cchadyānnātisāre ca nāsnātaḥ kṛtamaithunaḥ | pūte mahītale 
sthitvā paśuśravaṇavarjite || 

parityantasthitiṃ ceṣṭvā śivaṃ praṇavavigraham | gaṇādhyakṣaṃ 
guruṃ caiva kārakaṃ ca kriyāśrayam || 

pustakaṃ guptasatsūtraṃ vidhāyopari kasyacit | brūyādaṅga 
paṭhastveti kṛtārthaṃ prāgudaṅmukhaḥ || 

prārabheta gururvyākhyāmambarāryoktipūrvakam | 

atra saṃbandhalakṣaṇam - 

siddhāntasārāvalyām - 

p. 651) 

saṃbandhāśca pareṇunātha śivayorananda somomahān 
somāddevagatontarālavidito divyāmaniṣvāgamaḥ | 

anyatra pañcamaprakāra uktaḥ | tadyathā - 

viromahāstathā divyo divyādivyastathaiva ca | adivyaśceti pañcaite 
saṃbandhāḥ parikīrtitāḥ || 

addhvālaṃkādīṃ strayodaśavidhāśśiṣyān samyak parijñāya

bodhayet | tathā mataṅge - 

adhvālako nirādhāraḥ pramādī tamasoparaḥ | 
kṣatradhṛkcyāvakaściva pālako gālavastathā || 

p. 652) 

paurayo numanuścaiva pāvako puśca kīrtitāḥ | vicāradhīśca 
vijñeyāśśiṣyāssanti trayodaśa || 

sarvatantrārthamārge'sminsaṃpraviśyāvagāhanaḥ | tataścaitaiśca 
saṃbandhāt(d?) asaṃbandhivacoktiṣu || 

praviśyādālako yo'bhiproktassodhvālakaḥ śiśuḥ | 
gṛhītasyāpyathārthasya sthāturnāstimavamāśrayaḥ || 

prokto'pyādhāradaurbalyānnirādhāro mahāmuneḥ | notsaheta 
tvamālasya śrotuścaiva tu niścayāt || 

pramādī socyate tantre śiṣyaḥ kāraṇacocite | prayatnādapi 
yuktassroturnidrāvaśassadā tiṣṭhate || 

pūrṇamāno'pi sa śiṣya stāmaso mataḥ | ādāvante ca nipuṇo yo 
jñātvā śāstramaiśvaram || 

p. 653) 

īśchatramanuprāpya vidhāyopari kurvate | sa jñeyaḥ kṣatadṛk 
śiṣyaḥ pañcamaṃ śiṣyasantatau || 

vratāntavanmano'nyatprāk pracyutārthaṃ svato'nyataḥ | 
pra(pṛ?)cchedyaścāvakaḥ proktaḥ śiṣyaḥ śrāvakavartmani || 

tūṣṇīṃ nityaṃ vijāno'sau na jānunnapi saṃśayam | na pṛcchet 
svalpamapyarthaṃ pāti vāgitvamātmanaḥ || 

sa pālaka iti prokta svavartmaparirakṣaṇāt | avadhārya gurossamyak 
śāstramāmnāyadarśanāt || 

na madā kiñcidapyasmācchritaṃ syādgālavaśśiśuḥ | itaścetaśca 
purato vyākhyākāle kṛtodyamī || 

utkṛṣṭavāk susāntaiddhyāt guroḥ paureyakastu saḥ | pṛṣṭau 
vāpyathavā pṛṣṭau pracchakā cāryayorapi || 

p. 654) 

ajānannapyathaivaṃ syād brūyādanumatassadā | yathoktaṃ guruṇā 
sarvaṃ suvicāryāgnivatsadā || 

grasate ṛjumārgeṇa sa śiṣyaḥ pāvako mataḥ | ni(nī?)yate yena 
mārgeṇa yatra yatra vicārataḥ ||

samyak gṛhṇātyarthān? yatssadā ||

samākhyātā guroratra mahātmanaḥ ||

samarthāścāvadhāritum || 

vyākhyā kartavya guruṇā sadā yo padhāravituṃ muktaḥ 
padoktividhidarśanāt | 

p. 655)

yatra koṭidvayapadakramāt ||

taddeyaṃ padaṃ sūtramathāpi vā ||

samīpastho vākyena munipuṅgavāḥ || 

tatrātthitena kartavyedhyeṣaṇāsu niścayaḥ | 

ṣaṭsahasrikāyām -

yuktyā prakartavyaṃ na svābhiprāyato diśet || 

p. 656)

iti ||

p. 657)

svahānudarśane | 

candrārkabimbasthāḥ kīlakākṛtyutpātāḥ 

p. 658)

p. 659)

p. 660)

iti ||

p. 662) 

śivadharmottare -

siddhāntaḥ sevyate sadbhiśśaktipātapavitritaiḥ |

p. 663) 

uttamaṃ sarvaśāstrāṇāṃ śivaśāstrārthaṃ guha | guhāt(hyād?)

caiva nānyaśāstraratāya ca ||

mataṅge - 

p. 664) 

samāptau tu guroḥ pādau praṇamya virametsudhīḥ | kṛtvā

gurūttā?rayecchiṣyānasmātsaṃsāra sāgarāt || 

yaścaivaṃ śṛṇuyādbhaktaḥ śivārpitamanāḥ sadā | sa yogyo 
gurusantānamārgamatra pralāpitum ||

vā śivajñānasya yaḥ paṭhet || 

p. 665) 

anyebhyaḥ śrāvakebhyaśca tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu | tripudattā

ślokārthamīśvara jñānātpaṭhaṃstatphalamāpnuyāt iti |

yāvadakṣarasaṃjñānaṃ śivajñānasya pustake |

putra dharmasa(su?)hṛjjanaiḥ? | sāntaḥ

p. 666) 

yat śrīmatpustakavaramalekhyaṃ vinivedayet | śivajñānābhiyuktāya 
tasya puṇyaphalaṃ śṛṇu || 

yāvattatrapatrasaṃkhyānaṃ sañcaye'tīva śobhane | 
tāvadyugasahasrāṇi śivaloke sa pūjyate || 

yaḥ patramādyasūtrādyaṃ maṣībhājinalekhinīm | 
śaraśantrāsanādyaṅgaṃ śaryāṃ? tat(d?) bhaktavetanam ||

mahābhogaiśśivaloke mahīyate ||

proktaṃ śivaśāstraviśāradaiḥ || 

p. 667) 

śivapustakanikṣepa snānanirmāṇamāsta(sti?)kaiḥ | śivabhaktiriti 
proktā śivadṛṣṭaviśāradaiḥ || 

uktaṃ ca puṇḍarīkapuramāhātmye - 

śivaśāstraṃ vilikhitaṃ(daṃ?) yaḥ patrāṇi prayacchati | dvijāya

rakṣāṃ vā dattvā śivapuraṃ vrajet ||

prayacchati paścātsa śivasāyujyamāpnuyāt || 

ityātmārthapūjāpaddhatyāṃ 

siddhāntaśravaṇavidhiḥ ||

p. 668) 

|| mādhyāhnika pūjā vidhiḥ || 

atha madhyāhnasamaye snānādikaṃ vidhāya pūrvavacchivaṃ vistareṇa

tathāgamaḥ - 

muhūrtaiḥ pañcabhiryāgaiḥ kṛtvā madhyāhnikavidhim | 

ṣaṭsahasre - 

prātarmadhyāhnasamaye tathā cāstamite ravau | pūjayetparayā 
bhaktyā aṣṭapuṣpavidhānataḥ || 

daridrāṇāmanāthānāṃ bālastrībāliśeṣu ca | bhogināṃ 
vyādhijuṣṭānāṃ tathādhyayananāpatātmanām? 
śivaśāstrānuraktānāṃ nānnāsiddhi? hitātmanām | 

p. 669) 

anuraktaviraktānāṃ kathitāṣṭapuṣpikā | 

atra kramaḥ - ācamya sakalīkṛtya arghyāṇi dattvā devamabhiṣicya 
pīṭhe vinyasya ekena puṣpeṇāsanaṃ pareṇa mūrtiṃ pañcabhiḥ 
puṣpairnetraṃ vinā pañcāṅgāni prakalpya mūlena saṃpūjya 
yathāśaktiviśeṣopacārān vidadhyāt | 

tatraiva āsane prathamaṃ puṣpaṃ dātayaṃ śuddhacetasā 
mūrtidānaṃ hṛdā kāryaṃ puṣpeṇaivāpareṇa tu || 
pañcapuṣpapradānena pañcāṅgāni prakalpayet | śivaṃ 
tathāṣṭamenaiva kathitaṃ cāṣṭapuṣpikā || 

p. 670) 

tathā āsanamūrtimūlāṅganetrahīnāṣṭa puṣpikā | etāni 
cāṣṭapuṣpāṇi sarvasādhāraṇāni tu || 

sarvasiddhipradānīha tathā sarvāśrameṣu ca | 

yadvā pūrvārcita pīṭhasthaṃ devamaṣṭapuṣpikayā pūjayet | 
tathā kriyākāṇḍe - 

pūjayetparameśānaṃ kṛtvā snānādi pūrvavat | 
pūrvārcite'thavā deve pradadyādaṣṭapuṣpikām || 

athavā prātaḥ kālārcite deve tatkāla saṃkhyayāṣṭapuṣpikayā 
pūjāṃ kṛtvā visarjanaṃ kuryāt || 

rāmanātha paddhatyāṃ - 

aśakta prārasarcya sandhyāṃ mādhyandinīmapi | aṣṭapuṣpaṃ 
tadevārccannapi kṛtvā visarjayet || 

p. 671) 

yathā kathaṃ citkutaścidvaikalpātparārcite liṅge nityavidhiḥ 
kiriyate tadānīmapi tulyā niṣṭhā nīcetsaṃkṣiptau vidhiḥ | 

kāryā nu na kadācidapi miśro vidhiḥ kartavyaḥ | 

uktaṃ ca - 

atvarastanuliṅgādau kuryānnityaṃ parārcite | 

tulyāniṣṭhāni vā | puṣpikā vidhiṃ samāpayet | 

kriyāvidbhiḥ kriyā kāryā siddhāntoktā na cetarā | yo miśrāṃ 
śaru tantrābhyāṃ kriyāṃ kuryādvimūḍhadhīḥ || 

surāpānasamaṃ jñeyaṃ tasya pātakamuktam | iti | 

p. 672) 

atha sādhāraṇaliṅgapakṣe sādhāraṇamantrairevāarcanaṃ kāryam | 

tathā - 

praṇavo mātṛkā madhā(yā?) vyomavyāpi ṣaḍakṣaraiḥ | prāsādo 
ghoramantraśca saptasādhāraṇā matāḥ ||

mādhyāhnika pūjāvidhiḥ

|| bhojana vidhiḥ ||

||

gurudravyamantyajasvaṃ visarjayet ||

p. 675) 

prāsāṃ? pāpāni vardhante sūnādoṣaiśca pañcabhiḥ |

iti ||

garbhādikarmasaṃskāre śmaśāne naiva bhojanam | 
rajasvalādisaṃspṛṣṭaṃ śivabhakto visarjayet || 

mataṅge - 

garbhādhānādi cūḍānta saṃskāredhe prakīrtitam | 
saṃskārātteṣu viprendra na bhoktavyaṃ kadācana || 

jñānaratnāvalyāṃ - 

siddhe pāka iti | 

kiraṇe - 

taccakāryaṃ yathā bhāvātparocchiṣṭaṃ visarjayet || 

p. 697) 

ṣaṭsahasre - 

parocchiṣṭaṃ na bhuñjīta bhārtṛdattena doṣabhāk | 

kālottare - 

gurūnna bhojayitvā tu svayameva tu bhojanam | tadbhojanasamastaṃ hi 
viṣṭhāmevaṃ tu bhojanam || 

sadyaḥ patati senānireke caiva tu gacchatu | tasmāt sarvaprayatnena 
gurubhojyaṃ tatāśnute || 

gurostu śeṣamamṛtaṃ śāstrajñaissamudīritam | parokṣe nivedya 
bhuñjīyāccaturāśramasaṃsthitaiḥ || 

evaṃ bhuktvā kiñciducchiṣṭānnamādāya pretanarakavāsibhyaḥ 
svāheti bhūmau nivedya prakṣālitaṃ hastena cūlukaṃ 

p. 698) 

gṛhītvā oṃ hāṃ amṛtāpidhānamasi svāheti prāśya 
taccheṣajalaṃ kaniṣṭhāṅgulinā grāhya 
kulyasthacūrṇādibhirhastādikaṃ viśodhya pādau prakṣālya 
tacchiṣṭajalaṃ vihāyānyajalenācamya bhuktadoṣaviśuddhyarthaṃ 
oṃ hāṃ kālāgnirudrāya svāheti 
dakṣiṇakarāṅguṣṭhāgraprasṛtajalena 
dakṣiṇapādāṅguṣṭhāgrasthakālāgnirudramabhiṣicya bhasmanā 
snātvā sakalīkṛtya parighāmudrāṃ badhvā nābhi vāmadevena 
nadhāvattya(navadhāvartya?) ghoraṃ saptadhā japtvā maunaṃ visṛjya 
tāmbūlādinā mukhaṃ śodhayet | tathā pretanarakavāsibhyaḥ 

p. 699) 

svāhetyuccārya kamantrakaṃ? bhūmau kṣiptvālpamucchiṣṭaṃ 
saṃprakṣālitapāṇinā 

aṃśumati - 

āpośanābhidhānaṃ ca tacchiṣṭaṃ bhūgataṃ sṛjet | āsyaṃ ca 
jalapātraṃ ca bhṛṅkārakajalairyutam || 

hastaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā āsyamārjanameva ca | 
kulisthacūrṇasaṃmiśraṃ athavā mudgacūrṇakam || 

pādaprakṣālanaṃ kṛtvā taccheṣaṃ varjayejjalam || 

punarjalaṃ ca saṃgṛhya ācamya vidhinā budhaḥ | yāvadaśnāti 
piṇḍāni tāvatpāpena lipyate || 

alpameva tato'śnīyātprāṇarakṣaṇamātrakam | tatpāpasya 
viśuddhyarthaṃ dakṣapādanakhāgrake || 

p. 700) 

kālāgnirudra hṛnmantrī savyāṅguṣṭhena secayet | 
culukodakametatsyāt svātmanaḥ pādamūlataḥ || 

āropya vāmahastasya kaniṣṭhānnābhimaṇḍale | 
sāmavedda?kṣahastasya vāmāṅguṣṭhena yojayet || 

vitatau ca karau kṛtvā dakṣāṅguṣṭhe nyasenmukhe | parigheyaṃ 
samākhyatā duṣṭāhāra vināśinī || 

prāṇāgnihotramityevaṃ nityamevamudāhṛtam | śivamantramanusmṛtya 
hṛdā vāmena saṃspṛśet || 

anyagṛha bhojanennyasyāhṛta? śivagāyatrījapapūrvaṃ bhuñjīta | 

dharmasaṃkīrtanaṃ caiva purāṇaśravaṇaṃ tataḥ | 

yadvā - 

vāmapādāṅguṣṭhāgra jalena oṃ ātmatattvāya nama iti secayet || 

p. 701) 

kiraṇe - 

bhuktvaivaṃ vāmapādasthaṃ aṅguṣṭhaṃ vāriṇā plavet | 
dakṣahastasamurtthe?nāṅguṣṭhenādhomukhena tu || 

secayedātmatattvaṃ tu praṇavādi namo'ntakam | 

bhadra?vāle tu ekahastena mudrābandhavidhānācca 
dakṣiṇenaikahastena parighāṃ badhnīyāditi kecit | 

athoddhūlya kṛtanyāso gurudevaṃ praṇamya ca | aghoraṃ saptadhā 
japtvā maunaṃ paścādvisarjayet || 

iti || 

ityātmārthapūjāpaddhatyāṃ 

sāntānikabhikṣā bhojanavidhiḥ 

p. 702) 

bhikṣā bhojanavidhiḥ 

atha naiṣṭhikasādhakādi vṛttyarthaṃ bhikṣāṭanaṃ kuryāt | 
tatra tāvat(d?) bhaikṣaṃ caturvidham | sāntānikam ayācitam svayaṃ 
bhaktaṃ(dattaṃ?) mādhūkaramiti | taduktam pauṣkare - 

bhaikṣaṃ caturvidhaṃ proktaṃ sādhakānāṃ mahātmanām | 
mādhūkaraṃ svayaṃdattaṃ sāntānika mayācitam || 

tatra sāntānikabhikṣāsvarūpaṃ pūrvamuttamam(ktam?) | ayācita 
svayaṃdatta svarūpam 

cintyaviśva sādākhye - 

pradarśitam ayācitaṃ tu śuddhānnamādareṇa samanvitam | 
annādibhūmiparyantaṃ sā bhikṣāsyādayācitam || 

p. 703) 

kirātanaṭakaivartagopālapuruṣādibhiḥ | patitaiścādi(bhi?) 
śastaiśca tyajeddattamayācitam || 

bhikṣāyai saṃpraviṣṭāya bhramate svayameva vā | upanītā svayaṃ 
dattā bhikṣā niṣparigrahā || 

pauṣkare - 

svayaṃ dattaṃ sādhakasya bhaikṣaṃ pātrasya pūraṇam | 
gṛhādgṛhaṃ paryatosi bhaikṣaṃ mādhūkaraṃ smṛtam || 

acintyaviśvasādākhye - 

yathamadhvādadāno'pi bhṛṅgaṃ puṣpaṃ na bādhate | 
tadvadbhikṣāṃ kṣamī bhūtvā cādadīta gṛhādhipāt || 

etallakṣaṇasaṃyuktaṃ yuktaṃ upavāsassadāmatam | 
sadopavāsaśreṣṭhaṃ hi bhikṣānnaṃ nandikeśvara || 

p. 704) 

bhikṣāvidhau viśeṣeṇa mādhūkaramanuttamam | cāturvarṇaṃ 
caredbhaikṣamabhiśastaṃ vivarjayet || 

ekānnaṃ naiva bhuñjīta bṛhaspatikulādapi | 
prārthanābhyuparodhādvā bhikṣā kāryā na doṣakṛt || 

ajñāninā gṛhe vāpi śuddhā mādhūkarā smṛtā | 

iti || 

iyaṃ mādhūkarī dvividhā kaṇabhikṣā cānnabhikṣā ceti | tatra 
kaṇa? bhikṣā nāma taṇḍula bhikṣā | sā 
bugukṣamuviṣayā(bubhukṣumumukṣaviṣayā?) pūrvāhna eva kāryā | 
dvitīyā tu mumukṣuviṣayā | pratyakasthe divākare kāryā | tatra 
tāmralohajabrahmavṛkṣādipatrajāni 

p. 705) 

bubukṣubhikṣāpātrāṇi | tumbakādīni mumukṣu mumukṣupātrāṇi | 
tāni - 

dvādaśāṅgula vistārāṇyaṣṭāṅgulocchrayāṇi ca | kartavyaṃ 
pratyahaṃ tyā(pro?)kṣyamaunādbhikṣāṭanaṃ param || 

bhrātyā(ntyā?) pādena tattāvadbhikṣā sāsminnigamya(dya?)te | 
kaṇabhikṣāthavākāryā bahu deśikā na vā? || 

tadā pākaḥ svayaṃ kāryāṃ tridhā vā śuddhāyā yadi | 

sarvajñānottare - 

yadā tu vittasaṃpannaśca digbhuk satataṃ bhavet | 
vittahīnaścaredbhaikṣāṃ kaṇapakvānnakīmatha || 

etadvyākhyāyāmapi kaṇabhikṣāṃ pakvānnabhikṣāṃ vetyarthaḥ iti 
|| 

p. 706) 

mataṅge - 

atha bhikṣāṭanaṃ vakṣye nirvṛtyā yatibhiryathā | 
kartavyamakhilaṃ kṛtvā pratyaksthe tu divākare || 

madhyādvedikaraṇe - 

bhikṣāpātraṃ tadāvaśyaṃ tāmraṃ lohaṃ tathāpi vā | 
tadaṣṭāṅgulakojñāya(cchrāyaṃ?) dvādaśāṅgulavistṛtam || 

tathā - 

bhikṣārthaṃ tāmrajaṃ lohaṃ brahmavṛkṣādipatrajam | 
bubhukṣorvihitaṃ pātraṃ mumukṣostumbakādikam || 

bhikṣāpātraṃ prakartavyaṃ tāmraṃ vā patrakalpitam | 
brahmavṛkṣaḥ palāśaḥ | tathā - 

kālottare - 

palāśamadhukapatraiśca kadambāmrañca? patrakaiḥ | varjayecca 
vaṭāśvattha piśācākṣādikesaram || 

p. 707) 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

alābhe'lābukaṃ pātraṃ sakāmasya hitaṃ bhavet | 
mumukṣostumbakaṃ śreṣṭhaṃ pūrvoktamathavā hitam || 

atha pādau prakṣālyācamya bhasmanā snātaḥ kaupīnaṃ parivartya 
dhṛtottarīyassannaddhajaṭassamācamya kṛtanyāsaḥ svoditaṃ 
pātraṃ hṛdā saṃgṛhyāstreṇa prakṣālya 
nirīkṣaṇādibhissaṃskṛtakavacenābhimantrya kāṣāyādi 
śuddhavastreṇāvakuṇṭhya tanmadhye aiśānamastragāyatrībhyāṃ 
saha cintya vastradaṇḍe gāyatrīchatre vinyasya dakṣiṇakare 
dadhānaḥ sopānatkaḥ śivaṃ guruṃ 

p. 708) 

dyātvānnaṃ? kaṇemiti vijñāpya labdhānujño gṛhītamauno 
jvaladastreṇa javalantaṃ pātraṃ cintya mahāstrādikaṃ 
gṛhāṇananaḥ(hnānaḥ?) 
patitābhiśastaśivadravyopaṇītyādivagrahavadajyaṃ(gṛhatatm?) 
caturṣu varṇeṣu śuddhagṛheṣu bhikṣāmaṭet | tadā niṣṭhīvana 
kutsanakopavidādhānīnna kūrvīta | bhikṣārthaṃ gṛhāṅkaṇaṃ 
prāpya bhikṣāṃ dehi svapādāṅguṣṭhāgramapaśvaravatsvaṃ? 
goprāptakālāvadhi sthitvā gṛhāntaraṃ yāyāt | gacchannāhūto na 
punarāgacchet | tatraiva sthitvā bhikṣāṃ gṛhṇīyāt | na ca 
dārvādidattāmadadīta | 

p. 709) 

bhikṣāmaṭato bhikṣānnaṃ dadyāt | evamanyonyapi(ni?)ṣiddhāni 
varjayitvā bhikṣāṃ gṛhnīyāt | 

jñānaratnāvalyām - 

kiṃ pratigrahadoṣena(ṇa?) kiṃ vā dānaphalena ca | 
pākāye(yā?)sena kiṃ kāryaṃ bahudoṣāśrayeṇa tu || 

ityeteṣu viraktaścenmahāpuṇyajigī(cikī?)rṣayā | 

tīrthe - 

strīliṅgānāśraye kṛtvā tṛṇaparṇamayīṃ kuṭīm | 
tāvatparikaro bhūyādyāvanmantreṇa vartate || 

dehapādavyavahitaṃ pratīkṣanpādamavyayam | 
bodhāyanamayannityaśivāsta śrīśivāśrayā || 

viśuddha bhasmanā snātaḥ kaupīnaṃ cottarīyakam | parivarta(tya?) 
samācamya maunī dhyātvā śivaṃ gurum || 

p. 710) 

sarvajñānottare - 

vratinā grahaṇaṃ kuryātpātrasya satataṃ budhaḥ | 
kṣālayitvāstrabījena kavacena tu veṣṭayet || 

īśānaṃ cintayenmadhye gāyatryastraṃ ca mantravit | astraṃ tu 
japayennityaṃ bhikṣāmāvartayecchuciḥ || 

astraṃ daṇḍe tu vinyasya chatre gāyatrīmeva tu | tato 
bhikṣāmaṭennityaṃ patitāśastavarjitam || 

aśastā grāmahyā(ṇyā?)dayaḥ | 

mataṅge - 

vaidyanāpitagopālamaṇḍa śauṇḍakakā(jā?)lakān | 
santyajedbhaikṣabhuṅnityaṃ gṛhaṃ grāmaṇikasya tu || 

āsīda vāddhṛ(rdhu?)ṣī kuṇḍakolakāṃśca parityajet | 

p. 711) 

vṛṣalīpatayaścaiva kuśīlā grāmayāni? cakāḥ | tathā 
devalakāścānye bhraṣṭaśaucāścaye narāḥ || 

anyāyavṛttayassarve tyājyā bhikṣāṃśujāṃ(kṣābhu?) sadā | 
madyāmiṣapramodāḍhyaṃ tyājyaṃ tadveśma yatnataḥ || 

kukkuṭaśca(śva?) kharoṣṭrāṇāṃ gṛhe yatra parigrahaḥ | 
tadgṛhaṃ na praveṣṭavyaṃ yacca strīkalmaṣaṃ mune || 

cāturvarṇamaṭedbhikṣāṃ bhikṣārthī sādhakottamaḥ | 
duṣṭasaṅgona yastrāsmi(trāsti?) na ca svāmnāyavarjanam || 

devāgnyatithipūjādyaṃ pātraśuddhiśca dṛśyate | tasmingrāhyā 
mune bhikṣā avicākhyaṃ(rya?) sumedhasā || 

tailābhyaktau(ktā?) ca yā nānī(rī?) nirguṇī vātha kutsitā | 
tasyā bhikṣā sadā varjyānupādeyaṃ tu pāṇinā || 

p. 712) 

kriyākāṇḍe - 

na darvā?(rpyā?) na śca(ca?) hastena kāṃsyenātha na madīyatām | 
vṛṣalī garbhiṇī jvānavaktrāṇāntakareṇa vā || 

tadā pramāṇa niṣṭhīva sparśamunmārga sapphaṇaḥ | kutsanaṃ ca 
na kurvīta tyajeśvā(cchvā?)nādiṃ saṃkulam || 

naṅgastye na ca brāhmaṃ devadevyopajīvinaḥ | cāturvarṇāṃ 
śuddhavarṇāṃ samāsādya gṛhāṅkaṇam || 

bhikṣāṃ dehīti saṃjalpya pādāṅguṣṭhāgralocanaḥ | 
tāvatkālaṃ pratīkṣeta yāvadgaurvatsayogataḥ || 

prastapaṃ(sravaṃ?) samavāpnoti tato'nyatra vrajenmuniḥ | vrajannapi 
samāhūto nāgacchettatpradeśataḥ || 

yajamānajanānītāṃ bhikṣāṃ tatraiva cāharet | na bhikṣāṃ 
carato bhikṣāṃ na dadyānna vigarhitāḥ || 

p. 713) 

kiraṇe - 

pātre prapatitaṃ bhaikṣaṃ bhūgataṃ yadvisarjayet | 

yāvatpātrapūraṇamātmatṛptimātrakaṃ vā bhikṣāmādāya tatra 
grāmavāsināṃ śubhaṃ smṛtvā svāśramaṃ praviśet | 
nirmālyādyupahataścedbhikṣāpātrasahitaḥ snāyāt | 

cintyaviśvasādākhye - 

bhikṣitvā pātrapūrṇāntamathavā tṛptimātrakam | jagatāṃ 
śobhanaṃ jñātvā vrajetsvāśramamuttamam || 

bhikṣāpātraṃ śuddhisthale sthāpya pādāsi prakṣālya ca 
uddhūlya sakalīkṛtya bhaikṣaṃ hṛdā saṃprokṣya 
mṛtyuñjayenābhimantrya 

p. 714) 

manasā tridhā vibhajya devāya gurave nivedya śeṣasya 
parimitagrāsasya bhāgamekajalena bhāgamekaṃ vāyusaṃcārārthaṃ 
bhāgamekaṃ ca pūrayet | nodakadyāṃ pūrvoktavadbhuñjīyāt | 

taduktam - 

siddhāntaśekhare - 

grāsaiḥ parimitaiḥ paścāddhūpayuktairghṛtairyutaiḥ | 
pūrayesyadudarasyārdhi tadardhamudakena ca || 

prāṇāvāyu pracārārthaṃ taccheṣaṃ ca na pūrayet | 
bhikṣābhojananiyamavato kvacidapyekānnaṃ na bhuñjīta | 
bahirhastaṃ prakṣalya 

p. 715) 

dantakāṣṭhena dantānviśodhya vidhivadācāmyoddhūlya śivaṃ 
dhyātvā patrādinā mukhaṃ śodhayet || 

kiraṇe - 

bhikṣāmānīya tāme saṃsthāpya sthāne site same | prakṣālya 
hastapādādi pūrvavadvāvametpunaḥ hṛdā saṃprokṣya tāṃ 
dadyācchivasvagurave punaḥ || bhojanaṃ tu tataḥ kuryākṣureṇa 
abhimantritaḥ | tenāmṛtaṃ bhavatyāśutadbhuktvā mṛtyujidbhavet | 
maunamāsthāya bhoktavyaṃ na cātyantavirodhakṛt || 

p. 716) 

mataṅge - 

svagṛhe yācitaṃ bhaktyā yadānītaparāṇi tu | pūrvoktamakhilaṃ 
kṛtvā bhoktavyaṃ nānyathā kvacit || iti || 

ṣaṭsahasrikāyām - 

evaṃ bhikṣāṭanaṃ proktaṃ sarvasāmānyalakṣaṇam | nāsti 
bhikṣāsamaṃ puṇyaṃ nāsti bhikṣāsamaṃ tapaḥ || 

sarvatīrtheṣu yatpuṇyaṃ sarvayajñeṣu yatphalam | sarvadāne tu 
yatpuṇyaṃ tathā cāndrāyaṇādiṣu || 

tatphalaṃ koṭiguṇitaṃ bhikṣāśī prāpnuyānnaraḥ | pāke 
nivartite yatrātithiryāti parāṅmukhaḥ || 

devāśca pitarassarve yānti tasmātparāṅmukhāḥ | 

p. 717) 

prāpte kāle tu taddānamavicāreṇa yatnataḥ | kulānuddharate so hi 
daśa pūrvāndaśāparān || 

sarveṣāṃ caiva pātrāṇāṃ pātraṃ caivātithiḥ paraḥ | 
hīnāṅgo vātha pūrṇāṅgo'pya śuddhiśśucireva vā || 

avicāreṇa pūjyo'sau so'śvamedhaphalaṃ labhet | 
saṃprāptakāla(le?) yo bhaikṣaṃ dūrataḥ parivarjayet || 

tasya puṇyaphalaṃ sarvaṃ gṛhītvā yāti so'tithiḥ | atikrūḍa 
yadā kāle yotthayedavicārataḥ || 

brahmahā kathyate so hi bahurūpassadāśuciḥ | evaṃ jñātvā 
prayatnena kāryaṃ bhikṣāṭanaṃ sadā || 

siddhayastasya sidhyanti bhikṣāśī yo jitendriyaḥ | sarve 
pratigrahāḥ proktā vinābhikṣāṃ śikhidhvaja || 

p. 718) 

tathā - 

bhikṣādānaṃ mahādānaṃ bhikṣā naiva pratigrahaḥ | 
ubhayostatphalaṃ tulyaṃ dātṛ grāhakayorapi || 

tasmācchraddhāṃ samādāya bhikṣāṃ kuryātsadā vratī | 

śivadharme - 

mādhūkaraṃ ca bhaikṣaṃ ca sikānnaṃ? parivarjayet | upavāsaṃ ca 
yadbhaikṣamekānnaṃ gṛhīṇāṃ smṛtam || 

atra tu - 

madhyaṃ dine'thavā naktamavrajībhuñjīta nānyathā | bhoktavyaṃ 
gṛhīṇāṃ vāradvayaṃ vīryavivṛddhaye || 

sarvajñānottare - 

ācamya hi niṣkrānto bhakṣayeddantadhāvanam | iti |
